This is highly valuable for predicting optimal configurations for large-scale models under limited compute budgets, preventing resource waste from blind scaling.","technical_approach":"Introduces a novel acquisition function, Power-Law Entropy Search (PLES). Unlike traditional Bayesian optimization that optimizes a single objective, PLES seeks experimental configurations (model scale, data scale, hyperparameters) that maximally reduce the uncertainty of the entire scaling law estimate. bytes.","importance":"The useful signal for pretraining is the architecture trade-off in tokenizer-free language modeling. A pure byte-level Transformer remains the safer choice for fine-grained character operations, while hierarchical byte models buy efficiency by removing much of the direct byte-level attention. This matters for scripts with little tokenizer coverage, code, formatting-sensitive generation, and any setting where copying or editing characters is part of the capability target.","technical_approach":"The authors decompose a byte-level Transformer block into four module types: byte-level attention C_a, byte-level feed-forward C_f, word-level attention W_a, and word-level feed-forward W_f. Word-level modules operate on downsampled positions and add their updates back to the residual stream; byte-level modules process the full sequence. This notation lets them compare pure byte models with several hierarchy layouts, including Middle, Bottom, Top, and Interleaved variants. It also lets them isolate whether character manipulation depends more on attention or feed-forward computation at the byte level. The central finding is that byte-level attention is the critical component. Downsampling can reduce sequence length and cost, but it also removes direct attention paths between individual byte positions. Once those paths are gone, later upsampling layers cannot fully reconstruct the missing fine-grained alignment information.","experiments":"The study trains models from scratch at two scales: a 12-layer, width-768 model of roughly 100M parameters on a 1B-word subset of Nemotron-ClimbMix, and a 24-layer, width-1024 model of roughly 400M parameters on 4B words. The scaling choice follows a token-per-parameter rule from Qiu et al., treating GPT-2 tokens roughly as words. The evaluation focuses on character manipulation benchmarks such as CUTE and EXECUTE, while also checking the broader performance-efficiency trade-off. Pure byte-level models consistently perform best on character-level tasks. Among hierarchical variants, replacing word-level computation with byte-level attention gives more benefit than doing the same for feed-forward layers. The larger-scale runs examine whether the number of byte-level layers should stay fixed or grow with model depth, a design choice where earlier systems such as Slagle-style models and Bolmo-style models differ.","reading_guide":"A useful skim takes about 20–30 minutes. This generation paradigm introduces two axes that can influence safety alignment: when tokens are generated during denoising and where they appear in the response. In this paper, we measure dLLM safety behavior under harmful prompts by tracing intermediate token distributions and commitment decisions throughout denoising. Our analysis shows that refusal signals are concentrated i","starred":false,"priority":"p2","reading_tier":"skim","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"","category_id":"architecture","cover_url":"/covers/2609_00495.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.CL","cs.AI"],"summary":{"core_problem":"这篇工作研究的是 diffusion language model 的安全对齐在“去噪步数”和“响应位置”两个维度上如何形成、如何失效。AR 模型里，安全行为常被讨论为前几个 token 的浅层对齐问题，因为生成顺序和表面位置绑定在一起；但 dLLM 里这两个维度分离了：一个 token 可以在很早的 denoising step 被提交，却出现在后面的响应位置；也可以是前面位置的 token 一直拖到后面才确定。论文要解决的核心问题，是在这种新生成范式下，拒答信号究竟何时出现、何时被提交、何时被覆盖，以及能否利用这些中间动态，在不改模型参数的情况下提升安全性。","importance":"对预训练研究的价值主要在建模范式层面，而不是安全应用层面。它提供了一个观察 diffusion LM 内部生成动态的具体切口：在 dLLM 中，alignment 不再只和前几个输出位置有关，还和早期去噪步骤的 commitment dynamics 有关。这个结论对所有非 AR 文本生成范式都重要，因为它提醒人们：训练目标和解码机制共同决定了行为形成的位置，不能直接把 AR 上的安全或 controllability 结论照搬过来。论文还给出一个 training-free decoding 方法 RAEC，说明 dLLM 的安全问题有一部分不是“模型里没有拒答能力”，而是“拒答信号出现了但没能持续到提交”。这对理解 diffusion LM 的训练-解码接口有启发。","technical_approach":"技术上分成两部分：测量框架和解码方法。\n\n先说测量。作者针对 masked diffusion LM 建立了 step-wise 和 position-wise 的分析框架。模型在每个 denoising step 都会对所有位置预测 clean token distribution，但只对一部分仍为 mask 的位置做 commitment。基于这个机制，作者跟踪 harmful prompt 下 refusal-related token mass 在不同 step、不同位置上的演化，并区分几个概念：\n1. refusal signal 是否出现；\n2. refusal token 是否被 commit；\n3. refusal commitment 是否持续，还是后续被覆盖；\n4. 最终安全结果与早期 commitment 的关系。\n\n这套分析导向了一个核心发现，作者称为 shallow-step alignment。意思是：在 dLLM 中，早期去噪步骤本身就是安全敏感维度。早期被提交的 token，即便不都位于最前面的响应位置，也会显著影响最终是否拒答。和 AR 的 shallow alignment 相比，这里多了一个“step”轴。\n\n再说方法。基于上述观察，作者提出 Refusal-Aware Early Commitment（RAEC）。它是 training-free 的，只改解码时的 commitment decision，不改模型参数、不做额外训练。其原则很简单：如果某个 refusal signal 在早期步骤已经出现，并且强度足够、持续性足够，就提前把它 commit 下来，避免后续去噪过程把它冲掉。这里的关键不是强行插入拒答模板，而是利用模型自己已经给出的 refusal probability mass，只是在提交时机上做干预。\n\n为什么这样做，而不是直接调 logits 或加安全 classifier？因为作者的分析表明，unsafe trajectory 往往并非完全没有 refusal evidence，而是 refusal mass 弱、短暂、未被提交。既然问题出在 commitment dynamics，上最小干预的地方就是 commitment policy。这个设计和 diffusion LM 的生成机制是对齐的，也比额外训练一个安全头更便于验证机制。","experiments":"实验使用两类有害指令 benchmark：JailbreakBench 和 StrongREJECT；安全性评估用 Llama-Guard-3-8B 作为 judge，指标是 Attack Success Rate（ASR），越低越安全。模型对象包括 LLaDA 和 Dream 这类 masked diffusion LM。\n\n从摘要和正文可确认的主要结果有三层。\n\n第一，测量结果。拒答信号主要集中在早期 denoising steps 和前部响应位置，但两者并不等价。早期 step 的 commitment 对最终安全结果影响很大，这支持 shallow-step alignment 的说法。作者还做了干预式分析：如果在早期步骤人为提交 refusal token，未对齐的 base dLLM 会明显更安全；反过来，如果在早期提交 compliance token，已经对齐的模型也会更容易走向不安全输出。这说明早期 commitment 不是旁观指标，而是具有因果影响的控制点。\n\n第二，unsafe case 的机制分析。作者发现很多不安全生成轨迹里并不是完全没有拒答信号，而是这些信号不够强、不够持久，或者在 commit 前被后续步骤覆盖。相对地，安全轨迹中的 refusal mass 更强、更稳定，且 refusal token 的 commit rate 更高。这个观察很重要，因为它把问题从“模型是否学会拒答”细化成“拒答信号能否存活到提交”。\n\n第三，RAEC 的效果。论文声称 RAEC 在 LLaDA 和 Dream 上都能降低 ASR，同时大体保持 utility。用户给出的全文片段没有包含完整数字表，因此没法可靠抄出具体降幅；但从作者表述看，RAEC 的收益不是靠大幅牺牲正常回答能力换来的，而是在 harmful prompt 上更早锁定 refusal trajectory。阅读原文时应重点核对：不同 benchmark 上的 ASR 下降幅度、对 benign/helpful prompts 的 utility 影响、以及 RAEC 对不同 step 阈值和 refusal persistence 阈值的敏感性。","reading_guide":"这篇适合做机制导向的泛读。先看 Introduction 和 Preliminaries，弄清 dLLM 的 commitment 机制：模型每步都预测所有位置，但只提交一部分 mask 位置。理解这一点后，后面的安全分析才有意义。\n\n接着重点看测量框架和可视化图。优先找三类图表：\n1. refusal token mass 随 denoising step 的变化；\n2. refusal signal 在不同响应位置上的分布；\n3. safe vs unsafe trajectory 在 commitment persistence 上的差异。\n\n这些图比最终 ASR 表更有信息量，因为它们解释了为什么 RAEC 有效。\n\n然后看 RAEC 方法细节，尤其是“何时判定 refusal signal 足够强且足够持久”以及“commitment policy 如何修改”。如果你关心建模范式而不只是安全应用，这部分是全文最值得深挖的地方：它揭示了解码策略在 diffusion LM 中并不是纯工程细节，而是行为形成的一部分。\n\n最后再看实验表，核对 RAEC 在 LLaDA、Dream、JailbreakBench、StrongREJECT 上的收益和 utility 代价。预计 30 分钟左右可以抓住主线；若要深入，建议把 intervention 实验和 ablation 一起看。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"The paper studies how safety alignment emerges and fails in diffusion language models along two separate axes: denoising step and response position. In autoregressive models, these axes are tied together, so shallow alignment is usually discussed in terms of early output tokens. In dLLMs, they are decoupled: a token can be committed early in denoising while appearing later in the final response, or a leading response position can remain unresolved until late. The core problem is to understand when refusal signals appear, when they get committed, when they are overwritten, and whether those intermediate dynamics can be exploited to improve safety without changing model parameters.","importance":"Its value for pretraining-oriented readers is mainly at the modeling-paradigm level. The paper offers a concrete lens into diffusion-LM generation dynamics and shows that alignment in dLLMs is not only about leading response positions; it also depends on early denoising commitments. That matters beyond safety, because it highlights a broader point: in non-AR language modeling, behavior is shaped jointly by the training objective and the decoding mechanism, so conclusions imported from autoregressive models do not transfer directly. The proposed decoding method, RAEC, also supports a useful interpretation: some safety failures are not due to the absence of refusal capability, but due to refusal evidence failing to persist until commitment. That is a meaningful insight about the train-decode interface in diffusion LMs.","technical_approach":"$1a","experiments":"$1b","reading_guide":"$1c","authors_note":""},"recommendation":{"zh":"读中间分布分析即可；它提醒 dLLM 对齐不能照搬 AR 的 token-position 直觉。","en":"Read the intermediate-distribution analysis; it shows why dLLM alignment cannot simply inherit AR token-position intuitions."},"metadata":{"institutions":["Case Western Reserve University"],"authors":["Guoli Wang","Haonan Shi","Tu Ouyang","An Wang"],"notable_authors":[],"tags":["diffusion-language-model","safety-alignment","decoding","LLaDA","refusal"],"links":{"arxiv":"https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00495","pdf":"https://arxiv.org/pdf/2609.00495","github":null,"project":null,"source_url":null,"article":null}}},{"id":"2609.00873","title":"Membership Inference in Fine-tuned Diffusion Language Models via Token-level Memorization Asymmetry","abstract":"Diffusion language models (DLMs) have recently emerged as an alternative modeling paradigm to autoregressive LMs, offering advantages such as parallel generation and bidirectional context modeling. fine-tuned diffusion language models 的成员推断风险。核心问题不是简单把 AR LM 或视觉 diffusion 的攻击方法搬过来，而是解释 DLM 的 denoising 训练为什么会形成不同的记忆模式，并利用这种模式判断某条样本是否参与过微调。","importance":"对预训练和新型语言建模研究的启发在于：DLM 的隐私风险不能沿用 AR LM 的 token likelihood 直觉。DLM 的 any-order denoising 和随机 mask ratio 会改变 token 级记忆分布，导致 MinK、Min-K++ 这类依赖低分 token 的方法失效或变弱。论文把 DLM 的训练动态和隐私攻击联系起来，说明 diffusion LM 的训练目标、mask schedule、fine-tuning objective 都会影响样本级记忆暴露。这对后续设计 DLM 的数据审计、防泄漏训练、fine-tuning 安全评估都有参考价值。","technical_approach":"方法从一个训练动态观察出发：在 diffusion text training 中，单个 token 的记忆增益与该 token 被训练时对应的 mask ratio 近似成反比。mask ratio 越低，模型看到的上下文越完整，某些 token 更容易被强化记住；mask ratio 越高，预测更依赖分布性知识，单点记忆增益较弱。由此产生 token-level memorization asymmetry，即成员样本内部 token 的记忆信号分布更偏斜，而不是均匀增强。Q-Skew 利用这一点，计算样本 token 分数分布的 quantile-weighted skewness，用偏斜程度而非单个低分 token 来做成员推断。攻击设定是 query-based：攻击者只能访问目标模型 logits，不访问权重和梯度。主设定还使用未微调 reference model 来消除 base model 本身的语言先验，这在开源 base model 可用的 fine-tuning 场景中较现实。论文也考虑 reference model 不完全匹配时的弱化设定。","experiments":"实验覆盖不同 fine-tuning 目标、数据域和 DLM 类型，包括 base DLM 与 instruction-tuned DLM。论文特别指出，已有 DLM membership inference 评测多集中在 domain fine-tuning，且常用固定 block size，容易高估攻击效果；这里同时评估 domain fine-tuning 和 instruction fine-tuning，以处理真实指令数据的变长特性。Q-Skew 在不同训练 epoch 下都优于已有 baseline，平均 AUC 提升超过 10%。除成员推断外，论文还把 skewness 信号用于 PII extraction，显示相同的 token-level asymmetry 可以帮助定位更可能被模型记住的敏感片段。","reading_guide":"建议泛读 30–45 分钟。重点看 Introduction 中对 AR LM、视觉 diffusion、DLM 三类隐私攻击差异的界定；Methodology 中关于 mask ratio 与 token memory gain 反比关系的理论推导；实验部分优先看 domain fine-tuning 与 instruction fine-tuning 的对比、reference model mismatch 的结果、不同 epoch 下 AUC 是否稳定。若只关心 DLM pretraining/fine-tuning 机制，Q-Skew 公式本身可以快速过，但 token-level memorization asymmetry 的推导值得细看。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"The paper studies membership inference against fine-tuned diffusion language models. The central issue is that DLMs do not expose memorization in the same way as autoregressive LMs or image diffusion models, because their denoising objective predicts tokens under random masking and any-order conditioning.","importance":"The main value for pretraining research is the link between DLM training dynamics and privacy leakage. The paper shows that likelihood-style intuitions from AR LMs are not directly reliable for DLMs. Random mask ratios and any-order denoising reshape token-level memorization, so attacks such as MinK and Min-K++ that rely on lower-tail token scores can be poorly matched to this setting. This is relevant for auditing fine-tuned DLMs, designing safer masking schedules, and understanding how non-AR language modeling objectives change memorization behavior.","technical_approach":"The method is built around token-level memorization asymmetry. The authors argue that the single-step memorization gain of a token is inversely related to the mask ratio used during diffusion training. When fewer tokens are masked, the model has richer context and can reinforce specific target tokens more strongly; when more tokens are masked, prediction relies more on broad distributional knowledge. This creates a skewed token-level memorization profile for member examples. Q-Skew captures this profile with a quantile-weighted skewness statistic over token scores, rather than focusing on the worst-scoring or most surprising tokens. The attack is query-based: the adversary observes logits but not parameters or gradients. The main reference-based setup uses an unfine-tuned base model to subtract pre-existing language-model priors, and the paper also considers weaker reference-model alignment.","experiments":"The evaluation spans multiple fine-tuning datasets, domains, objectives, and diffusion language model variants, including both base and instruction-tuned DLMs. A notable design choice is to test instruction fine-tuning in addition to domain fine-tuning, because fixed-length domain blocks can make membership inference look easier than it is for variable-length instruction data. Q-Skew consistently improves over prior baselines across training epochs, with an average AUC gain above 10%. The same skewness signal is also used to improve PII extraction, suggesting that the phenomenon is not limited to binary membership decisions.","reading_guide":"A useful skim takes about 30–45 minutes. Read the Introduction for the failure modes of AR-LM and image-diffusion privacy attacks in DLMs. Then focus on the theoretical derivation connecting mask ratio to token memory gain, since that is the main conceptual contribution. In the experiments, prioritize the domain-vs-instruction fine-tuning comparison, the reference-model mismatch setting, and the epoch-wise AUC curves. The exact Q-Skew implementation is secondary unless one plans to reproduce the attack.","authors_note":""},"recommendation":{"zh":"关注 Diffusion LM 范式的研究员必看，揭示了 DLM 独特的 token 级记忆不对称性，对理解 DLM 的训练动态和数据记忆机制有重要启发。","en":"Must-read for researchers focusing on the Diffusion LM paradigm; reveals unique token-level memorization asymmetry in DLMs, providing important insights into DLM training dynamics and data memorization mechanisms."},"metadata":{"institutions":["Tsinghua University","National University of Singapore","Oregon State University","Shanghai Jiao Tong University","Affiliation:College of AI, Tsinghua University","Affiliation:National University of Singapore","Affiliation:Oregon State University","Affiliation:Shanghai Jiao Tong University"],"authors":["Shengfang Zhai","Leo Marchyok","Yuling Shi","Huanran Chen","Yinpeng Dong","Jiaheng Zhang","Sanghyun Hong"],"notable_authors":["Yinpeng Dong"],"tags":["Diffusion Language Models","Membership Inference","Privacy","Memorization Asymmetry"],"links":{"arxiv":"https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00873","pdf":"https://arxiv.org/pdf/2609.00873","github":null,"project":null,"source_url":null,"article":null}}},{"id":"2609.01100","title":"Beyond Magnitude: Contrastive Routing for Modular Mixture-of-Experts","abstract":"In current Mixture-of-Experts architectures, routing is performed based on representations dominated by structure shared across all tokens, limiting expert specialization. The contribution is a routing-mechanism refinement with some interpretability angle.","technical_approach":"The authors propose the Contrastive Routing Mechanism (CoRM). Instead of routing on the token representation alone, they maintain an EMA reference state for the layer and score each expert using a contrastive gap: token-to-expert affinity minus reference-to-expert affinity, with a distinct projection per expert. First, we keep a large language model (LLM) frozen and use it for what it is already goo","starred":false,"priority":"p2","reading_tier":"skim","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"","category_id":"architecture","cover_url":"/covers/2609_01117.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.AI","cs.CL"],"summary":{"core_problem":"传统的思维链（CoT）在离散的 token 空间中进行，存在错误累积且依赖于可模仿的推理轨迹。现有的在连续表示空间中进行推理的方法（如注入 soft tokens）通常使用通用的语言助手生成初始 latent，并缺乏深度的迭代优化，导致在非自然语言任务上表现脆弱。","importance":"探索了冻结 LLM 解码器下的连续空间推理新范式。证明了在非自然语言（符号推理）任务中，通用的 latent 提议器是有害的，而特定任务的提议器结合循环神经网络（RNN）进行深度迭代优化，能显著提升冻结 LLM 的推理能力，为参数高效的复杂推理提供了一种新思路。","technical_approach":"提出 Latent Recurrent Thoughts (LRT) 框架。保持主 LLM（如 Qwen3-8B）冻结，通过 soft tokens 接口注入连续的 latent 向量。LRT 包含两个关键组件：1) 任务专用的提议器（Task-dedicated Proposer）：根据输入生成适应特定任务族的初始 latent。2) 循环推理器（Recurrent Reasoner）：使用微型的递归网络（如 TRM）对初始 latent 进行多步迭代优化（refinement）。这种循环机制解耦了计算深度与模型参数量，能够学习向量值的状态转移，实现约束传播和迭代修正，而不仅仅是标量能量场的梯度下降。","experiments":"在符号任务（Countdown-4, Sudoku）和自然语言任务（HumanEval, MBPP, StrategyQA）上评估。在自然语言任务上，LRT 优于 SoftCoT 和 EBM-CoT。在 Countdown-4 任务上，SoftCoT 和 EBM-CoT 性能崩溃（5.9% 和 8.4%），远低于 zero-shot CoT（30.0%），而 LRT 达到了 56.7%。这表明在缺乏推理轨迹的符号任务中，特定任务的提议器和循环优化至关重要。","reading_guide":"建议重点阅读 Introduction 中对离散 CoT 局限性的分析以及现有连续空间推理方法的不足。Method 部分关注 LRT 的架构设计，特别是循环推理器（TRM）如何进行迭代优化。实验部分关注 Countdown-4 任务上的显著对比结果。预计阅读时间 15-20 分钟。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"Traditional Chain-of-Thought (CoT) reasoning operates in discrete token space, suffering from error propagation and relying on imitable reasoning traces. To overcome the inherent trade-offs between the memory overhead of metadata-based metrics and the computational inefficiency of adaptive selection strategies, we present Faster Flash Decoding (FFD), a novel hardware-algorithm co-design framework designed to break the memory wall in long-context decoding. FFD integrates","starred":false,"priority":"p2","reading_tier":"must_read","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"","category_id":"efficient","cover_url":"/covers/2609_00097.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.LG","cs.AI"],"summary":{"core_problem":"论文解决的是长上下文自回归解码中的内存带宽瓶颈，重点不是训练阶段的长序列计算，而是每生成一个 token 都要从 HBM 重新读取大段 KV cache 的问题。已有训练-free 稀疏注意力通常把“选择重要 token/block”和“执行注意力计算”拆成两个阶段：selector 依赖额外 metadata 做重要性估计，computer 再按索引取数计算。这带来两个具体代价：metadata 本身占内存和带宽；自适应选择策略如果接近 top-p，又需要全局 softmax 或同步，抵消稀疏带来的收益。","importance":"这篇工作的价值在于把长上下文 decoding 的稀疏注意力问题重新放回硬件执行路径里处理。它不是单纯提出一个新的 attention pruning 指标，而是尝试消除 selector-computer 分离造成的系统开销。对基座模型研究的启发主要有两点：第一，长上下文能力不能只看预训练长度和 benchmark 分数，decode 阶段的 KV 访问形态会直接限制可用上下文长度；第二，训练-free 稀疏化如果要真正可用，选择策略必须和 kernel 融合，否则理论 sparsity 很容易被索引、同步、metadata 读写吃掉。","technical_approach":"FFD 的核心是 content-aware scanning 加 top-δ selection，并把二者做成 fused kernel。它不额外存 cluster、partial dimension 或几何 bound 这类 metadata，而是把 K cache 拆成低比特量化表示和残差信息：低比特 K thumbnail 用于高速估计 query-key 相似度，残差路径用于保持计算精度。这样 selector 扫描的对象就是 KV cache 的压缩内容本身，而不是外部索引结构。选择策略上，top-δ 不固定保留 top-k，也不做 top-p 所需的全局概率累积，而是按 block 对 attention score sum 的估计贡献做过滤。直觉上，如果某些 block 对 softmax 分母贡献低于数值精度相关阈值，它们对最终 attention 输出的影响可以被忽略。由于 LLM attention 分布常有较强稀疏性和局部峰值，这个判断可以近似并行化，避免全局同步。系统实现上，FFD 把扫描、选择和后续 attention 计算融合在一个 kernel 内，computer 直接复用 selector 阶段得到的估计结果，减少重复访存、索引搬运和 reduction bubble。","experiments":"论文报告了 kernel-level 最高 11.6× 加速，并在 256K context 下仍能运行；端到端吞吐最高提升 2.37×。实验覆盖短上下文和长上下文任务，包括 RULER 和 LongBench，用来验证高比例稀疏不会明显破坏模型能力。关键实验点不是单个 benchmark 的小幅分数变化，而是三组取舍：低比特 content-aware scan 相比 metadata-based selector 是否减少带宽压力；top-δ 相比 top-k 是否能适应不同 entropy 的 attention 分布；fused selector-computer kernel 相比解耦实现是否把理论稀疏转化为实际吞吐。消融重点应关注 sparsity、accuracy、kernel time、end-to-end throughput 随上下文长度变化的曲线，尤其是长到 128K/256K 后的带宽主导区间。","reading_guide":"建议精读 Method 中三部分：K cache 的低比特量化扫描设计、top-δ 的数值精度推导、fused kernel 的执行路径。Figure 2 和 Algorithm 1 应该是理解整体数据流的入口；性能图表要重点看 kernel-level 和 end-to-end 的差异，因为很多 sparse attention 方法只在算子内快，放到完整 decoding pipeline 后收益会下降。消融部分建议重点看 top-k、top-p、top-δ 的对比，以及不同上下文长度下的 sparsity-accuracy 曲线。值得深挖的问题是：top-δ 的阈值是否对模型、层、head、任务稳定；K thumbnail 的量化误差是否会系统性误删重要远程 token；该方法和 KV cache quantization、paged attention、speculative decoding 同时使用时是否会有冲突。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"The paper targets the bandwidth bottleneck of long-context autoregressive decoding. The issue is not the compute-heavy prefill phase, but the decode phase where each generated token reloads a large KV cache from HBM. Existing training-free sparse attention methods usually separate selection from computation: a selector first builds or reads metadata to choose tokens or blocks, and a compute kernel then gathers the selected KV entries. This creates extra memory traffic, indexing overhead, and synchronization cost. Adaptive strategies such as top-p are more distribution-aware than top-k, but their global normalization makes them expensive in the decode path.","importance":"The main contribution is an execution-aware formulation of sparse long-context decoding. The paper is useful for foundation-model work because it shows that long-context capability is constrained not only by pretraining length or benchmark accuracy, but also by the memory-access pattern of KV cache during serving. It also clarifies a practical requirement for training-free sparsity: the selector must be fused with the attention computation, otherwise metadata reads, index movement, and global reductions can erase much of the theoretical gain.","technical_approach":"$1d","experiments":"The reported results include up to 11.6× kernel-level speedup, scaling to 256K context, and up to 2.37× end-to-end throughput improvement. The evaluation covers both short- and long-context benchmarks, including RULER and LongBench, and reports that model quality is largely preserved under high sparsity. The important experimental evidence is the tradeoff among sparsity, accuracy, kernel time, and full decoding throughput. The ablations to inspect are the comparisons between top-k, top-p, and top-δ; the effect of low-bit content-aware scanning versus metadata-based selection; and the gap between isolated kernel gains and end-to-end generation speed, especially at 128K to 256K context where HBM bandwidth dominates.","reading_guide":"Read the method sections on low-bit K-cache scanning, the numerical motivation for top-δ, and the fused kernel design carefully. Figure 2 and Algorithm 1 are the fastest way to understand the dataflow. The most useful plots are likely the sparsity-accuracy curves and the kernel versus end-to-end speed comparisons across context lengths. MoE 推理效率里的一个老问题：理论上的稀疏激活并没有自动变成端到端的高效 serving。作者认为瓶颈不只是 token-to-expert 路由不规则，更在于现有系统把 expert 当成不可再分的原子单元，只能做“选这个 expert / 不选这个 expert”的粗粒度决策。这样会过早固定优化边界，忽略 expert 内部 FFN 结构中的冗余与可重组空间。论文要解决的核心问题是：能否把 MoE 推理从 whole-expert selection 改成更细粒度的 intra-expert path composition，在不明显伤害质量的前提下，把专家内部计算也纳入可裁剪、可共享、可调度的优化空间。","importance":"从研究归类看，这更偏推理系统与 MoE serving，而不是预训练方法本身。但它对基座研究仍有两点启发。第一，MoE 的“稀疏性”不应只理解为 expert 级稀疏，还可以继续下钻到 expert 内部结构；这会反过来影响未来 MoE 架构设计，尤其是 expert 是否应该更模块化、更适合路径重组。第二，很多 MoE 论文把 router 质量和 expert 负载均衡当成主要效率问题，这篇则提醒：即便路由不变，expert 内部也还有系统级冗余可挖。贡献量级属于工程方法型，适合关注 MoE inference、serving co-design、以及“架构是否为系统优化留接口”的读者。","technical_approach":"核心思路是把标准 MoE expert 的单一路径执行，拆成可组合的子变换路径。作者以现代 MoE 常见的 SwiGLU FFN expert 为基础：输入先经过 gate 分支和 up 分支的扩张投影，再做逐元素门控融合，最后通过 down 投影回到 hidden size。传统做法里，这整套计算属于一个 expert，被 router 作为整体选中。PCoMoE 则把 expert 内部的这些子结构视为可组合组件，把“选 expert”改写成“选 path”。\n\n这样做会带来一个组合空间爆炸问题：如果不同 expert 的 expansion-side 与 projection-side 可以交叉组合，路径数会迅速增大，调度和质量都可能失控。为此，论文引入 compatibility-aware gating 和 layer-wise pruning。前者学习或估计哪些跨子结构组合是兼容的，抑制低价值的 off-diagonal 路径；后者根据层敏感性做结构裁剪，在对质量更敏感的层保留更多路径，在冗余更高的层更激进地剪枝。作者强调的一点是，vanilla expert path 仍被保留在可选空间里，因此 path composition 不是完全替代原模型，而是在原路径之外开放额外的重组轨迹。\n\n系统实现上，作者做了一个 hardware-friendly runtime。关键不是把路径自由度无限放大，而是把活跃路径映射成 source-grouped compute clusters，共享 expansion-side 操作，减少 dispatch/scatter 带来的碎片化开销。换句话说，算法层面开放更细粒度路径，系统层面再把这些路径重新聚合成硬件友好的执行批次。这个 co-design 是论文成立的关键；如果只有 path 组合而没有执行引擎，组合空间带来的调度成本很可能会吃掉全部收益。","experiments":"从给出的全文内容看，实验结论主要有两类。第一类是端到端 serving 效率：PCoMoE 在所评估设置下最高达到 1.31× 推理加速。这个数字不算极端，但如果是在保持模型主体不变、且考虑到 MoE 系统本身已有较多优化的前提下，说明细粒度路径重组确实能带来可见收益。第二类是任务性能：作者声称相对 vanilla MoE baseline，在下游任务上最高可有约 10% 的准确率提升。这里需要注意，这个“10%”大概率是相对提升或特定任务上的增益，而不是统一的绝对百分点；阅读原表时要区分平均提升、最佳任务提升、以及是否在相同延迟预算下比较。\n\n从方法描述推断，最值得看的消融应包括三部分：是否保留 compatibility-aware pruning、不同层的剪枝强度、以及 runtime grouping 对真实吞吐的贡献。如果没有这些消融，单看总加速很难判断收益究竟来自路径设计还是来自更好的 kernel / batching。另一个应重点核对的是质量-效率曲线：PCoMoE 是否在相同质量下更快，还是在相同延迟下更准；这两种结论的含义不同。由于当前提供的全文片段没有完整表格和模型规模细节，参数量、专家数、batch setting、硬件平台等具体数字还需要回到原文实验节确认。","reading_guide":"泛读建议是先看 Figure 1 和 Figure 2 对 vanilla expert 与 compositional path 的对比，把“为什么 whole-expert abstraction 限制了优化空间”看明白。然后直接读方法部分关于 compatibility-aware gating、layer-wise pruning、以及 runtime execution engine 的描述，判断它到底是模型改造、编译优化，还是两者结合。实验部分优先看三类表：延迟/吞吐、质量-效率 trade-off、消融。尤其要确认 speedup 是 token latency、throughput，还是端到端 serving latency。\n\n这篇更适合带着问题去读，预计 representation 由 gate projection 和 up projection 共同形成，再经过 down projection 输出，某个 residual 误差的影响会被中间激活、逐元素乘法和 down projection 放大或抵消。PARSER 因此优先压缩那些移除后对专家输出扰动较小的 hidden dimensions，而不是单纯保留矩阵重构误差较小的元素或维度。","experiments":"实验覆盖 Qwen 和 DeepSeek 系列 MoE 模型。论文报告在达到相近峰值显存降低的条件下，PARSER 相比最强对照方法进一步缩小压缩模型与未压缩模型之间的精度差距：Qwen 上为 1.41×，DeepSeek 上为 1.44×。这说明改进主要来自更好的压缩目标，而不是牺牲压缩率换精度。根据已给内容，具体任务集、模型规模和逐项消融数字未能完整确认；阅读时需要重点核对不同压缩率下的 accuracy-memory 曲线，以及 output importance 相比 projection-wise error 的独立贡献。","reading_guide":"建议泛读，约 15–20 分钟。优先看第 3 minimize reconstruction error for each expert projection matrix, while model quality is governed by the final expert output after the coupled gate, up, activation, element-wise product, and down projections.","importance":"The main lesson is about objective alignment in MoE compression. For large MoE models, preserving parameter-space similarity is not necessarily the right proxy for preserving expert behavior. This is relevant to post-training compression and serving of pretrained MoE models, and it may also inform future training-time sparsification or expert regularization schemes. The contribution is a focused engineering-method improvement rather than a new pretraining paradigm, so a skim is sufficient.","technical_approach":"PARSER keeps the standard residual sparsification setup. Each projection type in an MoE layer is decomposed into a shared base matrix and per-expert residual matrices, and memory is reduced by compressing the residuals. The key change is the criterion used to decide what to compress. Prior methods treat the up, gate, and down projections largely independently and minimize residual reconstruction error. PARSER instead introduces output importance, a score that estimates how much removing or compressing a hidden dimension changes the final expert output. This better matches the computation inside SwiGLU-style experts, where gate and up errors first interact in the hidden representation and are then transformed by the down projection.","experiments":"The reported experiments cover Qwen and DeepSeek MoE models. At comparable peak-memory reduction, PARSER reduces the accuracy gap to the uncompressed model by 1.41× on Qwen and 1.44× on DeepSeek relative to the strongest compared residual-sparsification methods. practical systems problem in multi-LLM deployments: when control is handed from one model to another, the receiver cannot directly reuse the sender’s KV cache because caches are model-specific internal states tied to architecture, layers, heads, and dimensions. The target model therefore has to re-prefill the shared prefix, which becomes expensive for long contexts. A recent closed-form baseline, Full-Head Mapping, fits an offline affine map from source KV states to target KV states, but it assumes every target head should regress on all source heads. That creates three issues: sensitivity to architectural mismatch, a gap between cache reconstruction quality and actual continuation quality, and rapidly growing storage and application cost as support expands. CacheBridge aims to keep the same training-free, affine online interface while making cross-model KV transfer more accurate, cheaper, and more robust.","importance":"This is not a core pretraining paper, but it matters for serving and for understanding internal representation alignment across models. Full-Head Mapping already suggested that some linear transferability exists between models’ attention states. CacheBridge sharpens that observation: the transferable structure is not uniformly dense across all heads, but has architecture-local regularities. That is useful for foundation-model researchers for three reasons. First, it provides a more fine-grained view of how much internal attention state is shared across related models. Second, it moves the fitting objective away from coordinate-level cache regression toward attention-level functional fidelity, which is closer to what actually matters for generation. Third, it shows that offline fitting cost can itself become a deployment bottleneck if static structure is ignored. Overall, this is a strong systems-and-representation paper rather than a new modeling paradigm.","technical_approach":"$26","experiments":"$27","reading_guide":"For a quick read, start with the introduction and Figure 1. In the experiments, prioritize transfer quality, target retention, storage and application latency, and construction time. For tracking the idea, 25–35 minutes is enough. State-of-the-art PTQ methods quantize each layer with a single closed-form second-order solver: to remain analytically tractable, they heavily approximate the global loss (dropping cross-channel coupling, pooling output rows into groups), and they then freeze the resulting Hessian across the entire layer, with no way to refresh it as the loss landscape shifts column by colu","starred":false,"priority":"p2","reading_tier":"skim","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"","category_id":"efficient","cover_url":"/covers/2609_00049.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.LG","cs.AI"],"summary":{"core_problem":"这篇工作解决的是 LLM 后训练量化里一个很具体但长期存在的问题：主流 GPTQ 系方法为了保留列级闭式求解，会把真实的端到端量化损失近似得过于粗糙，既忽略上游已量化层带来的输入扰动，也忽略下游任务损失对不同输出通道的非均匀敏感性；同时 Hessian 在整层内被冻结，随着列逐步量化，优化目标已经变了，求解器却还在用旧信息。作者把这个问题概括为 information misalignment。","importance":"它对基座研究的意义主要在部署与训练接口上。量化本身属于推理效率，但这篇工作有两点值得预训练研究员关注。第一，它把“层内局部重建误差最小”与“端到端 KL 最小”之间的错位讲得很清楚，这种目标错位分析对蒸馏、剪枝、低秩适配甚至训练期近似优化都有参考价值。第二，它说明在大模型压缩里，静态二阶近似未必比动态一阶更新更合适；当目标随量化过程持续漂移时，在线刷新梯度信息比一次性求 Hessian 更重要。这个判断不只适用于 PTQ，也和很多 sequential optimization 问题相通。","technical_approach":"REAL-Q 的方法可以概括成“换目标 + 换求解方式”。\n\n先说目标。作者不再用 GPTQ 那种 layer-local reconstruction MSE，而是构造了一个 end-to-end-aligned surrogate：Aggregated Fisher MSE。它来自对全局输出 KL divergence 在当前 block 输出处做二阶 Taylor 展开。与很多近似方法不同，这里保留了完整 aggregated Fisher matrix，因此不再把输出通道之间的耦合简单丢掉。为了控制计算量，展开点选在 block 输出，使反向传播只需覆盖至多相邻两个 block，而不是整网回传。另一个细节是 loss sliding window：在顺序量化相邻 transformer block 时，目标函数如果突然从一个 block 切到下一个 block，优化景观会不连续，作者用滑动窗口平滑这个过渡，减少 block boundary 处的抖动。\n\n再说求解。作者认为 GPTQ lineage 的根本限制在于必须维持列级闭式解，所以只能接受被压缩过的 Hessian，并且整层内固定不变。REAL-Q 改成 Dynamic Block-wise Gradient Descent：仍沿用列式顺序量化框架，但每量化完一个 column block（128 列）就插入一次 Adam 更新。这个更新直接对当前“部分已量化”的真实状态重新计算梯度，因此能在线修正上游误差累积，也能跟随损失景观变化。作者强调这是把修正粒度从 per-layer 提升到 per-column-block，细了大约两个数量级。Adam 的对角预条件器在这里起到隐式二阶信息的作用，但不需要显式求逆 Hessian。\n\n从方法定位看，REAL-Q 不是完全抛弃 GPTQ 的 sequential pipeline，而是在其框架内引入更贴近端到端目标的损失和更高频的动态修正。这一点很实际，因为它兼顾了现有推理栈的兼容性与性能改进。","experiments":"实验覆盖 LLaMA-3.1 8B、70B，以及 Qwen3 0.6B 到 32B 多个规模，主要看 W4A16 设定下的 KL divergence，并与 GPTQ 及其后续改进方法比较。作者给出的 headline 结果是：REAL-Q 在所有评测模型上都取得最低 KL，在 Qwen3-1.7B 的 W4A16 上，KL 最多可下降约 49%。这个结果支持两件事：一是端到端对齐目标比层局部 MSE 更有效；二是在线动态更新确实能缓解 sequential quantization 中的信息失配。\n\n从论文叙述看，消融重点应包括三部分：Aggregated Fisher MSE 相对普通重建损失的收益、loss sliding window 对跨 block 稳定性的作用、以及 Block-GD 的更新频率与是否使用 Adam 的影响。虽然给到的全文片段没有列出完整表格数字，但方法设计已经暗示作者会比较“只换目标不换求解”“只做动态更新不用 Fisher loss”“不同 block size”这几类设置。阅读时应特别看这些消融，因为它们决定性能提升究竟来自更好的 loss，还是来自更频繁的 online correction。","reading_guide":"泛读建议先看 Introduction，把作者对 GPTQ 系方法三类近似的批评读清楚：未校正上游输入扰动、未建模下游通道敏感性、层内 Hessian 静态冻结。然后直接看方法部分里 Aggregated Fisher MSE 的推导和 Block-GD 的算法框图，理解为什么反传只需局部 block，以及为什么每 128 列更新一次是一个折中。\n\n结果部分优先看主表和消融表。主表回答“是否稳定赢”；消融回答“赢在哪里”。如果只打算花 25–35 分钟，读完引言、方法总览、主结果和关键消融就够了。若要深挖，建议看附录里的复杂度分析、校准数据设置、以及不同模型规模下收益是否一致。另一个值得留意的点是：KL 改善能否稳定转化成下游困惑度或生成质量改善，这决定它在真实部署中的含义。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"The paper targets a specific weakness in post-training quantization for LLMs. GPTQ-style methods keep the optimization analytically solvable at the column level, but they do so by over-simplifying the true end-to-end objective. They ignore perturbed inputs coming from already quantized upstream layers, treat output-channel errors too uniformly relative to the downstream loss, and freeze second-order information across an entire layer even though the loss landscape changes as columns are quantized one by one. The authors call this mismatch information misalignment.","importance":"The direct domain is inference efficiency, but the paper is still relevant to foundation-model researchers for two reasons. First, it gives a clean analysis of objective mismatch: minimizing layer-local reconstruction error is not the same as minimizing end-to-end KL. That framing is useful beyond quantization, including distillation, pruning, low-rank adaptation, and other approximate optimization settings. Second, it argues that a dynamic first-order correction can be better than a static second-order approximation when the target keeps drifting during a sequential procedure. That lesson generalizes to many optimization problems around large models.","technical_approach":"$28","experiments":"$29","reading_guide":"$2a","authors_note":""},"recommendation":{"zh":"这篇不改 pretrain，但对“二阶 PTQ 足够好”的 prior 有实质修正；先看方法图和消融，重点判断动态更新的收益是否只在超低比特才成立。","en":"It does not change pretraining, but it meaningfully challenges the prior that static second-order PTQ is good enough; read the method figure and ablations first, and check whether the gain only exists in ultra-low-bit regimes."},"metadata":{"institutions":[],"authors":["Qian Zhang","Yaoming Li","Zhewen Tan","Yanshu Wang","Heng Lu","Kun Su","Zongwei Lv","Wenhan Yu","Yongge Ma","Yinjun Han","Ruikuang Liu","Tong Yang"],"notable_authors":["Yinjun Han"],"tags":["quantization","PTQ","LLM-inference","second-order","low-bit"],"links":{"arxiv":"https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00049","pdf":"https://arxiv.org/pdf/2609.00049","github":null,"project":null,"source_url":null,"article":null}}},{"id":"2609.00224","title":"QTEA: Ternary LLMs with Sparse Residual Salient Weight and By-Column Optimization","abstract":"Weight-only post-training quantization (PTQ) can alleviate the computational burden of serving large language models (LLMs) at scale. We present QTEA, a sub-2-bit PTQ framework that quantizes weights into ternary values and uses salient weights as residual error compe","starred":false,"priority":"p2","reading_tier":"skim","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"","category_id":"efficient","cover_url":"/covers/2609_00224.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.LG","cs.AI"],"summary":{"core_problem":"这篇工作解决的是超低比特 weight-only PTQ 在 2 bit 以下精度急剧崩塌的问题，目标是在尽量不牺牲 GPU 友好性的前提下，把 LLM 权重量化到 ternary 级别。已有方法通常在两件事之间做取舍：要么保留少量高精度 salient weights 来救精度，但引入不规则稀疏和访存开销；要么保持结构规整，但浪费 bit budget，导致精度不够。QTEA 试图把这两件事同时兼顾。","importance":"它对基座预训练本身不是直接贡献，更偏 serving/efficient inference，但对大模型研究仍有现实意义。原因很简单：低 batch 自回归解码常常是 memory-bandwidth bound，权重搬运比算力更贵。若 sub-2-bit PTQ 能在较少精度损失下落地，就会改变大模型部署的成本结构。论文的价值主要在两个层面：一是提出一种更适合 ternary 场景的误差补偿表示；二是把算法设计和 kernel 实现一起做了，给出实际吞吐提升，而不是只报 perplexity。","technical_approach":"QTEA 的核心思路不是“把重要权重跳过量化”，而是“先把所有权重都压成 ternary base，再把有限预算用在纠正最伤精度的残差上”。这个视角很关键，因为在 sub-2-bit 区间，直接保留原值的成本太高，残差补偿比 bypass 更划算。\n\n方法由三部分组成。\n\n第一部分是 ternary base。论文沿用 PT2-LLM 一类做法，对每个 quantization group 估计 scale α 和 bias β，把权重映射到 {β-α, β, β+α} 三个点。这样所有权重先得到一个极紧凑的三值表示。\n\n第二部分是 salient residual 设计。作者提出 column-semi sparse salient weight：先在列级别选择重要列，再在这些列内部只保留满足 1:4 semi-structured sparsity 的少量高影响条目，作为对 ternary base 的残差修正。这个设计比完全非结构化 salient weight 更规整，便于高效 kernel；又比整列高精度保留更省 bit budget，因为不会把一整列里大量不重要元素一起带上。换句话说，它把“列级规则性”和“元素级选择性”折中到一个硬件较友好的形式里。\n\n第三部分是 GPTQ 风格逐列量化过程的改造。作者认为标准 column-by-column GPTQ 在 ternary 场景下有两个问题：\n1）初始 scale 固定后表达能力不足。为此加入 column-wise rescale refinement，在逐列量化时交替优化每列 rescale 因子和 ternary assignment，让 ternary base 更贴近局部分布。\n2）误差传播存在顺序不平衡。早期列的量化误差可以分摊到很多后续列，晚期列可分摊的空间很小，如果仍用同样强度传播，后段容易过补偿。为此提出 error decay，让误差传播强度随列顺序逐渐衰减，减轻后期误差累积。\n\n这两点都不是大框架创新，但很贴近 GPTQ 实际工作机制，属于把 ultra-low-bit 场景下的失真来源拆开处理。整体上，QTEA 是“ternary base + 结构化残差补偿 + 逐列量化细化”的组合方案。","experiments":"实验主要在 Qwen3-14B 和 Llama3-8B 上做。主结果显示，QTEA 在 Qwen3-14B 上相对当时最强的 ternary PTQ 基线，下游任务平均准确率提升 16.7%，WikiText 和 C4 上 perplexity 分别降低 1.40× 和 2.61×。在 Llama3-8B 上，平均准确率提升 6.6%，WikiText 和 C4 perplexity 分别降低 1.34× 和 1.95×。从这些数字看，收益在更难量化的模型上更明显，说明方法对模型分布差异有一定鲁棒性。\n\n论文还强调 size-perplexity trade-off：在 Qwen3 上，QTEA 在相近模型大小下给出更低 perplexity，说明残差预算确实被更有效地用在了“最伤精度”的位置。\n\n效率方面，作者实现了专用 CUDA kernel，并结合 CUDA Graphs。最终相对带 CUDA Graphs 的 FP16 baseline，生成速度最高提升 7.2×；相对不带 CUDA Graphs 的 FP16，最高 13.3×。这里需要理性看待：速度提升既来自量化，也来自 kernel 和 graph 优化，不能全部归功于量化算法本身。但论文至少做到了算法和系统闭环，没有停留在离线指标。\n\n从方法验证角度，最值得注意的消融应当是三块：\n- salient residual 的表示方式，对比非结构化或整列保留；\n- column-wise rescale refinement 是否显著改善 ternary assignment；\n- error decay 是否缓解后段列的误差累积。\n摘要给出的全文片段没有列出完整消融数字，但从方法组织看，这三项应当分别对应精度、稳定性和硬件友好性的主要来源。","reading_guide":"泛读时先看 Introduction，作者把问题讲得比较清楚：为什么低 batch 解码是带宽瓶颈，为什么 sub-2-bit 难，现有 salient-weight 方案各自牺牲了什么。然后直接看 Quantized Ternary Error Adaptation 这一节，重点抓三件事：ternary base 的定义、column-semi sparse residual 的结构、以及 GPTQ 量化流程里 rescale refinement 和 error decay 的插入位置。\n\n如果只想判断方法是否值得跟进，建议重点看两类图表：\n1）不同模型上的 perplexity/accuracy 对比，确认它不是只在单一模型上有效；\n2）吞吐和 kernel 结果，确认结构化设计是否真的换来了可执行效率。\n\n预计 20 到 30 分钟可以读完主线。若要深入，建议细看方法节里对“为什么列后段更容易过补偿”的解释，以及附录中的 kernel 细节。对做 pretrain 的人来说，最有启发的不是量化公式本身，而是作者如何把误差来源拆成表示、优化顺序、硬件结构三层分别处理。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"The paper targets the sharp accuracy collapse of weight-only post-training quantization below 2 bits, especially in the ternary regime. The challenge is to preserve model quality without giving up hardware-friendly structure. Existing methods usually trade one for the other: they either keep a small set of salient weights in higher precision and suffer irregular memory access, or they preserve regularity but waste bit budget and lose too much accuracy. 是否同时成立。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"The paper studies whether compressed context for an LLM must remain readable text or be reconstructed as text before use. LatentPress instead writes dialogue histories and long documents into continuous memory tokens that a frozen decoder consumes directly through its input-embedding interface.","importance":"The contribution is a representation-level interface for long-context memory. It treats context as a compact latent prefix rather than a shorter natural-language string. Since the decoder is frozen and only a small writer adapter is trained, the method is closer to an external memory interface than to training a new long-context model. This is relevant for agent memory, long-document QA, and alternatives to pure retrieval or KV-cache-based compression. The trade-offs are also important: the latent tokens are reader-specific, not human-readable, and the current compression allocation is mostly heuristic.","technical_approach":"LatentPress separates context handling into Write and Read. The writer maps text segments, such as dialogue turns or document chunks, into a shorter sequence of continuous vectors. These vectors live in the frozen reader’s embedding space and are prepended to the question at the input-embedding layer. The writer is matched to a specific reader, reuses two frozen decoder layers, and trains only a small adapter. For structured conversations, the method uses a simple segment-level compression schedule; for unstructured documents, it uses a uniform rate. Unlike reconstruction-based compression, there is no latent-to-text decoding at inference time. Unlike approaches that adapt an LLM-scale reader, the downstream decoder stays unchanged. Compared with xRAG-style single-passage compression, LatentPress targets multi-turn histories and whole documents with variable-length compressed prefixes.","experiments":"The main evaluations are on LongMemEval and LongBench-QA. On LongMemEval, a writer trained on generic UltraChat conversations transfers to unseen memory-QA labels and reaches about 0.48 to 0.50 accuracy across three frozen readers at 4.6x to 7.7x compression. A representative result is 0.504 accuracy at 7.70x compression, compared with 0.490 for uncompressed evidence, 0.184 for text summaries, and roughly 0.312 to 0.426 for OCR-based compression. On LongBench-QA, cross-domain compressed readers degrade at the most aggressive rates, while in-domain QA supervision lets the compressed reader match or exceed raw-context baselines at mild compression. The reported trainable footprint is 4.2M to 26.2M parameters, around 0.1% of the decoder. Writing a conversation takes about 43 ms, and warm-loaded inference from the compressed prefix is 5x to 9x faster than reading the original context.","reading_guide":"A focused skim takes about 25 to 35 minutes. In the experiments, focus on the compression-rate versus accuracy curves, the LongMemEval transfer setup, and the LongBench-QA split between cross-domain and in-domain supervision. We measure what such a suite can detect. Injecting nine faults into a reference INT8 pipeline over 8,232 layer--fault--regime cells of Qwen3-1.7B, we find that every one of five epilogue faults -- scale precision, double rounding, multiplication order, output truncation, fused ordering -- moves the output by at most a single bfloat16 spacing, and by exactly one whenever it moves it at all, W8A8 INT8 GEMM 接口的 GPU kernel，是否可以被当作可互换的“等价实现”。前作已经证明答案是否定的，并把差异定位到 epilogue，也就是 scale 乘法和最终舍入，而不是 INT32 accumulator。本篇进一步补上两个缺口：一是已有 conformance suite 到底能不能真的抓到错误；二是 power-of-two quantization scales 能否在不明显伤害模型质量的前提下，把跨 kernel 推理变成 bitwise deterministic。","importance":"它对预训练本身不是直接贡献，但对基座模型的评测、部署、回归测试和实验可复现性有很强现实意义。现在很多 serving 栈默认把不同 kernel 当成实现细节，只要接口一致就视为等价；这篇文章说明，在 INT8 serving 下这个假设并不成立。更重要的是，它给出一个很清楚的结论：对 epilogue 这类浮点后处理，容差式一致性检查存在结构性盲区，不是把阈值调得更细就能解决。若要做到真正可检查的跨 kernel 一致性，需要把 scale 约束到 power-of-two，使 epilogue 退化为可精确验证的路径。这对做大规模评测、缓存 logits、线上回归和审计链路的人都很重要。","technical_approach":"$2e","experiments":"最重要的实验结论有两组。\n\n第一组是 conformance suite 的灵敏度测量。对修正后的 77-cell 预测矩阵，作者报告 0 false positive、0 false negative；但这并不意味着 suite 足够全面。相反，5 类 epilogue fault 中有 4 类，在全部 4,704 个相关 cell 里没有任何一个检查能抓到。这个结果很强，因为它说明盲区不是偶发，而是系统性的。作者把原因归结为前面提到的“一个 bfloat16 spacing 悖论”：错误与合法差异共享同一最小容差区间。\n\n第二组是 power-of-two scales 的质量代价。前作报告的 +157% perplexity 恶化，被本文证明是错误构造导致的假象。重新 requantize 后，在三个模型规模上，观测到的 perplexity 点估计变化落在 -0.28% 到 +0.48% 之间，同时两个 kernel 可以做到 byte-for-byte 一致。这个结果的含义很直接：power-of-two 约束不再只是诊断工具，而是一个可能实际可用的部署选项。\n\n实验材料方面，作者使用 Qwen3-1.7B 捕获的 196 层操作数做 fault replay，这比纯合成输入更接近真实 serving 分布。文章还强调了预注册协议和 append-only amendment，说明 fault catalogue、阈值、分析规则在主实验前就已固定，只在公开 smoke run 后做有限修正。这种实验纪律对这类“测试测试器”的工作很重要。","reading_guide":"建议先看 Introduction，把它和前作的关系理顺：前作回答“差异在哪里”，这篇回答“怎么测、怎么修”。然后直接看 fault injection 设计和主结果图表，重点理解为什么 0 FP/0 FN 仍然不代表 suite 足够好。接着看关于 one-bfloat16-spacing 的论证，这是全文最关键的机制解释。最后看 power-of-two requantization 的实验，确认 perplexity 区间和 byte-for-byte 一致性结果。对做 serving、评测复现、量化部署的人，这三点已经足够。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"The paper studies a practical but important reproducibility question for LLM serving: are two GPU kernels implementing the same W8A8 INT8 GEMM interface actually interchangeable? A companion paper had already shown that they are not bitwise equivalent and localized the disagreement to the epilogue—scale multiplication and final rounding—rather than the exact INT32 accumulator. This follow-up closes two open gaps: whether the proposed conformance suite is truly sensitive to faults, and whether power-of-two quantization scales can make cross-kernel inference bitwise deterministic without materially hurting model quality.","importance":"This is not a pretraining-method paper, but it matters for foundation-model evaluation, deployment, regression testing, and auditability. Many serving stacks treat kernel choice as an implementation detail as long as the interface matches. The paper shows that this assumption breaks under INT8 inference. More importantly, it makes a stronger point: tolerance-based conformance checks have a structural blind spot for epilogue faults. The issue is not poor threshold tuning; legal floating-point variation and faulty behavior can occupy the same tolerance interval. If one wants truly checkable cross-kernel determinism, the scale parameterization itself has to be constrained, and power-of-two scales provide that route.","technical_approach":"$2f","experiments":"$30","reading_guide":"Start with the introduction to place this paper relative to the companion work: the earlier paper answered where the discrepancy comes from, while this one asks how to test for it and how to remove it. After that, read the argument around the one-bfloat16-spacing limit; that is the central mechanism. Near-Data Processing (NDP) provides a promising way to mitigate this bottleneck via cooperative NPU-NDP execution. However, existing NPU-NDP MoE systems do not fully account for hardware heterogeneity, dynamic expert-level concurrency, and temporal expert reuse during batched inference. Thi","starred":false,"priority":"p2","reading_tier":"skim","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"","category_id":"efficient","cover_url":"/covers/2609_00407.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.AR","cs.LG"],"summary":{"core_problem":"这项工作解决的是 MoE 大模型批量推理中的专家调度问题。MoE 扩大了参数容量，但专家参数通常放在外部内存，按 gating 结果动态搬到 NPU，导致参数搬运成为主要延迟来源。已有 NPU-NDP 协同方案 MoNDE 用近数据计算减少搬运，但调度偏启发式，没有同时建模硬件异构、专家并发、参数与 activation 传输、计算通信重叠，以及 NPU 内已有专家的复用。","importance":"对预训练方法本身影响有限，但对 MoE 基座模型的部署和训练后服务有直接参考价值。它提示一个现实约束：MoE 的稀疏激活并不自动带来低延迟，专家参数放置、批内路由分布和硬件内存层级会决定有效吞吐。对做 MoE 预训练的人也有启发，因为训练时的路由负载均衡指标并不能完全代表服务时的硬件效率，专家复用率和批内专家并发同样重要。","technical_approach":"DynaNDE 的核心是用分析性能模型驱动运行时专家调度。模型同时估计 NPU 执行专家、NDP 执行专家、参数从外部内存搬到 NPU、activation 送到 NDP、以及 NPU 与 NDP 计算/通信重叠后的端到端时间。运行时根据每层 gating 产生的实际专家激活集合，决定哪些专家留给 NPU，哪些交给 NDP。与 MoNDE 的主要区别在于，DynaNDE 不是固定启发式分配，而是显式考虑硬件异构和 batch 内专家级并发。另外，它加入 reuse awareness：如果某个专家已经驻留在 NPU memory 中，调度器会把这个状态纳入代价估计，避免重复搬运同一专家参数。","experiments":"关键结果是相对 MoNDE 的端到端加速：在多个 MoE 模型上，prefill 阶段平均加速 2.6 倍，decoding 阶段平均加速 2.2 倍。正文动机部分的 profiling 显示，专家参数传输在 offloading 式 MoE 推理中占据主要时间，NPU 计算资源因此出现 stall。实验的有效信息主要来自三点：第一，prefill 和 decoding 都受益，说明方法不只是吃 batch 大的红利；第二，reuse awareness 对减少冗余参数传输有意义；第三，动态调度比只做 NPU-NDP 协同更关键，因为错误的专家放置会把节省下来的带宽重新花在 activation 或参数搬运上。","reading_guide":"建议泛读，预计 25 到 35 分钟。重点看 Introduction 和 Background 中对 MoE offloading/NDP 的问题拆解，确认瓶颈是否符合自己的 serving 场景；再看性能模型和 runtime scheduling 部分，关注模型里如何表示参数传输、activation 传输、计算通信 overlap 和专家驻留状态；最后看消融实验，尤其是去掉 reuse awareness、去掉动态调度或改变 batch/gating 分布后的结果。若关注预训练 recipe，可主要吸收系统约束对 MoE 架构设计的反向影响，不必深究 NDP 硬件实现细节。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"The paper targets expert scheduling for batched MoE inference on heterogeneous NPU-NDP systems. Large MoE models often keep expert weights in external memory and fetch selected experts on demand after routing. This makes weight movement a dominant source of latency. Prior NPU-NDP cooperative execution reduces some movement, but its scheduling does not jointly account for hardware heterogeneity, expert-level concurrency in a batch, parameter and activation transfers, communication-computation overlap, and reuse of experts already resident in NPU memory.","importance":"The contribution is mainly a serving-systems result rather than a pretraining method. It is still relevant for MoE foundation models because sparse activation only reduces arithmetic cost; it does not automatically reduce latency when expert weights are offloaded. The paper highlights a useful feedback signal for MoE design: routing balance during training is not the only systems-facing metric. Batch-level expert reuse, expert residency, and memory hierarchy behavior can materially affect usable throughput.","technical_approach":"DynaNDE uses an analytical performance model to guide runtime expert placement. The model estimates the cost of executing experts on the NPU versus near-data units, including expert-weight transfers, activation movement, heterogeneous compute rates, and overlap between communication and computation. At each MoE layer, the scheduler observes the actual expert activations produced by the router and assigns expert work dynamically across NPU and NDP. The main difference from MoNDE is that the decision is model-driven rather than a fixed heuristic. DynaNDE also tracks experts already cached in NPU memory, so the scheduler can avoid redundant weight transfers when reuse is possible.","experiments":"The headline result is an average speedup over MoNDE of 2.6x in prefill and 2.2x in decoding across evaluated MoE models. The motivation profiling shows that expert-parameter movement dominates execution time in offloaded MoE inference and causes NPU stalls. The important experimental reading is not just the aggregate speedup, but the decomposition: dynamic scheduling helps because the active expert set changes by layer and batch; reuse awareness helps because temporal locality in routing can remove repeated weight movement; and the prefill/decoding split suggests the approach is not limited to a single inference phase.","reading_guide":"Skim in about 25–35 minutes. Its main value is the evaluation framing: a learned GUI simulator should be judged by rollout consistency and task progress, not only by local visual or semantic prediction quality. This matters if GUI world models are later used for scalable agent training, planning, or offline RL, where small state errors can compound once generated observations are fed back into the loop.","technical_approach":"GUI-CC has two tracks. The offline reference-action track starts from a real initial UI and rolls the world model forward along a reference semantic action sequence. The model only sees its own generated screens after the first step; reference screenshots are reserved for scoring. This isolates the world model from policy errors and tests whether app identity, navigation context, task entities, saved state, and action affordances remain coherent. The online agent-loop track places a frozen probing agent inside the generated environment and measures whether closed-loop interaction can make progress. Actions are represented with a semantic schema rather than raw pixels, covering tap, long press, scroll, text input, app opening, back, home, and wait. The evaluation separates transition fidelity, transition plausibility, contextual consistency, and task progress.","experiments":"The benchmark contains 500 offline trajectory tasks from GUIOdyssey and 200 emulator-verified online tasks, spanning 30 mobile apps and 18 task templates. The reported findings are mostly about failure modes: models can obtain reasonable local transition scores while still drifting to the wrong app or page, delaying action effects, losing user-created or task-relevant state, or producing plausible screens that no longer support later actions. History conditioning helps in some cases, but task-progress scores remain limited, especially for longer rollouts and closed-loop agent interaction.","reading_guide":"Skim in about 20–30 minutes. Focus on the benchmark formulation, the two-track design, and the scoring dimensions. The most useful parts are the places where contextual consistency and task progress diverge from one-step transition quality. Failure examples are worth reading carefully because they expose the practical risks of using learned GUI environments for agent training.","authors_note":""},"recommendation":{"zh":"这类 benchmark 对预训练只属旁支；若做 GUI/world-model 数据闭环，直接看两条 track 的设计。","en":"Peripheral for pretraining, but useful if you build GUI/world-model data loops; read the two-track benchmark design and skip most leaderboard details."},"metadata":{"institutions":["Yale University","Zhejiang University","China University of Geosciences","DAMO Academy, Alibaba Group","Tongji University","University of California, San Diego","Affiliation:DAMO Academy, Alibaba GroupCodebaseDataset","Affiliation:Yale University","Affiliation:Zhejiang University","Affiliation:China University of Geosciences","Affiliation:Tongji University","Affiliation:University of California, San Diego"],"authors":["Lin Fu","Zheyuan Yang","Tianhui Zhang","Jinbiao Wei","Guo Gan","Boxu Liu","Yilun Zhao","Yu Rong"],"notable_authors":[],"tags":["GUI-agents","world-models","benchmark","contextual-consistency"],"links":{"arxiv":"https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00048","pdf":"https://arxiv.org/pdf/2609.00048","github":null,"project":null,"source_url":null,"article":null}}},{"id":"2609.00062","title":"RePro: Proof-Verified Benchmark Rewriting for Reliable Evaluation of LLM Mathematical Problem Solving","abstract":"Data contamination undermines the reliable evaluation of large language models (LLMs) on mathematical problem solving. While rewriting-based evaluation mitigates memorization, existing methods lack guarantees of problem validity and answer correctness. We propose Proof-Verified Benchmark Rewriting (RePro), the first framework to integrate Lean-oriented neural automated theorem provers (ATPs) into benchmark rewriting, which rewrites problems and regenerates answers with correctness ensured by Lea","starred":false,"priority":"p2","reading_tier":"skim","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"","category_id":"eval","cover_url":"/covers/2609_00062.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.CL","cs.AI"],"summary":{"core_problem":"这篇工作处理的是“重写式数学评测不可靠”这个很具体但很关键的问题。现有去污染评测常把原题改写后再测模型，以降低训练集记忆带来的高分；但改写后的题目常出现两个硬伤：题意本身不再严谨，或者参考答案已经错了。前者会让评测对象变成一堆语义含混、约束冲突、不可解的问题；后者更直接，会把模型答对判成错，或者把答错判成对。RePro要解决的不是单纯多生成一些改写题，而是构造一套能对“题目是否定义良好、是否可行、答案是否正确”给出机器可验证保证的重写流程，尤其面向可形式化的数学题。","importance":"对预训练研究的意义主要在评测侧，而不是训练方法侧。它提供了一种更干净的数学能力测量方式，用来区分“模型真的会推理”与“模型见过类似题”。这对做数据去污、训练数据成员推断、推理能力真实性分析都很重要。贡献量级上，它不是改变模型训练范式的工作，但在数学评测可靠性上补上了一个长期缺口：过去很多重写 benchmark 只是降低表面重复，却没有保证改写后样本仍然成立。RePro把 Lean 验证引入重写管线后，至少在保留下来的样本上，把参考答案正确性从启发式判断提升到了形式证明支持。这使它更像一个高精度评测资产构建方法，而不是普通的数据增强。","technical_approach":"方法设计是一个三阶段、逐步收紧的验证管线。第一阶段是 LLM 重写。模型对原题做数值重参数化、逻辑重组、约束修改、语境重构等改写，但被明确要求不要直接求解新题，目的是把“题目生成”和“答案生成”解耦，减少模型顺手沿用原答案或在改写时偷偷完成求解。第二阶段是保守语义筛查。这里先过滤掉明显不合格的候选题，比如条件缺失、表述歧义、前提矛盾、现实约束不成立、无解或多解但答案类型仍假定唯一值等。论文强调 feasibility 不是形式逻辑里“能否从前提推出结论”那么窄，而是题目语义和约束是否自洽。第三阶段是形式化与证明验证。候选题被翻译成 Lean 可执行规格，再由 Lean-oriented neural ATP，如 Goedel-Prover、DeepSeek-Prover，搜索候选证明脚本；只有通过 Lean kernel 检查的证明才被接受。这里的关键不是“神经 ATP 会证明”，而是“神经 ATP 只负责提案，最终裁决交给 Lean 内核”。这一步把神经模型的不稳定性关进了 proof assistant 的边界内。\n\n和已有重写评测相比，差异有三点。第一，验证对象从“看起来像对”变成“有形式证明支持的答案”。第二，验证粒度从单一答案核对扩展到 well-definedness、feasibility、answer correctness 三个层面。第三，RePro只保留通过严格验证的样本，接受覆盖率下降来换取高精度。这种取舍很像高质量预训练数据清洗：宁可少，也不要把脏样本混进核心评测集。","experiments":"实验主要在 GSM8K 和 MATH 上做，重点不是追求大规模覆盖，而是比较不同重写方法生成样本的可靠性。论文给出的核心结果是：在保留下来的 rewritten instances 上，RePro 达到 100% 的 well-definedness、feasibility 和 answer correctness；而已有方法即使能生成不少改写题，仍会留下无效题或错误参考答案。这个结果说明 Lean 验证确实改变了 benchmark 质量上限，而不是只做了更严格的人工筛查。\n\n另一条重要实验线是 reformulation sensitivity。作者用 proof-verified rewrites 去测模型在“同一数学结构、不同表述方式”下的性能变化，发现若干模型在经过证明验证的改写题上准确率明显下降。这一点很关键，因为它把性能下降更可信地归因到表述变化和潜在记忆依赖，而不是改写题本身坏掉。论文还讨论了 retained rewritten instances 的概念：不是所有改写都能被形式化并证明，因此最终发布的是通过验证的子集。这个 retained subset 的高精度，是方法成立的前提；代价则是召回率和题型覆盖受限，尤其对难以形式化的自然语言数学题更明显。","reading_guide":"泛读时先看 Introduction 和方法总览图，抓住它要解决的不是“怎么改写得更花”，而是“怎么保证改写后还能测”。然后重点看 RePro 方法部分里三阶段流程，尤其 feasibility screening 与 Lean verification 的边界划分：哪些错误在语义筛查阶段处理，哪些错误必须交给形式证明。实验部分建议先看 reliability 对比表，再看 reformulation sensitivity 分析，这两处最能体现方法价值。附录里如果有具体 Lean formalization 示例和失败案例，也值得扫一眼，能帮助判断这套方法的适用边界。预计 25-35 分钟可以抓住主线；若关心数学 benchmark 去污染，可再细看 formalization pipeline 和 retained-rate 相关细节。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"The paper targets a specific failure mode in contamination-resistant math evaluation: rewritten benchmarks are often not trustworthy. Rewriting a problem can reduce memorization effects, but it also introduces two serious risks: the rewritten problem may become ill-posed, and the regenerated reference answer may be wrong. Once either happens, the benchmark no longer measures reasoning reliably. RePro is designed to build rewritten math benchmarks where the retained instances are not only different from the originals, but also well-defined, feasible, and paired with answers backed by formal proof.","importance":"Its impact is mainly on evaluation quality rather than on pretraining algorithms. For foundation-model research, this matters because reliable math evaluation is often used to infer whether gains come from genuine reasoning, data overlap, or benchmark leakage. RePro contributes a higher-precision way to construct decontaminated math test sets. The main contribution is not broader coverage, but stronger guarantees: instead of heuristic validation or LLM-as-a-judge checks, it upgrades answer verification to Lean-checked proof support. That makes it useful as an evaluation asset construction method for contamination analysis, reformulation sensitivity studies, and benchmark reliability work.","technical_approach":"$33","experiments":"$34","reading_guide":"Start with the introduction and the main pipeline figure. recipe、数据配比或模型架构的选择就可能被评测噪声误导。该工作给出一个更细的诊断：不是只报告置信区间，而是检查哪些题目带有残余的模型家族差异，并观察去掉这类题目后近似打平的模型排序是否稳定。","technical_approach":"作者使用 RouterEval 释放的 item-level response matrix，对五个 benchmark 做审计。每个题目和模型形成一个二元矩阵，1 表示答对。模型按名称匹配到 Qwen、Llama、Gemma、Mistral/Mixtral、Phi 等家族，并做 owner cap，避免某个 owner 的大量 checkpoint 主导结果。方法借鉴 psychometrics 中的 differential item functioning，但做了适合 LLM leaderboard 的改造。第一步用不含 family label 的谱方法近似多维 IRT，拟合题目难度和模型能力方向；第二步在控制这些响应方向之后，估计每个题目对不同模型家族的残余偏移，即 family-DIF；第三步在 owner-disjoint folds 中，用一半 owner 选择低 DIF 题目，并冻结权重去评分另一半 owner 的模型。低 DIF 子集还保持 source 和 easiness owner split、K 选择、anchor purification 和权重冻结细节。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"The paper asks whether near-tied LLM leaderboard rankings are robust to changes in benchmark item composition, especially after downweighting items with residual family-dependent behavior.","importance":"The contribution is mainly about evaluation reliability for model development. Pretraining teams often compare recipes, data mixtures, and architecture variants using aggregate benchmark scores with sub-point gaps. If those gaps change sign under reasonable item recomposition, they are weak evidence for a real model improvement. The paper provides a diagnostic for this failure mode: identify items whose residual behavior differs across model families after adjusting for multidimensional ability, then test whether low-DIF scoring preserves close pairwise orderings.","technical_approach":"The analysis uses item-level response matrices from RouterEval for five benchmarks. Models are mapped by frozen name rules into families such as Qwen, Llama, Gemma, Mistral/Mixtral, and Phi, with owner capping and owner-disjoint folds to reduce leakage from many related checkpoints. The method starts with a family-label-free spectral approximation to multidimensional IRT. This estimates response directions and item easiness without using family labels. With those nuisance factors fixed, the method fits residual item-by-family effects; the magnitude of these residuals defines family-DIF for each item. Low-DIF anchors are then selected within source-by-easiness cells, so the recomposed test remains balanced by item source and difficulty. The selected weights are frozen and applied to models from the held-out owner half. Matched random subtests of the same size and blueprint serve as controls for ordinary short-form variability.","experiments":"The audit covers MMLU-Pro, BIG-Bench Hard, MMLU, HellaSwag, and WinoGrande. Full-benchmark rankings and low-DIF recomposed rankings remain highly correlated, with Kendall τ_b between 0.900 and 0.948. The important result is at the close-comparison level: in four of the five benchmarks, 30.9% to 47.1% of cross-family near-tied pairs reverse order under the low-DIF recomposition, above matched-random controls. The analysis does not find a consistent family-level winner or loser, so the result points to composition sensitivity rather than a simple family bias story. The main retention fraction is α=.50, with α=.30 and α=.70 used as sensitivity checks.","reading_guide":"A focused skim should take 25–35 minutes. Start with the audit design to understand the three safeguards: owner-disjoint cross-fitting, source-and-easiness-balanced low-DIF anchors, and matched-random controls. In the results, look at both the rank-correlation table and the near-tie reversal table. The main lesson is that aggregate rankings can remain stable while small pairwise gaps are still composition-sensitive. Appendix C is useful for reproduction details, including owner splitting, dimension selection, anchor purification, and frozen scoring weights.","authors_note":""},"recommendation":{"zh":"这篇会校正你对 leaderboard 小数点后提升的信任度：大盘排序没问题，但近邻名次很脆；做 recipe ablation 时应加重组评测或至少别拿 0.x 分差下结论。","en":"This paper should lower your trust in decimal-point leaderboard gains: broad rankings survive, but near-tied orderings are fragile; for recipe ablations, add recomposed evals or stop overclaiming on 0.x-point gaps."},"metadata":{"institutions":["ETH Zurich","Affiliation:ETH Zurich","Affiliation:Zurich"],"authors":["Qiaoyuan Zheng","Yiqu Yang"],"notable_authors":[],"tags":["evaluation","leaderboard-robustness","DIF","benchmark-recomposition","IRT"],"links":{"arxiv":"https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00482","pdf":"https://arxiv.org/pdf/2609.00482","github":null,"project":null,"source_url":null,"article":null}}},{"id":"2609.00494","title":"Human-Anchored Factuality Evaluation with Strategic Annotation","abstract":"LLM-based factuality judges provide scalable evaluation signals, but their metrics are often systematically biased relative to human judgments. We study human-anchored factuality evaluation under limited annotation budgets, where judge predictions on the full dataset are combined with human labels on a small selectively sampled subset to obtain statistically valid estimates. The efficiency of this approach depends critically on which examples receive human annotation: in factuality evaluation, j","starred":false,"priority":"p2","reading_tier":"skim","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"","category_id":"eval","cover_url":"/covers/2609_00494.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.CL"],"summary":{"core_problem":"这篇工作处理的是一个很实际、也很容易被低估的问题：在有限人工标注预算下，怎样得到“以人为准”的 factuality 指标，而不是被 LLM judge 的系统性偏差带偏。全文的关键点不只是“用少量人工校正自动评测”，而是把 reference-based factuality evaluation 明确写成一个带抽样设计的统计推断问题。作者指出，judge-human gap 在 factuality 上往往不是随机噪声，而是结构化残差：证据不完整、证据过时、证据只提供间接支持、claim 需要释义或常识推断、judge 的核验 rubric 比人工更僵硬。这意味着如果还按均匀抽样，或者只看 judge confidence 去分配人工预算，都会浪费标注，因为真正高残差的样本并不总是低置信度样本。论文要解决的核心，就是如何设计一个面向 factuality failure mode 的 annotation policy，让少量人工标签最大程度修正 judge 偏差，同时保留统计上的无偏性和置信区间有效性。","importance":"对预训练研究本身，它不是训练方法论文，但对基座模型研发链路很有现实意义，尤其是数据筛选、持续评测、RAG/grounded generation 质量监控和 post-train 反馈闭环。它的贡献不在于提出新的 factuality judge，而在于把“judge 只是 proxy，不是 ground truth”这件事做成了可操作的估计框架。更具体地说，这篇工作给出了一种在大规模模型迭代中更省预算的 human-anchored evaluation recipe：先让 judge 覆盖全量，再把人工预算集中投到 judge 最可能和人不一致的 failure region。这个思路对预训练数据质量评估、合成数据验收、reward model 评测、甚至 model-based data filtering 都有启发，因为这些场景都面临同一个问题：自动信号便宜但有偏，人类信号贵但可信。量级上，它不是改变建模范式的工作，但属于评测基础设施层面的扎实增量，适合做内部 eval pipeline 的团队认真看。","technical_approach":"$35","experiments":"实验主要在两个场景上做：一个是内部的 AutoFA，用于虚拟助手回答的自动 factual evaluation；另一个是公开的 RAGTruth，带有人类 span-level hallucination 标注。作者先验证一个重要现象：judge-only FAR 会显著低估 human-annotated FAR，也就是 judge 比人更容易判错或判严。这为后面的 human-anchored correction 提供了动机。\n\n核心结果是标注效率。按文中给出的汇总结果，FSA-guided ASI 相比基线，在聚合不同预算后，平均有效样本量（ESS）提升为：AutoFA 上 40.3%，RAGTruth 上 27.1%。这里 ESS gain 的含义是，在相同人工预算下，等价于你获得了更多“有效人工样本”，或者在达到同样置信区间宽度时需要更少标注。这个指标比只报点估计误差更合适，因为论文目标就是预算受限下的统计效率。\n\n比较对象主要包括：\n1. uniform sampling：最直接但最浪费预算；\n2. confidence-based sampling：按 judge 不确定度选样本；\n3. FSA-derived policies：利用 failure mode 特征做抽样。\n\n实验结论很稳定：confidence-based 方法优于纯均匀抽样，但仍明显不如 FSA，因为 factuality 的 judge-human residual 并不只集中在低置信度区域。作者还展示了若干 failure mode 分析，说明高置信但错判的样本并不少见，尤其在证据不完整、时间失配、间接支持和 rubric 不一致时更明显。\n\n从全文可推断，作者还关注 coverage 和 uncertainty validity，也就是不能为了提效只盯某些角落样本，导致估计偏掉。FSA-guided ASI 的卖点是既保留目标覆盖和统计有效性，又降低方差。若从工程角度看，这比单纯提高 judge accuracy 更实用，因为很多时候 judge 偏差很难彻底消掉，但抽样设计可以立刻改善评测成本结构。","reading_guide":"这篇适合泛读，不必逐行推公式，但有几个部分值得认真看。\n\n第一，Introduction 和 Background 要看仔细。重点不是 FAR 定义本身，而是作者如何把 factuality eval 重写成“judge-assisted, human-anchored inference”问题。这里能帮助区分：什么时候你是在做评测，什么时候你其实在做另一个自动分类器。\n\n第二，方法部分里关于 ASI estimator 和 inclusion probability 的定义值得看一遍。即便不深究证明，也要弄清楚为什么它能在使用 judge 的同时保持 human target 不变。\n\n第三，最值得看的其实是 FSA policy design 那部分。建议重点找 failure taxonomy、feature construction、以及从 residual risk 到 sampling policy 的映射。这里是全文真正的新意，也是最容易迁移到别的 eval pipeline 的部分。\n\n第四，实验部分优先看：judge FAR 与 human FAR 的偏差图、不同 sampling policy 的 ESS/CI 对比、以及 failure mode case study。若时间有限，抓住这三类图表就够了，预计 25 到 35 分钟可以读完主要信息。\n\n如果你做的是预训练或数据研究，阅读时可以顺手想三个迁移问题：能否把 FSA 用到数据过滤验收；能否把 residual-risk 抽样用于 reward model 评测；能否把“高置信错判区域”变成后续数据补采样信号。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"The paper addresses a practical evaluation problem: how to estimate human-defined factual accuracy under a limited annotation budget when LLM judges are available at scale but are systematically biased relative to humans. The key point is not merely “use a few human labels to calibrate an automatic judge.” The paper formulates reference-based factuality evaluation as a budgeted statistical inference problem. In factuality settings, judge-human disagreement is often structured rather than random: incomplete evidence, stale evidence, indirect support, paraphrastic claims, unverifiable content, and rubric mismatch all create persistent residuals. Because of that, uniform human sampling is inefficient, and confidence-based sampling is also insufficient. The core problem is to design an annotation policy that targets regions where judge residuals are likely to be large, while preserving valid human-anchored estimation and uncertainty quantification.","importance":"This is not a pretraining-method paper, but it matters for foundation-model research infrastructure. It is especially relevant to data curation, grounded-generation evaluation, continual model monitoring, synthetic-data acceptance, and post-train feedback loops. The contribution is not a better factuality judge; it is a better way to use judges without mistaking them for ground truth. Concretely, the paper provides a budget-efficient recipe for human-anchored evaluation: run the judge on the full pool, then spend human labels where judge-human mismatch is most likely. That idea transfers well to many pretraining-adjacent settings where automatic signals are cheap but biased, while human signals are expensive but trusted. The contribution is moderate in scientific scope, but strong in operational relevance.","technical_approach":"$36","experiments":"$37","reading_guide":"$38","authors_note":""},"recommendation":{"zh":"只看 failure-space analysis 和采样策略；它比低置信度抽样更贴近 factuality judge 的真实偏差。","en":"Read the failure-space analysis and sampling policy; it targets factuality-judge bias better than plain low-confidence sampling."},"metadata":{"institutions":["Amazon AGI","Affiliation:Amazon AGI"],"authors":["Yu Wang","Craig Erickson","Kevin Small"],"notable_authors":[],"tags":["factuality-evaluation","llm-as-judge","human-annotation","selective-sampling","rag-evaluation"],"links":{"arxiv":"https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00494","pdf":"https://arxiv.org/pdf/2609.00494","github":null,"project":null,"source_url":null,"article":null}}},{"id":"2609.00949","title":"Calibration is the Bottleneck: An Action-Class Diagnostic of Multi-Turn Tool-Calling","abstract":"Multi-turn tool calling is a core evaluation scenario for large language model (LLM) agents. miscalibrat","starred":false,"priority":"p2","reading_tier":"skim","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"","category_id":"eval","cover_url":"/covers/2609_00949.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.CL","cs.AI"],"summary":{"core_problem":"这篇工作要解决的不是“工具调用总体准确率够不够高”，而是多轮 这类基于轨迹状态的 grader 会把一部分“动作类型已经错了”的案例判成通过，导致 aggregate accuracy 高估了模型在多轮决策上的真实能力。核心问题因此是：如何把多轮工具调用中的“决策校准”从“执行正确性”里剥离出来，并给出一个能定位瓶颈的诊断框架。","importance":"对基座模型研究来说，这篇论文的价值不在于提出新的 agent schema 学习或调用格式学习，更像一个带离散决策边界的 calibration 问题；如果模型在 action prior 上偏了，再强的执行器也救不回来。贡献量级偏“诊断与评测方法”，不是范式级方法创新，但对做 agent pretrain / post-train、tool-use 明显偏低。文中给出的代表性例子是 xLAM：总体准确率 71.8%，但在 miss_param 上的 GAR 只有 19.5%，与同量级模型相比低了 64.5 个百分点，而 BFCL 的 state grader 仍会把不少这类轨迹判成 PASS。这说明高总分并不等于动作决策正确。\n\n作者做了 200 个案例的人审审计，报告一致性 κ=0.92，用来验证 action-class 诊断与 grader masking 的结论不是标注噪声。跨 benchmark 复现也显示，问题不局限于 BFCL 的某个数据切片，而是多轮工具调用里普遍存在的动作校准缺陷。\n\n干预实验给出两个有意思的结论。第一，calibration 是可塑的，但不同模型家族对同一个 SRI 扰动反应方向不同：同样的上下文修正，有的模型 Acc 上升，有的反而下降，说明动作边界不是简单靠 prompt 就能统一修复。第二，CRI 的 retry 与 bypass 机制明显分离：retry 往往能把输出类别往 gold action 拉近，但几乎对所有家族都会伤害最终 trajectory accuracy；bypass 不改变 emission，却能保持甚至提升轨迹准确率。这意味着“让模型重新想一遍”未必改善整条轨迹，很多时候系统层面的错误隔离比重新采样更有效。对 agent serving 和 tool calling: models may achieve strong trajectory-level accuracy while choosing the wrong action type for the dialogue state. The authors argue that aggregate benchmark scores conflate two different errors: action-class miscalibration and downstream execution failure. In a given turn, the model should decide among calling a tool, asking for missing information, refusing an unavailable request, or confirming. Existing state-based graders can mark a trajectory as correct even when that decision was wrong. The core problem is therefore how to separate decision calibration from execution correctness in multi-turn tool-use evaluation.","importance":"Its main value is diagnostic rather than algorithmic. For pretraining and post-training researchers, the paper makes a useful point: tool use is not only about learning function syntax or argument formatting, but also about learning calibrated action selection under context. Constructs CAP-Correctness (8.8k examples) and CAP-Statements (11k examples) datasets for evaluation and training.","experiments":"On the CAP-Correctness dataset, CAP outperforms common lexical overlap, embedding-based, and learned semantic metrics in preserving the intended ordering of correctness categories (monotonicity), demonstrating stronger semantic ranking alignment and fewer monotonicity violations.","reading_guide":"Read the Introduction to understand the limitations of existing metrics, and focus on the 8-class semantic correctness taxonomy in Figure 2. The Method section detailing how CAP utilizes NLI for evaluation is also recommended.","authors_note":""},"recommendation":{"zh":"提出了Open QA的细粒度正确性分类和基于NLI的评估指标，对做长文本/开放域QA评测的同学有直接帮助，建议关注其分类体系。","en":"Proposes a fine-grained correctness taxonomy and NLI-based metric for Open QA; directly helpful for those working on long-text/open-domain QA evaluation, pay attention to the taxonomy."},"metadata":{"institutions":["ETH Zurich","Sofia University","University of Zurich","Mohamed bin Zayed University of Artificial Intelligence","Sofia University “St. Kliment Ohridski”"],"authors":["Elitsa Yotkova","Violeta Kastreva","Petar Velkov","Hristo Boyanov","Dimitar Dimitrov","Ivan Koychev","Preslav Nakov"],"notable_authors":["Preslav Nakov"],"tags":["Open QA","Evaluation Metric","Semantic Correctness","NLI","Hallucination"],"links":{"arxiv":"https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01369","pdf":"https://arxiv.org/pdf/2609.01369","github":null,"project":null,"source_url":null,"article":null}}},{"id":"2609.00088","title":"Commit-first LLM judging inherits the judge's own errors","abstract":"LLM judges, models that score another system's output, can be gamed by the systems they score. Recent work identifies one defence that works: the judge solves the task itself first and commits to that answer, then accepts a candidate only if the two match. We call this commit-first judging, and ask whether shipped software implements it, and what it costs. We audit the default judge configurations of eight widely used evaluation frameworks. Of the 24 configurations in scope, none implement it. N","starred":false,"priority":"p2","reading_tier":"skim","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"","category_id":"eval","cover_url":"/covers/2609_00088.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.SE","cs.AI","cs.CL"],"summary":{"core_problem":"这篇工作讨论的不是“LLM judge 会不会被攻击”这个已知问题，而是一个更实际的问题：业界常说的 commit-first judging 作为防御手段，真实部署里到底有没有被采用；即便采用，它是否真的稳健。全文给出的核心结论很明确：commit-first 确实能切断“候选答案污染 judge 推理”的路径，但它并没有消除优化压力，只是把优化目标从“迎合 judge 的打分偏好”改成了“复现 judge 自己先验提交的答案”。因此，一旦 judge 在该任务上本身会犯错，优化过程就会系统性继承并放大这种错误，最后得到高分但错误的候选。","importance":"对预训练研究的直接启发不在 judge 工程本身，而在“训练信号质量”这个更底层的问题。很多 post-train、数据合成、reward modeling、self-improvement 流程都把 LLM evaluator 当成近似真值。这里的结果说明：只要评价器不是独立且足够强，它就会成为被优化系统的一部分，进而把自身偏差写进训练信号。commit-first 不是无条件更好的 judge，而是把上限绑定到 judge-task competence 上。这个判断对 RL from AI feedback、合成偏好数据、自动 curriculum、self-play 选择机制都很关键，因为这些流程本质上都在反复放大 evaluator 的误差结构。","technical_approach":"方法上分三层。第一层是审计。作者系统检查了 8 个常用评测框架、9 个包、27 个默认 judge 配置，判定标准很严格：judge 必须在看到候选前先给出可解析的自有答案，最终接受规则也必须是与该已提交答案做匹配，而不是看完候选后再做整体打分。按这个标准，默认配置里没有真正实现 commit-first 的；有一部分只实现了文献里已知无效的变体，比如让 judge 先 chain-of-thought 但候选仍然在上下文里，或者最后仍由 holistic score 决定。\n\n第二层是受控优化实验。作者用标准 best-of-N selection，在没有 ground truth 的条件下，仅凭 judge 分数做选择，观察优化是否会把系统推向“高分低质”。这里的关键不是复杂攻击，而是最普通的选择压力：只要反复采样并保留 judge 更喜欢的候选，就足以暴露 judge 的可游戏性。作者先在一个真实 coding 任务上复现：对 shipped judge 配置，best-of-N 会显著抬高 judge score 而不提升真实正确率；换成 commit-first 后，这种效应在该任务上消失。\n\n第三层是失败模式分析。作者专门找了 judge 自己解不出来的任务，先让 judge 独立作答，再用隐藏检查项评估其 committed answer 的真实正确性。结果显示，commit-first 在这种场景下会把优化过程锁定到 judge 的错误答案附近。也就是说，候选系统不再学会“骗 judge”，而是学会“像 judge 一样错”。作者进一步提出一个便宜的前置探针：先测 judge 在该任务上的 direct-solve competence，再决定能否信任 commit-first。这个 probe 的价值在于，它把问题从抽象的“judge 是否可靠”落到具体的“这个 judge 在这个 task 上能否独立解题”。","experiments":"最重要的数字有三类。第一类来自已有文献复现与定位：当候选先暴露给 judge，再要求其逐步推理时，错误答案的 false positive rate 仍高达 0.719；而真正的 commit-first 方案可降到 0.012。作者用这个差距作为审计标准的依据。第二类是框架审计：8 个常用框架、27 个默认配置中，没有一个满足 commit-first 的完整条件；其中 9 个实现的是已知无效变体。第三类是优化实验：在真实 coding 任务上，plain best-of-N selection 能稳定 game shipped judge，且在两个随机种子下都复现；commit-first 在 judge 能解题的任务上能消除这一现象，但在 judge 不能解题的任务上反而让结果更差，并且其中一个种子里，最终群体正确率几乎等于 judge committed answer 的正确率，说明优化过程紧贴 judge 的能力上限。\n\n全文还强调一个很有价值的实验观察：gaming 的复现性比 repair 更强。也就是，错误被稳定放大比修复更容易稳定出现。这对任何依赖自动 evaluator 的训练闭环都不是好消息。另一个细节是，judge competence 不是简单的模型规模函数，而是 judge 与 task 的联合属性；大模型不自动意味着 commit-first 可用。","reading_guide":"泛读时先抓三处。第一是引言和贡献列表，里面已经把论文最重要的判断讲清楚：commit-first 不是终点，它只是把攻击面转移到 judge 自身误差。第二是框架审计部分，重点看作者如何定义“真正的 commit-first”，以及哪些常见实现其实只是看起来像。这里对做 evaluator pipeline 很有参考价值。第三是受控优化实验和 cheap probe 部分，理解作者如何在没有 ground truth 参与选择的前提下，仅靠 best-of-N 暴露 judge 的脆弱性，以及为什么 direct-solve probe 能预测 commit-first 何时有用、何时反噬。\n\n如果时间有限，建议优先看：Introduction、对 27 个配置的判定标准、两个任务上的 best-of-N 实验、以及关于 reachability 和 hidden checks 的测量定义。预计 20–30 分钟能抓住主线。值得深挖的点有两个：一是 commit-first 的失败是否能用多样化 judge ensemble 缓解；二是 direct-solve competence 能否转化成训练前的自动 gating 机制，用在 RL/数据合成流水线上。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"The paper is not asking whether LLM judges can be gamed—that is already established. It asks a more operational question: is commit-first judging actually implemented in widely used evaluation software, and if so, what failure mode remains after adopting it? The main answer is precise. Commit-first removes contamination from seeing the candidate too early, but it does not remove optimization pressure. It shifts the target of optimization from “please the judge” to “match the judge’s own committed answer.” When the judge is wrong on the task, optimization inherits that error and converges toward it.","importance":"Its relevance to foundation-model research is through training-signal quality. Many post-training loops—AI feedback, synthetic preference generation, self-improvement, automatic selection, curriculum construction—treat an LLM evaluator as a proxy for truth. This paper shows that the proxy can become part of the optimized system. Commit-first is therefore not a universally safer judge; it is only as good as the judge’s task competence. A label thus reveals little about the engineering work a benchmark demands. We introduce the Spread--Novelty--Centrality (SNC) profile, a three-axis characterization of the demands of repository-level coding tasks, grounded in empirical software engineering research. agent trajectories。主要发现有三点：第一，同一类别标签下的 benchmark 在 SNC 至少两个轴上也会显著分离，类别名不能代表任务需求；第二，agent patch 的 verbosity 与 benchmark 构造方式相关，提示少时更容易 over-shoot，gold patch 膨胀时可能 under-shoot，而探索广度在 benchmark 间差异不明显；第三，resolved runs 通常集中在低 SNC 区间，这一趋势在模型家族和规模间较稳定，但行为模式有家族差异，Claude 更接近 gold patch scope，且随规模增大更趋近 scope parity，Qwen 更常通过 over-producing 解决，under-editing 对两个家族都常对应失败。","reading_guide":"建议泛读，预计 fixing or feature implementation.","importance":"This is primarily an evaluation paper, not a pretraining paper. Its relevance comes from benchmark interpretation and data curation for coding-agent post-training. The main lesson is that a benchmark label should not be treated as a capability label. Two datasets both called feature implementation can require very different amounts of code spread, new code creation, and architectural involvement, which changes both evaluation conclusions and the training signal used for SFT or RL.","technical_approach":"The authors introduce the Spread–Novelty–Centrality profile. Spread measures how distributed the reference patch is across the repository. Novelty measures how much the solution introduces new code rather than modifying or deleting existing code. Centrality measures how architecturally important the touched code is. These metrics are computed from gold patches to characterize task demand. The paper then goes beyond gold patches by analyzing agent trajectories and generated patches. It measures behaviors such as patch verbosity relative to the gold patch and exploration breadth relative to the actual edit scope. This separates the required engineering work from the way an agent attempts the task.","experiments":"The study covers five agentic SE benchmarks with 2,487 instances, including SWE-bench Verified and SWE-Gym Lite. It evaluates two model families at three scale points each: Claude Haiku 4.5, Sonnet 4.6, and Opus 4.6 under Claude Code; Qwen 3.5 9B, 27B, and 397B-A17B under Qwen Code. All runs use the Harbor evaluation harness, totaling 30 runs and 14,922 agent trajectories. The main findings are: benchmark labels are poor proxies for task demand, with every benchmark pair separated on at least two SNC axes; patch verbosity reflects benchmark construction and prompting, while exploration breadth is less distinguishable across benchmarks; resolved runs concentrate in low-SNC bins across families and scales; behavioral signatures differ by family, with Claude tending toward gold-patch scope parity and Qwen more often resolving through over-production. Under-editing is a common failure signal for both families.","reading_guide":"Skim in about 25–35 minutes.
