Implementation details are only necessary if reproducing the harness or building a new coding-agent benchmark. The most useful takeaway for LLM researchers is to use SNC-like profiles as a data and evaluation diagnostic for coding tasks, especially when analyzing weak benchmark transfer or unstable post-training mixtures.","authors_note":""},"recommendation":{"zh":"读 SNC 定义和主要结果表；它能校准 SWE benchmark 的难度解释，避免把数据构造差异误读成模型能力差异。","en":"Read the SNC definitions and main result table; it helps separate SWE benchmark curation effects from genuine model capability differences."},"metadata":{"institutions":["Queen’s University","Radin Shayanfar, Keheliya Gallaba, Ahmed E. Despite rapid progress, their reliability properties remain largely uncharacterized. We present the first systematic reliability evaluation of hallucination and bias in dLVLMs, benchmarking six diffusion models against competitive AR baselines across four dimensions. Our ","starred":false,"priority":"p2","reading_tier":"skim","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"","category_id":"eval","cover_url":"/covers/2609_01318.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.CV","cs.CL"],"summary":{"core_problem":"这篇工作要回答的不是“diffusion LVLM 能不能做题”，而是“当生成范式从自回归改成离散扩散后，模型的可靠性失效模式会不会跟着变”。作者把问题拆成四类：二元视觉问答里的 object hallucination、开放式描述里的 caption hallucination、人口统计偏置，以及多选题里的 selection bias。关键点在于，他们不是把 dLVLM 当成 AR 模型的一个速度变体，而是把它视为一种会改变生成动力学的建模范式，因此需要单独刻画其偏差结构、语言退化模式和推断过程信号。","importance":"对预训练和建模范式研究的意义主要有两层。第一，它补上了 diffusion LM / dLVLM 研究里长期缺的一块：大家常报告任务分数和采样速度，但很少系统分析 reliability profile。这里给出的结论很明确，dLVLM 不是简单继承 AR 的失效模式，而是出现了方向相反或更严重的新偏差，比如 yes-bias 反转、极强的长度偏置、不同家族间相反极性的 gender bias。第二，它提示“生成目标改变了误差形态”，这对后续做 diffusion LM 的训练目标、解码策略、post-training 校准都很重要。尤其是文中把 hallucination 与 linguistic quality 分开看，这一点对很多只看最终文本质量的工作是个提醒：语言流畅不等于 grounded，反过来也成立。","technical_approach":"$3f","experiments":"实验覆盖面比较完整，但这篇工作的价值更多在模式发现而不是单一数字。模型侧评测了 6 个 dLVLM，对照 7 个 AR LVLM；任务侧覆盖 POPE、CHAIR、FairFace 和长度控制 MCQA。文中最重要的结果有四个。\n\n第一，在 POPE 上，dLVLM 整体没有表现出 AR 模型常见的高 Yes% 倾向，反而经常走向相反方向。也就是说，AR 常见的问题是“看到不确定内容也容易答有”，而 dLVLM 更像“在不确定时更容易答没有”。这会改变 precision / recall 的平衡：某些 diffusion 模型 precision 可能不差，但 recall 会因为保守回答而受损。\n\n第二，在 CHAIR 上，dLVLM 的 hallucination rate 与强 AR baseline 大体可比，但生成文本的语言质量更弱。作者强调这两者是可分离的失败维度。一个模型可以少编造对象，但句子组织、细节表达、自然度仍然差；这对只看 groundedness 指标的评测是不够的。\n\n第三，在 FairFace 上，作者观察到 underrepresented racial groups 上接近零准确率的极端现象，而且不同 diffusion 家族的 gender bias 方向相反。这个结果说明不能把“diffusion 模型的偏置”当成单一现象，它高度依赖具体实现。\n\n第四，在长度控制 MCQA 上，dLVLM 的长度偏置明显强于 AR。更重要的是，作者通过去噪过程分析发现，这种偏置在第一步就已经出现。这个发现很像训练动态里的 early commitment 问题：如果模型在高噪声阶段就把长度当成强线索，后续迭代很难纠正。\n\n从消融和附加分析看，作者还比较了不同 padding 条件、不同模型家族，以及置信度轨迹与 hallucination 的相关性。虽然摘要里没有给出所有具体数值，但全文的重点不是追求某个 benchmark 上的百分点差距，而是证明这些现象在多模型、多任务上都稳定存在。","reading_guide":"泛读建议是先看 Introduction 和实验总表，抓住四个结论：yes-bias 反转、hallucination 与语言质量分离、人口统计偏置更尖锐、长度偏置在首步去噪就出现。然后重点看 object hallucination 和 selection bias 两节，因为这两部分最能体现 diffusion 范式与 AR 的结构差异。\n\n如果时间有限，优先看三类图表：一类是 POPE 上的 Yes% 与 F1/Recall 对照，能直观看到回答倾向如何改变；一类是 MCQA 中不同选项长度下的选择分布，以及 first denoising step 的分析图；一类是 token confidence / commit step 与 hallucination 的关系图。这些图比最终平均分更有研究价值。\n\n预计阅读时间 25–35 分钟。若做 diffusion LM 或离散去噪生成，建议额外细看机制分析部分，思考三个问题：训练目标是否诱导了过早提交；解码时的 unmask 策略能否抑制长度偏置；置信度轨迹能否作为 post-training 或 inference-time 校准信号。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"The paper asks a reliability question rather than a capability question: once generation moves from left-to-right autoregression to discrete diffusion denoising, do the failure modes of vision-language models change in a systematic way? The study focuses on four axes: object hallucination in binary visual queries, open-ended hallucination in captioning, demographic bias, and selection bias in multiple-choice settings. The important framing is that diffusion LVLMs are treated as a different generative paradigm, not merely a faster decoder, so their reliability profile needs to be characterized on its own terms.","importance":"Its value for pretraining and modeling research is twofold. First, it fills a gap in diffusion LM / dLVLM work: many papers report benchmark accuracy and decoding efficiency, but very few examine reliability in a structured way. The findings show that diffusion models do not simply inherit AR failure modes. Some biases flip direction, and some become more severe, such as strong length bias and family-dependent gender bias with opposite polarity. Second, the paper makes a useful point for objective design: changing the generative objective changes the shape of errors. The separation between hallucination and linguistic quality is especially relevant. A model can be less hallucinatory yet still produce weaker language, and the reverse can also happen.","technical_approach":"$40","experiments":"$41","reading_guide":"For a skim-level read, start with the introduction and the main result tables to lock in the four takeaways: reversed yes-bias, separation between hallucination and language quality, sharper demographic failures, and first-step emergence of length bias. Atabuzzaman","Chris Thomas"],"notable_authors":[],"tags":["diffusion-VLM","reliability","hallucination","bias","length-prior"],"links":{"arxiv":"https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01318","pdf":"https://arxiv.org/pdf/2609.01318","github":null,"project":null,"source_url":null,"article":null}}},{"id":"2609.01603","title":"Efficient SWE Agent Benchmarking via Trajectory-Aware Evaluation","abstract":"Evaluating software engineering agents on realistic benchmarks is costly, since each task may require multi-step code exploration, modification, and test execution. Existing efficient evaluation methods select representative subsets to estimate full-benchmark performance, but are largely result-only: they fit historical pass/fail response matrices or static task semantics, discarding how agents solve problems. We propose PTA-IRT, a Privileged Trajectory-Aware Item Response Theory framework that ","starred":false,"priority":"p2","reading_tier":"skim","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"","category_id":"eval","cover_url":"/covers/2609_01603.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.SE","cs.AI","cs.CL"],"summary":{"core_problem":"这篇工作解决的是一个评测效率问题：SWE agent 的完整 benchmark 运行太贵，能不能只跑一个小子集，就较准确地恢复模型在全量 benchmark 上的分数和相对排名。已有 IRT 方法大多只看最终 pass/fail，把长轨迹的探索、编辑、测试、回退这些过程信息全部压成一个二值结果。作者认为这会丢掉大量测量信号，尤其是在 agent 任务里，失败方式和成功路径本身就携带难度与区分度信息。","importance":"它对预训练本身不是直接贡献，但对大模型研究流程有现实意义，尤其是代码 agent 和长程工具使用系统的评测成本控制。更具体地说，这篇工作提醒了一点：当任务是多步交互时，结果标签往往过于粗糙，过程轨迹可以作为更强的“特权信息”来帮助建模 item difficulty 和 model ability。这个思路对 agent eval 有用，对 post-train 数据筛选、trajectory distillation、甚至训练时的 curriculum 设计也有启发。","technical_approach":"方法核心是 PTA-IRT，完整名字是 Privileged Trajectory-Aware Item Response Theory。它在经典 IRT 框架上加了两层东西：一层是 trajectory-aware item calibration，另一层是用 LUPI 把轨迹信息迁移到测试时只依赖少量结果观测的 ability estimator。\n\n具体来说，作者先收集历史 agent 在 benchmark 上的执行轨迹。轨迹不是直接喂原始日志，而是先转成结构化语义摘要，里面包含代码上下文探索、编辑尝试、工具调用、验证路径等过程特征。然后基于这些摘要学习 trajectory-aware 的 4PL IRT item 参数。4PL 比常见 2PL/3PL 更灵活，除了 difficulty 和 discrimination，还能建模 guessing / upper asymptote 一类效应，更适合 agent 任务里非对称的成功概率曲线。\n\n在 subset selection 阶段，作者不是只按 outcome matrix 选题，而是利用 trajectory-aware Fisher information 做难度分层采样，挑出对能力估计最有信息量的一小组 calibration tasks。直觉上，这样选出来的题不只是“结果上有区分度”，而是“从过程上也能看出不同 agent 在哪里卡住”。\n\n在 ability estimation 阶段，测试时的新 agent 不需要提供历史轨迹全集，只需在选出的 calibration subset 上运行。模型再通过 Learning Using Privileged Information 的方式，把训练阶段学到的轨迹侧知识蒸馏进一个只依赖有限观测结果的估计器，进而预测该 agent 在全 benchmark 上的总分和排名。\n\n方法设计里一个值得注意的点是协议设定比较现实：轨迹只在离线训练阶段可用，在线评测时仍然只要求跑少量题。这避免了“为了省评测成本却还要收集大量轨迹”的自相矛盾。","experiments":"实验在四个 SWE benchmark 上进行，并与已有 IRT 基线比较。摘要和正文给出的主结论比较一致：在低 calibration budget 下，PTA-IRT 在 score recovery 和 ranking recovery 上都优于 outcome-only 的 IRT 方法。也就是说，当只能跑很少一部分题时，利用历史轨迹做 item calibration 的收益最大。\n\n作者还做了几类关键对照。第一，去掉 trajectory 信息，只保留 pass/fail 或静态任务语义，恢复效果会下降，说明提升不是来自更复杂的模型形式，而是来自过程信号本身。第二，把轨迹摘要打乱或污染后，性能也会变差，说明模型确实在利用有意义的求解路径信息，而不是仅仅吃到更长文本。第三，作者比较了 issue text、submitted patch 与结构化 trajectory summary 的信息量，结论是结构化轨迹摘要更能提供额外测量信号。\n\n从研究者角度，最重要的实验信息不是某个绝对百分点，而是收益集中在 low-budget regime。这很符合实际：如果预算充足，直接全量评测就行；只有在预算紧张时，subset-to-full 的估计质量才真正重要。PTA-IRT 的价值也主要体现在这个区间。","reading_guide":"泛读时先看问题设定和协议图，确认它解决的是 budgeted subset-to-full evaluation，而不是提升 agent 本身。然后看方法图，抓住三步：轨迹摘要、trajectory-aware 4PL calibration、LUPI ability estimation。最后看 low-budget 区间的主结果图和 ablation，判断收益是否稳定。\n\n如果只花 20–30 分钟，建议重点看：方法总览图、主表里不同 budget 下的 score/rank recovery、以及去掉 trajectory summary 的消融。对 IRT 不熟的话，只需要理解 difficulty / discrimination / ability 这几个量如何被轨迹信息修正，不必陷入公式细节。\n\n这篇更适合做 agent eval 或 benchmark 设计的人读。对纯 pretrain 研究员，阅读重点在“过程信号比结果标签更有测量价值”这个方法论启发。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"The paper tackles an evaluation-efficiency problem for software engineering agents: full-benchmark runs are expensive, so can we execute a new agent on only a small subset of tasks and still recover its full-benchmark score and ranking reliably? Existing IRT-based approaches mostly reduce each task to a final pass/fail outcome. The authors argue that this is too lossy for agentic settings, where long trajectories contain useful measurement signals about exploration, edits, tool use, and failure modes.","importance":"This is not a direct pretraining contribution, but it matters for the research loop around large models, especially code agents and long-horizon tool-using systems. The main methodological lesson is that for multi-step tasks, outcome labels are often too coarse, and process trajectories can serve as privileged information for estimating item difficulty and model ability. That idea is relevant beyond benchmarking: it may also inform post-training data selection, trajectory distillation, and curriculum design.","technical_approach":"$42","experiments":"$43","reading_guide":"$44","authors_note":""},"recommendation":{"zh":"将 Agent 的执行轨迹（过程信号）引入 IRT 评估模型，比单纯看 pass/fail 更科学，对设计低成本、高保真的 Agent 评测集很有参考价值。","en":"Incorporating Agent execution trajectories (process signals) into IRT evaluation models is more rigorous than relying solely on pass/fail outcomes; highly valuable for designing low-cost, high-fidelity Agent evaluation sets."},"metadata":{"institutions":["Sun Yat-sen University","Zhejiang University"],"authors":["Kefeng Duan","Dewu Zheng","Yanlin Wang","Xiwen Wang","Ensheng Shi","Xilin Liu","Yuchi Ma","Jiachi Chen","Mingwei Liu","Zibin Zheng"],"notable_authors":[],"tags":["SWE Agent","Evaluation","Item Response Theory","Trajectory-Aware"],"links":{"arxiv":"https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01603","pdf":"https://arxiv.org/pdf/2609.01603","github":null,"project":null,"source_url":null,"article":null}}},{"id":"2609.00505","title":"ViTAL-X: Video-Text Alignment with Cross-Modal Temporal Edits","abstract":"Video-text models adapted from image-text architectures (e.g., CLIP) frequently exhibit temporal blindness, the inability to perceive fundamental cues like order, direction, and motion dynamics. Standard datasets mask this limitation by enabling models to exploit static spatial shortcuts. To systematically evaluate this, we introduce XTE-Bench, a diagnostic probe revealing that even large-scale video-language models struggle with basic temporal reasoning, indicating that parameter scaling alone ","starred":false,"priority":"p2","reading_tier":"skim","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"","category_id":"eval","cover_url":"/covers/2609_00505.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.CV"],"summary":{"core_problem":"这篇工作聚焦视频-文本模型里的一个结构性缺陷：temporal blindness。很多视频文本模型是从图文模型改出来的，视觉端先逐帧提特征，再用平均池化或其他近似置换不变的聚合方式得到视频表示。这样做保住了图像语义，但会把“先发生 A 再发生 B”和“先 B 后 A”压成几乎相同的 embedding。作者要解决的问题不是一般的视频理解，而是如何在不重做大规模视频预训练的前提下，给现有图文基础模型注入真正的时间敏感性，让模型学会顺序、方向、因果这类最基础的时间线索。","importance":"对基座研究的意义主要有两层。第一，它指出从 image-text foundation model 迁移到 video-text 时，很多失败不是参数不够，而是训练信号和表示结构没有把时间当成一等公民。这个判断对统一多模态预训练很重要，因为“把视频当多张图”在工程上很方便，但会系统性丢掉时序信息。第二，作者提出的 XTE 属于一种可程序化生成困难负样本的自监督数据机制：通过同步改写视频时间轴和文本描述，构造只在时间结构上不同的对比样本。这种思路对视频预训练之外的多模态对齐也有启发，尤其适合那些缺少人工标注、但可以做结构化 counterfactual 编辑的场景。论文更像是在补训练目标和数据构造，而不是发明全新 backbone。","technical_approach":"方法核心是 XTE，Cross-Modal Temporal Edits。作者从原始视频-文本对 (V, y) 出发，定义一组时间编辑算子 A_k 及其对应的文本改写函数 g_k。对视频侧，编辑包括片段反转、事件重排、时间遮蔽等；对文本侧，则同步加入 before、after、reversed 等时间修饰，使编辑后的视频和文本仍然语义一致，但与原样本形成严格配对的时间反事实。关键设计点在于：原视频与编辑视频共享几乎相同的帧内容，空间信息不变，唯一被系统操控的是时间结构。这样随机负样本里常见的背景、主体、场景 shortcut 被压掉，模型必须依赖时序线索完成对比学习。\n\n在模型侧，ViTAL-X 并没有重训一个大视频模型，而是在冻结的图文 backbone 上做轻量时序适配。论文使用 OpenCLIP 或 SigLIP-2 作为基础编码器，再叠加一个浅层 spatiotemporal transformer 来建模跨帧依赖，并配合 targeted LoRA 微调。这个设计的取舍很明确：保留大规模图文预训练带来的静态语义能力，只在必要位置补时间建模，避免全量更新导致遗忘，也控制训练成本。\n\n训练目标是针对 XTE 构造的时间困难负样本做对比学习。与普通 video-text contrastive learning 相比，区别不在于 loss 形式本身，而在于负样本分布被重新设计：负样本不再是完全不同的视频，而是同一视频的时间反事实版本。这样模型如果继续依赖静态外观特征，将无法区分正负样本。\n\n论文还提出 XTE-Bench 作为诊断协议，用来专门测顺序、方向、因果等时间能力，而不是让模型在常规 benchmark 上靠静态空间线索拿分。这个评测资产和方法是配套的：没有这种 probe，很难证明模型真的学到了时间，而不是只在训练集上适配了某些模板。","experiments":"从提供的全文内容看，实验结论主要集中在三点。\n\n第一，XTE-Bench 暴露出当前大规模视频语言模型在基础时间推理上仍然薄弱，说明单纯扩大参数规模并不能自动解决 temporal blindness。作者把这作为方法动机，而不是把时间问题当成数据量不足的自然副产品。\n\n第二，ViTAL-X 在效率上很激进：模型规模约 0.4B，只训练 1M clips，却能超过一些 7B 级模型，以及训练数据量高出约 600 倍的基线。这里最值得注意的不是绝对数字，而是“针对性时间监督”比“继续堆规模和数据”更有效地修补这类结构性缺陷。对预训练研究者来说，这是一种典型信号：当任务失败来自目标函数和负样本设计错误时，scaling 的边际收益会很差。\n\n第三，作者声称方法不仅在自建的 XTE-Bench 上有效，也能泛化到多个独立视频理解 benchmark。这一点如果在主表中成立，说明 XTE 没有把模型过拟合到某一种时间编辑模板，而是确实增强了时序表征。\n\n由于给出的正文片段没有完整表格和具体分数，精读时需要重点核对几类消融：不同 temporal edit 算子的贡献，是否所有编辑都有效；冻结 backbone 与 LoRA/浅层时序模块各自带来的增益；以及在保留静态图像语义能力方面是否存在明显退化。若这些消融充分，论文的说服力会明显更强。","reading_guide":"这篇适合按“问题是否来自结构，方法是否真的隔离了时间变量”来读。\n\n先看 Introduction 对 temporal blindness 的定义，以及为什么平均池化式视频适配会把顺序信息抹掉。然后直接看 Method 里 XTE 的构造，重点不是编辑种类有多少，而是视频编辑与文本改写是否严格同步、是否真的只改变时间结构。若这一点成立，XTE 的训练信号就比普通随机负样本干净得多。\n\n接着看 ViTAL-X 架构部分，关注浅层 spatiotemporal transformer 放在什么位置、LoRA 插在哪些模块、为什么要冻结 backbone 而不是全量微调。最后看实验时，优先读 XTE-Bench 的设计和主结果表，再看泛化 benchmark 与消融。若时间有限，预计 20–30 分钟可以抓住主线；若要判断它对统一多模态预训练是否有借鉴意义，需要把数据构造和负样本设计部分读细一些。\n\n值得深挖的点有三个。第一，XTE 生成的文本改写是否足够自然，还是会引入模板化语言线索；第二，时间编辑是否覆盖了真实视频理解中的长程依赖，而不只是局部顺序判断；第三，这种“结构化 counterfactual 负样本”能否迁移到音频、语音或交错多模态序列建模里。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"The paper targets a structural weakness in video-text models: temporal blindness. Many video-language systems are adapted from image-text encoders by extracting frame features and then aggregating them with nearly permutation-invariant operations such as average pooling. That preserves static semantics but collapses videos with different event order into almost identical embeddings. The problem addressed here is not generic video understanding. It is how to inject genuine temporal sensitivity, including order, direction, and causality, into existing image-text foundation models without rebuilding large-scale video pretraining from scratch.","importance":"Its relevance to foundation-model research is twofold. First, it argues that the failure of image-to-video transfer is often not a pure scaling issue. The representation structure and the training signal simply do not treat time as a first-class variable. That matters for unified multimodal pretraining, because treating video as a bag of frames is convenient but systematically discards chronology. Second, XTE is a useful example of programmable hard-negative generation: synchronized edits on the video timeline and the caption create counterfactual pairs that differ only in temporal structure. That idea extends beyond video. Any multimodal setting where structured counterfactual edits are possible but manual labels are scarce could benefit from a similar data-construction strategy. The paper is therefore more about fixing objectives and data than inventing a radically new backbone.","technical_approach":"$45","experiments":"$46","reading_guide":"$47","authors_note":""},"recommendation":{"zh":"这篇不在文本 pretrain 主线，但它把“scale 能否自动补齐时序能力”这个 prior 打得很具体；读 benchmark 设计和 hard negative 构造就够了。","en":"Not core text pretraining, but it gives a concrete counterexample to the prior that scale will fix temporal reasoning; read the benchmark design and hard-negative construction."},"metadata":{"institutions":["University of Illinois Urbana-Champaign","Adobe Research","Affiliation:University of Illinois Urbana-Champaign","Affiliation:Adobe Research"],"authors":["Sethuraman T V","Savya Khosla","Onkar Kishor Susladkar","Aditi Tiwari","Seoung Wug Oh","Kushal Kafle","Joon-Young Lee","Derek Hoiem","Simon Jenni"],"notable_authors":["Joon-Young Lee","Derek Hoiem","Simon Jenni"],"tags":["video-language","temporal-reasoning","benchmark","self-supervision","hard-negatives"],"links":{"arxiv":"https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00505","pdf":"https://arxiv.org/pdf/2609.00505","github":null,"project":null,"source_url":null,"article":null}}},{"id":"2609.01515","title":"TempCloze: Can Video-LLMs Identify the Missing Middle?","abstract":"Temporal reasoning benchmarks for Video-LLMs are often mediated by language, leaving room for linguistic shortcuts from option wording, answer correlations, or language priors. To reduce such shortcuts, we introduce TempCloze, a video cloze benchmark for evaluating visual temporal reasoning in Video-LLMs. Given the beginning and ending clips of a video, models must identify the true missing middle from four candidates. TempCloze contains 1,521 carefully filtered videos from seven sources, mainly","starred":false,"priority":"p2","reading_tier":"skim","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"","category_id":"eval","cover_url":"/covers/2609_01515.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.CV","cs.AI"],"summary":{"core_problem":"现有的视频大语言模型（Video-LLMs）时序推理评测高度依赖语言，容易让模型通过选项措辞、答案相关性或语言先验等“捷径”作弊，无法真实反映模型对视觉时序的理解能力。","importance":"构建了一个纯视觉驱动的时序推理 Benchmark，剥离了语言捷径，暴露了当前 Video-LLMs 在时序对齐（Temporal Alignment）上的核心短板，对后续多模态预训练中如何强化时序建模有直接指导意义。","technical_approach":"提出 TempCloze 视频完形填空任务。给定视频的开头和结尾片段，要求模型从四个候选片段中选出正确的中间片段。为了防止模型仅靠视觉匹配作弊，干扰项均来自同一视频源（共享场景和物体），并沿三个维度构造：语义（Semantic，发生什么事件）、对齐（Alignment，何时发生，如平移或扩展时间跨度）、进展（Progression，如何发展，如倒放或乱序）。","experiments":"评测了 31 个 Video-LLMs。结果显示，模型在识别事件内容（语义）和进展方向上表现尚可，但在时序对齐（Alignment）上普遍挣扎，尤其是面对“扩展时间跨度”的干扰项时错误率极高。此外，模型更依赖开头而非结尾的上下文，且增加帧密度反而可能稀释关键信息。","reading_guide":"重点关注 Error Pattern Analysis 部分，了解当前模型在处理时序信息时的具体失败模式（如对齐失败主导因素）。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"Current temporal reasoning benchmarks for Video-LLMs are heavily mediated by language, allowing models to exploit linguistic shortcuts (e.g., option wording, language priors) rather than demonstrating true visual temporal understanding.","importance":"Introduces a visually driven temporal reasoning benchmark that strips away linguistic shortcuts, exposing a critical bottleneck in current Video-LLMs regarding temporal alignment. This provides clear direction for improving temporal modeling in multimodal pretraining.","technical_approach":"Proposes TempCloze, a video cloze task where models must identify the correct missing middle clip given the beginning and ending clips. To prevent visual matching shortcuts, distractors are constructed from the same source video (sharing scenes/objects) along three dimensions: Semantic (what happens), Alignment (when it happens, via shifting/expanding spans), and Progression (how it unfolds, via reversing/reordering).","experiments":"Evaluated 31 Video-LLMs. Results reveal that while models can identify event content and progression, they struggle significantly with temporal alignment, particularly failing against 'expanded span' distractors. Existing agentic benchmarks report end-to-end success but confound this state-tracking difficulty with instruction interpretation, give no control group that isolates it, and are vulnerable to shortcuts such ","starred":false,"priority":"p2","reading_tier":"skim","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"","category_id":"eval","cover_url":"/covers/2609_00012.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.AI","cs.CR","cs.MA"],"summary":{"core_problem":"检验 LLM 是否能在长达 196 次、每一步都依赖前一步状态的工具调用链中，稳定携带精确中间状态，而不是只在短程或可容错任务上表现正常。","importance":"对预训练研究的价值主要在评测信号，而不是新训练方法。它把长程 agent 失败拆成一个更干净的问题：模型能否在不断增长的上下文中保存、读取、更新精确状态。这个设定比一般 agent benchmark 更可控，因为 MD5 每一步都有唯一正确轨迹，最终 digest 可按 bit 校验，几乎没有“看起来合理但实际错”的灰区。对基座模型训练和评测有两个启发：第一，长程能力不只来自单步 reasoning accuracy，还依赖上下文中状态表示的可恢复性；第二，保留模型自己的 reasoning trace 会显著影响后续状态跟踪，这提示 pretrain/posttrain 阶段对 scratchpad、tool trace、长对话轨迹的建模方式可能会改变长程执行能力。","technical_approach":"任务选择为单个 512-bit block 的 MD5 计算。MD5 有 64 轮，每轮维护四个 32-bit word，即 a、b、c、d。作者把 padding、常数生成、F/G/H/I 轮函数、模加、左旋、mixing、final addition 等拆成工具，形成 canonical 196-step dependent tool-call trace。driver LLM 负责按顺序调用工具，并把每次返回的状态继续传给下一步。强配置中，primitive tool 不再是 CPU 代码，而是 worker LLM：worker 负责 32-bit bitwise operation、modular addition、rotation 等基础计算，CPU 实现只用于离线校验，不参与生成答案。关键设计是 driver/worker 分离：driver 的主要难点是状态跟踪和流程控制，worker 的主要难点是局部精确算术。worker 通过 self-consistency，即多次采样后多数投票，提高 primitive 可靠性。另一个关键条件是 reasoning-in-context：每轮保留模型自己的推理内容，而不是只保留压缩后的工具调用结果。这个选择提高了状态可恢复性，但也增加上下文长度和潜在噪声。","experiments":"核心实验使用 gpt-oss-120b，文中称其为 MoE 模型，每 token 约 5.5B active parameters，temperature 设为 0，并使用短固定 system prompt。结果显示，在非记忆化输入上，模型能够完成全部 196 次调用，并在多数已完成 runs 中复现正确 MD5 digest。进一步用 worker LLM 替换全部 primitive arithmetic 后，driver/worker 组合仍可从零计算 hash。消融结论比绝对分数更重要：去掉 reasoning-in-context 会显著损害长程执行；worker 不做 self-consistency 时，局部算术错误会沿链传播。失败来源被分为 driver state error、worker arithmetic error 和 serving/infrastructure error。由于给定内容没有完整表格，具体成功率、投票样本数和不同配置间的精确差值不宜补写。","reading_guide":"建议泛读 20–30 分钟。优先看任务构造和 trace alignment 部分，确认 196 次调用如何对应 MD5 的 reference trace；再看 driver/worker 设计和 self-consistency 设置，理解作者如何把状态跟踪与算术可靠性拆开；最后看 failure taxonomy。预训练研究员可重点关注 reasoning-in-context 的消融，因为它直接关联训练语料中是否保留中间推理、工具轨迹和长程 scratchpad 的问题。MD5 算法细节本身不用深读，除非要复现实验。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"The work tests whether an LLM can preserve and update exact intermediate state across a 196-step chain of dependent tool calls, under a setting where every step has a bit-checkable ground truth.","importance":"Its main value is as a controlled evaluation design for long-horizon execution. Many agent benchmarks mix state tracking with instruction parsing, open-ended judgment, and shortcutable final answers. MD5 removes most of that ambiguity: the model either follows the canonical trace and returns the correct digest, or it does not. For foundation-model research, the useful signal is that long-horizon reliability is not well captured by single-step accuracy. It also shows that the way scratchpad and tool traces are kept in context can change execution success, which is relevant to pretraining and mid-training data design for tool-use trajectories.","technical_approach":"The benchmark decomposes MD5 on a single 512-bit block into a fixed sequence of 196 dependent tool calls. The driver model must call padding, constants, round functions, modular addition, rotation, mixing, and final addition in the correct order while carrying the four 32-bit state words across the full run. The stronger variant replaces CPU primitive tools with a worker LLM, so bitwise functions and 32-bit arithmetic are also produced by a model. The CPU implementation is used only as a reference for verification. The main system idea is a driver/worker split: the driver handles procedure control and state propagation, while the worker handles local arithmetic. Worker reliability is improved by self-consistency voting. A second critical condition is keeping the model’s own reasoning in the context, rather than reducing the transcript to bare tool outputs.","experiments":"The reported model is gpt-oss-120b, described as an MoE model with about 5.5B active parameters per token, run at temperature 0 with a short fixed system prompt. On non-memorized inputs, it completes the full 196-call procedure and returns the correct digest on a majority of completed runs. In the all-LLM configuration, where primitive arithmetic is handled by worker LLM calls, the driver/worker system can still compute the hash from scratch. The important ablations are qualitative in the provided text: removing reasoning-in-context hurts long-horizon execution, and removing worker self-consistency makes arithmetic errors dominate. Residual failures are attributed to driver state errors, worker arithmetic errors, and serving-level issues. Exact success rates and voting counts are not available in the supplied content.","reading_guide":"Skim in 20–30 minutes. Read the benchmark construction and trace-alignment sections first, because they determine whether the task really isolates state tracking. Then read the driver/worker setup and the ablations on reasoning-in-context and self-consistency. The failure analysis is the most useful part for training researchers. The MD5 background can be read lightly unless reproduction is the goal.","authors_note":""},"recommendation":{"zh":"设计了一个非常 clean 的 MD5 逐步计算任务，纯粹测试 LLM 在长序列工具调用中的状态跟踪（state-tracking）能力，揭示了长程错误级联的严重性，对评估和改进长上下文/Agent 能力很有价值。","en":"Designs a very clean step-by-step MD5 task to purely test LLMs' state-tracking ability in long tool-call sequences, revealing the severity of long-horizon error cascades, valuable for evaluating and improving long-context/Agent capabilities."},"metadata":{"institutions":[],"authors":["Dheeraj Mohandas Pai","Lu Xian"],"notable_authors":[],"tags":["Long-Horizon Tasks","State Tracking","Tool Calling","LLM Evaluation","Error Cascading"],"links":{"arxiv":"https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00012","pdf":"https://arxiv.org/pdf/2609.00012","github":null,"project":null,"source_url":null,"article":null}}},{"id":"2609.00264","title":"The Answer Is Not the Argument","abstract":"Chain-of-thought monitoring is proposed for AI oversight, yet evaluations often provide monitors with a trusted reference answer. We ask whether answer access improves reasoning verification or mainly exposes incorrect conclusions. The reference standard combined physicist annotatio","starred":false,"priority":"p2","reading_tier":"skim","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"","category_id":"eval","cover_url":"/covers/2609_00264.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.AI"],"summary":{"core_problem":"这篇工作要回答的不是“监控模型能不能判断答案对错”，而是更细的一层：当推理链本身有错误、但最终答案恰好是对的时，监控模型能否独立发现并定位推理中的首个错误。很多 CoT 监控设定会把一个“可信参考答案”一并给监控器，这会把“检查论证是否成立”和“看到答案后判断结论像不像对”混在一起。作者用 HLE 物理题上的真实模型解答，而不是人工注入错误，专门构造并标注了这种最关键也最难的情形：正确答案掩盖了错误推理。","importance":"对预训练本身没有直接方法贡献，但对 process supervision、CoT monitorability、以及训练时把“可读推理”当作监督接口这条路线有很直接的约束意义。结论很明确：给监控器参考答案，主要提升的是对错误结论的识别，而不是对论证有效性的验证；一旦答案正确，监控能力明显下降，且错误定位几乎不改善。这意味着很多现有 CoT 监控评测可能高估了真实部署中的监督能力。对于做 reasoning model、process reward model、step-level verifier、甚至 RL 训练中引入监控信号的人，这篇文章提醒的是评测协议本身可能在测“答案一致性偏好”，而不是“论证审查能力”。","technical_approach":"方法设计很克制，核心是把“答案访问”这个变量单独拎出来做信息控制。数据来自 79 道 Humanity’s Last Exam 物理题，共 237 条 step-numbered 解答，由 3 个前沿模型生成，不插入任何人工错误。每条解答都被标注两个标签：最终答案是否正确，以及首个错误步骤在哪里。参考标准不是单一来源，而是物理学家标注、独立 LLM debate、再加 source-masked adjudication 组合而成，目的是尽量降低单一裁判偏差。\n\n监控阶段设置了一个 information ladder，只改变监控器是否能看到答案及其“可信度”：blind（看不到答案）、unverified answer（给一个未认证答案）、certified answer（给可信答案）、blind commitment 后再 reveal answer（先盲判再给答案），外加 reconsideration control 和 stepwise online 条件。这样可以把两个能力拆开：一是 error detection，二是 first-error localization；同时再按 wrong-answer traces 和 correct-answer-but-flawed traces 分层。这个实验设计的关键点在于：生成时不暴露答案，监控时才改变答案可见性，因此可以比较干净地测“读者侧的答案依赖”。\n\n从全文叙述看，作者还刻意避免了跨题 correctness prediction 那类容易利用题目难度、题型先验的评测方式，而是固定同一条 trace，在不同监控条件下重复评估。这样测到的差异更接近“同一份推理文本在不同信息条件下是否更可验证”，而不是“模型会不会猜这题大概难不难”。","experiments":"数据规模不大，但标注质量和实验控制做得比较细。总计 237 条解答，来自 79 道 HLE physics 题；生成模型在这批过滤后的物理题上最终答案正确率约 65%，因此既有足够的正例，也有足够的负例。作者特别指出，最终得到 24 条 critical traces：最终答案正确，但推理链中存在真实错误。这 24 条是整篇文章最关键的分析对象，因为它们排除了“看答案就知道错了”的捷径。\n\n监控器方面用了 8 个 LLM monitors。摘要里给出的核心结果是：当提供 certified answer 时，平均检测率会提高，但这种提升主要来自错误答案样本；在 critical traces 上，答案认证带来的增益很小，且对 first false step 的定位几乎没有帮助。换句话说，monitor 在“答案错”时更像 outcome checker，在“答案对但过程错”时才真正暴露出 argument verification 的上限。\n\n全文前言还给出一个很重要的背景数字：在这批题上三种生成模型的 trace 中，65% 最终答案正确，但并不意味着推理链可靠。作者把这类 answer-process decoupling 和 reward hacking 做了结构类比：输出可接受，不代表过程合规。若监控器只能借答案做判断，就会系统性放过这类样本。\n\n从实验结构推断，值得注意的消融不是模型间谁最好，而是条件差分：blind vs unverified vs certified vs commit-then-reveal。这个差分直接支持文章主张，即“答案访问改变了评测对象”。如果只看 aggregate detection，很容易误以为 monitor 很强；一旦拆到 correct-answer flawed traces，能力会明显塌缩。","reading_guide":"泛读建议是先看引言和方法里的任务分解，再直接看结果表中按 trace 类型拆分的指标。最值得看的不是总准确率，而是三类切片：1）wrong-answer traces；2）correct-answer but flawed traces；3）first-error localization。只要这三块分开看，文章的结论会很清楚。\n\n阅读时重点抓三个问题。第一，reference standard 是怎么做的，尤其是 physicist annotation、LLM debate、source-masked adjudication 各自承担什么角色。第二，information ladder 为什么能隔离“答案帮助验证论证”与“答案帮助检查结论”。第三，commit-then-reveal 和 reconsideration control 这两个条件在多大程度上说明模型是被答案锚定，而不是单纯多看一遍就更好。\n\n预计 20–30 分钟可以抓住主结论。如果时间更少，只看 introduction、methods 中的 condition design、以及结果里 critical traces 的表格和案例分析即可。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"The paper asks a narrower and more important question than generic CoT monitoring: can a monitor detect and localize reasoning errors when the final answer is still correct? Many evaluations give the monitor a trusted reference answer, which conflates two abilities: checking whether the argument is valid, and noticing that the conclusion looks wrong. The authors isolate the hard case where the answer is correct but the derivation contains a genuine mistake, using naturally occurring model traces rather than injected errors.","importance":"This is not a pretraining-method paper, but it matters for process supervision, verifier training, reasoning-model oversight, and any setup that treats exposed reasoning as a reliable supervision interface. The main implication is simple: answer access mostly improves outcome checking, not argument verification. If a benchmark reports strong CoT monitoring performance with trusted answers available, that number may substantially overstate what a monitor can do in deployment. For work on step-level rewards, process labels, monitor-guided RL, or legible reasoning during training, the paper is a useful warning about evaluation leakage.","technical_approach":"$48","experiments":"$49","reading_guide":"Start with the introduction and the methods section that defines the task decomposition. Current benchmarks evaluate this dialogue history as information retrieval over long horizon, temporal reasoning, or knowledge updates, while crucially ignoring the fundamental dynamics of human-agent interaction, i.e. how they said it. To address this gap, we present VoiceLongMemEval (VLME) bench","starred":false,"priority":"p2","reading_tier":"skim","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"","category_id":"eval","cover_url":"/covers/2609_00570.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.AI","cs.LG"],"summary":{"core_problem":"这篇工作关注一个当前长上下文助手评测里几乎空缺的问题：模型能否在超长、多会话历史中记住用户“是怎么说的”，而不只是“说了什么”。也就是把长期记忆和副语言信息结合起来评测。若答案依赖情绪、语调、停顿、笑声、叹气等声学线索，而这些线索在词面上不可恢复，现有只看文本内容的 long-memory benchmark 就不够了。","importance":"它的价值主要在评测资产，而不是方法创新。对基座预训练的直接影响有限，但对语音/音频 LM、原生多模态助手、以及统一建模里“文本转写是否丢失关键信号”这个问题有现实意义。文章给出的结论很直接：即使模型有很长的上下文窗口，若输入里缺少副语言通道，CoT 也补不回来；而显式情绪标签带来的增益最稳定。这对做 speech-text unified pretraining、audio tokenization、以及 memory agent 的人有一个明确提示：长期记忆能力不能只按 lexical retrieval 来测，声学属性是否被编码、保留、可检索，同样会成为系统瓶颈。","technical_approach":"核心贡献是构造了 VoiceLongMemEval。每个样本都要求正确答案只能从副语言元数据恢复，不能从文字内容本身恢复。为此作者在 LongMemEval 风格的长历史框架上，给每个用户 turn 加了五类注释：12 类日常情绪标签、prosody tuple（语速、音高、响度、停顿、强调词）、五类 voice events（笑、叹气、咳嗽、清嗓、吸气）、pragmatic flags（如 sarcasm、uncertainty）、以及 free-text delivery description。这里有个设计细节很重要：free-text 描述被严格限制为“麦克风能听到的声学事实”，不能直接写解释性情绪词，否则任务会退化成字符串匹配。\n\n数据构造分四步：annotation、evidence authoring/hardening、question generation、speech synthesis。每个实例把少量真正有用的 evidence sessions 埋进约 100k token 的长历史里，并加入大量 topic-screened filler sessions 和 emotive but answer-free distractors，防止模型靠情绪密度或主题重合走捷径。needle 位置被分层到早期、中期、晚期，多证据样本还会跨 session 分布。\n\n最关键的是三阶段 adversarial gate，用来排除 lexical leakage。G1 要求 blind render（只有转写文本）不可解；G2 检查 descriptive render（带副语言描述）是否可解；G3 做静态 surface-clean 检查，过滤解释性词、模板化短语和带倾向性的问法。作者先用 7B judge-adversary 迭代，再用 Qwen2.5-72B-Instruct-AWQ 做冻结文件上的终审，并要求连续两次 clean run，以降低随机性。这个 gating 设计说明作者很清楚 benchmark 最容易失真的地方：一旦文字本身泄露情绪，整个任务就不再测 paralinguistic memory。","experiments":"基准总计 523 个问题，核心集 202 个，分布在 326 个带副语言注释的 evidence sessions 中，嵌入约 100k token 的历史。任务覆盖六类问题：affect recall、affective preference、affect update、cross-session affect、temporal-affective reasoning、prosody-disambiguated interpretation，并带 abstention 变体。\n\n作者评测了 8 个模型，包含 3 个闭源和 5 个开源系统。最核心结果是存在稳定的 affect gap：在所有系统上，带副语言信息的条件相对 blind 条件有 +0.09 到 +0.38 的提升，且都达到 p<0.001。进一步的组件消融显示，emotion tags 是最强信号；即便给模型 CoT，也不能替代缺失的副语言上下文。作者还做了 controlled counterfactual，估计纯由 metadata 内容带来的净增益约为 +0.067。\n\n构造过程中的一个有用细节是 gating 失败分析。72B blind adversary 仍能解出 8 个样本，占 7.5%，作者把泄漏机制归纳为五类：pragmatic-prior leakage、outcome tells、gold-matches-prior、default-recovery priors、A/B gifts。后续批次据称在 authoring-time leaks 上降到 0。这部分虽然是 benchmark engineering，但信息量不低，因为它展示了长上下文评测里“题目写法泄漏答案”的常见模式。\n\n从预训练研究视角，实验最有启发的不是谁分数最高，而是两个结论：一，文本转写会系统性丢失长期记忆所需的信息；二，结构化副语言标签比自由 CoT 更能稳定提供可用信号。这说明如果目标是构建真正的语音助手，训练和评测都不能把语音简单压缩成 ASR 文本。","reading_guide":"泛读时先看 benchmark construction 和 adversarial gate，这两部分决定了任务是否真的在测“记住怎么说”。然后看 taxonomy 和 ablation，尤其是 emotion tags、prosody descriptors、voice events 分别贡献多少。最后再看 full vs oracle、blind vs descriptive vs audio 这些输入形态的比较。\n\n如果只想抓住要点，重点看三处：1）五类 annotation 的定义和 lexical gate；2）G1/G2/G3 如何防止 text-only leakage；3）主结果里的 affect gap 与 counterfactual 分析。预计 20 分钟左右可以完成一次有效泛读。\n\n不必花太多时间在具体题目主题上，真正值得借鉴的是 benchmark A natural extension closes the loop: fine-tune the cheap student on the verifier's rejections so the escalation rate, and cost, fall each round. We measure this loop on real LLMs and report four findings. First, the verifier's blind spot, the fraction of the student's wrong answers it accepts, is large and moves adversarially: it grows with student capabi","starred":false,"priority":"p2","reading_tier":"skim","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"","category_id":"eval","cover_url":"/covers/2609_01345.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.AI","cs.CR","cs.LG"],"summary":{"core_problem":"论文研究推理级联中“便宜学生模型 + 强 verifier”的闭环训练是否可靠：当 verifier 只能作为代理判断器而非真实 oracle 时，系统会不会把错误藏进 verifier 的盲区，并在自我训练中放大。结论偏负面：成本节省和真实可靠性之间存在明显张力。","importance":"这篇论文对 pretrain/post-train 接口有直接启发。它提醒训练信号质量比训练闭环本身更关键。把 verifier 的拒绝样本用于继续微调，看起来像在线蒸馏或错误修正，但如果 verifier 有系统性漏检，学生会被训练成更会通过代理判断，而不是更接近真实答案。这和 reward model overoptimization、RLHF 中的 Goodhart 问题是一类风险。对基座研究员来说，价值不在级联系统本身，而在于它把“用模型生成训练信号”的可靠性成本量化了。","technical_approach":"论文形式化了 self-improving cascade：学生模型先回答，verifier 决定接受或拒绝；被拒绝的样本交给 teacher/frontier model 生成替代答案，并用于后续微调学生。作者定义了 verifier blind-spot rate β，即学生错误答案中被 verifier 接受的比例。关键区别在于，循环只能看到 verifier 可检测的错误，无法看到“错误但被接受”的部分。论文进一步推导了一个误差下界：用户侧错误不会随循环消失，而会受初始“错误且被接受”的质量控制，近似锚定在 q0β0。这个结论解释了为什么 verifier 视角的指标可以持续变好，而真实质量没有同步改善。","experiments":"实验使用真实 LLM，在 GSM8K 和 hard MATH 上测试 Qwen2.5 系列学生模型，规模从 0.5B 到 32B，verifier 使用 OpenAI 系列模型，最高到 gpt-5-mini。主要发现有四点。第一，固定 verifier 时，学生越强，错误越隐蔽，β 从 0.5B 学生的 0.12 上升到 14B 学生的 0.55。第二，更强 verifier 可以降低盲区，例如 hard MATH 上 frontier verifier 可把 β 降到约 0.05，但它会对 46% 查询升级，而真实错误率是 39%，也就是通过过度拒绝换取低漏检，成本优势被削弱。第三，基于 verifier rejected tail 的朴素纠错微调没有让小学生模型变好，反而导致退化，跨模型家族和同家族 teacher 都观察到类似问题。第四，系统内部监控指标会失真：verifier 计算的错误率看起来约为 3%，但真实交付错误率可摆动到 32%。","reading_guide":"建议泛读 40–60 分钟。重点看 Introduction 中的四个实测发现、Section 3 的级联形式化、Section 5 的 blind-spot 随学生和 verifier 能力变化的实验、Section 6 的误差下界推导。若关心训练信号污染，可以细看自我微调失败的实验设置和 Appendix A 的 two-population proof。最值得带走的是一个工程判断：级联 verifier 不是 oracle，用它筛出的训练数据会带有选择偏差；闭环优化会优先消除可见错误，留下更难被监控发现的错误。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"The paper studies whether an inference cascade can safely close the loop by fine-tuning a cheap student on examples rejected by a stronger verifier. The central issue is that the verifier is not an oracle. It has blind spots, and a training loop driven by its decisions may optimize the student toward passing the verifier rather than producing correct answers.","importance":"The main value for pretraining and post-training research is the measurement of proxy-signal failure. The setup resembles online distillation, corrective fine-tuning, and reward-model-based improvement. The result is a cautionary one: if the signal only covers verifier-detectable errors, training can reduce visible failures while preserving or amplifying hidden ones. This is directly relevant to synthetic data filtering, model-as-judge pipelines, RLHF-style optimization, and continual improvement systems.","technical_approach":"The authors define a self-improving cascade with a student S, a verifier V, a teacher G, and an oracle O used only for analysis. The cascade serves the student answer when V accepts it and escalates to G when V rejects it. The parametric loop then fine-tunes the student on the rejected tail. The key metric is the verifier blind-spot rate β: the fraction of wrong student answers accepted by the verifier. The paper argues that the loop only trains on verifier-visible errors. Errors that are wrong but accepted remain outside the training signal. This yields an error-floor argument: delivered error cannot be driven to zero by the loop and is anchored by the initial mass of confidently wrong accepted answers, approximately q0β0.","experiments":"The empirical study uses real LLMs on GSM8K and hard MATH. Students are Qwen2.5 models from 0.5B to 32B, and verifiers include OpenAI models up to gpt-5-mini. With a fixed verifier, β increases as the student becomes more capable, from 0.12 at 0.5B to 0.55 at 14B. Stronger verifiers reduce the blind spot, but at a cost: on hard MATH, a frontier verifier brings β down to about 0.05 while escalating 46% of queries, compared with a true error rate of 39%. Naive corrective fine-tuning on rejected examples does not improve the small students tested; it degrades them and can lead to cumulative collapse. The most operationally important result is observability failure: verifier-computed error can stay near 3% while true delivered error reaches 32%.","reading_guide":"Skim in about 40–60 minutes. Read the introduction for the four measured findings, Section 3 for the formal cascade setup, Section 5 for the blind-spot measurements, and Section 6 for the error-floor argument. The fine-tuning failure experiments are worth checking if you work on synthetic correction data or model-as-judge filtering. The practical lesson is that verifier-generated supervision is selected by what the verifier can notice, and this selection bias can dominate the training loop.","authors_note":""},"recommendation":{"zh":"这篇该看，尤其 β 随 student 变强反而上升这一点，会直接影响你对 weak verifier 造训练信号的信任；先看摘要里的数字，再找 blind spot 定义和闭环实验。","en":"Worth reading for the β trend alone: verifier blind spots growing with student capability should change how much you trust weak verifiers as training signal generators."},"metadata":{"institutions":[],"authors":["Dushyant Rajput"],"notable_authors":[],"tags":["verifier","inference-cascade","reliability","weak-supervision","cost-quality"],"links":{"arxiv":"https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01345","pdf":"https://arxiv.org/pdf/2609.01345","github":null,"project":null,"source_url":null,"article":null}}},{"id":"2609.01552","title":"Can LLMs Discover Scientific Laws in Real and Parallel Worlds?","abstract":"Scientific equation discovery has long been central to scientific progress, proceeding through iterative cycles of hypothesis generation, observational testing, and refinement under scientific constraints. As LLM capabilities advance and their role in AI for Science expands, it remains an open problem whether they can genuinely discover scientific laws and how this ability should be evaluated. Existing evaluations, however, often either simplify discovery through synthetic settings or reuse publ","starred":false,"priority":"p2","reading_tier":"skim","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"","category_id":"eval","cover_url":"/covers/2609_01552.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.AI","cs.LG"],"summary":{"core_problem":"这篇工作要回答的不是“LLM会不会做符号回归”这么窄的问题，而是更严格的：在真实科学数据、真实噪声、真实领域约束下，LLM能否提出既拟合数据、又有科学意义、还能经得起验证的定律。现有评测要么用教材级经典公式，容易测到记忆；要么用合成世界，控制性强但离真实科研太远。SciLaws-Bench试图把这两类缺口同时补上：一方面用较少被模型记住的真实文献与真实数据构造开放式发现任务；另一方面把同一问题改写成可主动实验的 parallel world，用隐藏的结构变体来测“发现新结构”而不是“复述已知公式”。","importance":"对预训练研究的直接价值不在于提出新的训练算法，而在于把“模型是否真的具备科学归纳能力”拆成了几个可测的部分：记忆、拟合、结构发现、实验设计、候选式选择。这种拆法对基座模型评测很有启发，因为它避免把高分简单等同于能力强。更具体地说，这个 benchmark 说明：当前 frontier LLM 在开放式规律发现上已经有一定能力，但主要优势仍偏向检索和重组已知模式；一旦问题要求从真实噪声中识别新结构，性能会明显掉下来。对做预训练的人，这提示数据记忆、工具使用、主动采样和候选重排序可能是不同能力源，不能只靠通用 perplexity 或常规科学问答来判断。","technical_approach":"方法上有三个关键设计。第一，数据构建不是直接抓教材公式，而是从较活跃但不那么“公众熟知”的科学文献里收集 linked paper-dataset pairs，最后形成 118 个问题、291 条候选规律、381 篇论文、约 8M 数据点，覆盖六个学科，并区分 single-group 与 multi-group 两类任务。multi-group 设定要求模型发现共享函数形式，同时允许部分参数按组变化，这比单一全局拟合更接近真实科研中的“结构共享、参数异质”。\n\n第二，评测被拆成两个互补环境。SciLaws-Real 给定固定真实观测，要求模型提出闭式公式。这里已发表公式只是 reference baseline，不是唯一正确答案；评测同时看 held-out predictive fit 和 source-grounded scientific validity。也就是说，公式不能只在数值上拟合，还要满足文献与领域约束，例如物理可解释性、适用区间内行为合理、不能引入不存在的机制。这个设计比单纯 symbolic regression 更严格，因为它把“科学上站得住”单独拿出来判。\n\n第三，SciLaws-Parallel 把同一科学问题转成一个可查询世界。隐藏规律不是原论文公式，而是基于已发表形式合成出的结构变体，系数和残差噪声按真实数据校准。模型从零开始，预算内自己决定做哪些测量，再去恢复这个未发表的隐藏规律。这样做的好处是：既保留真实科学语境，又能明确区分“记住文献”与“发现新结构”。\n\n执行层面，作者用统一的 ReAct-style agent 框架评测 9 个前沿模型，给 Python sandbox；在 Parallel 设定下再给固定预算的实验接口，每个 trial 最多 30 轮交互。这个统一 agent protocol 很重要，因为它把工具使用和多轮探索纳入评测，而不是只测一次性文本输出。","experiments":"核心结果有三点。第一，模型很难同时兼顾拟合和科学有效性。文中给出的例子是某核物理任务里，模型能写出拟合更好的公式，但它引入了并不存在的共振峰，因此在 validity 检查里失败。这说明仅看 held-out error 会高估能力。\n\n第二，模型在“复现已知规律”与“发现新结构”之间表现差异很大。冷启动记忆审计显示，SciLaws-Bench 相比教材级公式更不容易被直接召回；在这种设定下，模型若记忆较弱，会更愿意探索超出已发表公式的候选式，但真正成功恢复 novel structure 的比例仍然不高。也就是说，探索行为出现了，但结构归纳还不稳定。\n\n第三，模型生成候选式的上限高于它们自我选择最佳候选的能力。best-of-N 搜索能持续挖出更强候选，但模型自己的 self-selection 只能拿到其中一小部分收益。这一点很关键：瓶颈不只在 proposal generation，也在 ranking / verification / model selection。对后训练和 test-time scaling 都有直接启发。\n\n从规模上，benchmark 包含 118 个问题、291 个候选规律、381 篇论文、约 8M 真实数据点；评测对象是 9 个 frontier LLM，包括 GPT-5.5、Claude Opus 4.8、Gemini 3.5 Flash、DeepSeek-V4 Pro 等，在统一 agent 框架下比较。摘要与引言没有给出完整总表数字，但全文结论很明确：当前模型“有能力迹象，但远未可靠”。","reading_guide":"泛读时先看 Introduction 和 Figure 1，抓住 SciLaws-Real 与 SciLaws-Parallel 的分工。然后重点看 benchmark construction：为什么要从不那么热门的文献取题、怎样做 cold-recall audit、single-group 与 multi-group 分别测什么。接着看 evaluation protocol，尤其是 predictive fit 与 scientific validity 如何分开判，以及 Parallel world 的 hidden-law synthesis 如何避免退化成纯合成玩具题。\n\n结果部分建议优先看三类分析：一是 fit 与 validity 的冲突案例；二是 recall 与 novel-structure recovery 的关系；三是 best-of-N 与 self-selection 的差距。如果时间有限，抓住这三点就够了，预计 35–50 分钟能读完主要信息。若要深挖，值得看 validity rubric 是否足够稳定、parallel world 的结构变体是否会引入分布偏移，以及 agent budget However, accuracy alone misses properties that matter for real long-horizon tasks. We introduce ECCBench, a benchmark and evaluation protocol that measures memory beyond a system's capacity--its raw accuracy at a specific budget--via three axes we call ECC: efficiency--the computation, in FLOPs, needed to answer from memory; compres","starred":false,"priority":"p2","reading_tier":"skim","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"","category_id":"eval","cover_url":"/covers/2609_00103.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.LG","cs.AI"],"summary":{"core_problem":"论文提出的问题是：只用问答准确率评估 LLM/VLM 的长程记忆是不够的，因为高准确率可能来自反复扫描原始输入、利用问题偏置，或者在不确定时仍然猜测。","importance":"ECCBench 对预训练研究的启发在于，它把“记忆能力”拆成 capacity 之外的三个可测维度：效率、压缩、校准。效率关注回答需要多少 FLOPs；压缩关注模型是否能利用输入冗余，而不是只记住显著信息；校准关注模型是否会根据不确定性和错误代价选择 abstain。这对长上下文模型、视频模型、外部记忆模块和 recurrent/SSM 类架构的评估都有参考意义。","technical_approach":"论文把记忆系统形式化为在线写入和读取问题。系统逐步观察输入流，维护内部状态，之后基于该状态回答查询。评估不假设具体实现，可以是上下文窗口、循环状态、检索索引或显式 memory store，只要求能统计写入和读取的计算量。ECC 的三个轴分别是：Efficiency，用写入和读取总 FLOPs 衡量；Compression，通过控制输入流的信息含量和冗余程度，区分真正压缩能力与 saliency bias；Calibration，通过提供 abstention 选项和错误代价，观察系统是否在不确定时降低覆盖率。","experiments":"论文发现，测试的预训练 VLM 在文本上表现出一定压缩记忆能力，但在视频上通常不能有效利用可压缩结构；在文本和视频两种模态上，校准都较差，错误代价提高后覆盖率变化不明显。论文还比较了不同 memory backbone 在超过训练序列长度后的行为，发现若干非 RoPE Transformer 架构能取得更好的 compression-calibration tradeoff，说明架构会显著影响记忆的效率、压缩和校准性质。","reading_guide":"建议泛读 Introduction 和 Background，重点看 ECC 三个指标的定义，以及为什么 accuracy 会被 brute-force scanning、query saliency 和不校准回答混淆。实验部分优先看 VLM 在文本与视频上的差异，以及 memory backbone 对比。预计 20 到 30 分钟可以抓住主要结论；如果做长上下文评测或外部记忆系统，再细看 FLOPs 统计方式和 abstention/cost 设计。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"The paper argues that accuracy alone is an incomplete measure of memory in LLMs and VLMs. A system can answer correctly by brute-force rescanning the input, exploiting biased queries, or guessing when uncertain.","importance":"ECCBench is relevant because it reframes memory evaluation beyond raw capacity. It separates three properties: efficiency, measured by the compute needed to answer from memory; compression, measured by whether the system benefits from redundancy in the input rather than only saliency priors; and calibration, measured by whether it abstains when uncertainty and error cost warrant it. This is useful for evaluating long-context models, video-language models, retrieval-augmented systems, recurrent states, and explicit memory modules.","technical_approach":"The paper formalizes memory as an online write-read process. A system observes a stream, updates an internal state, and later answers queries from that state. The framework is implementation-agnostic: the memory can be a context window, a recurrent hidden state, a retrieval index, or an editable store. The only required instrumentation is compute accounting for writing and reading, reported in FLOPs. Compression is tested by varying the information content and redundancy of the stream, which helps separate compression from saliency. Calibration is tested by giving the model an abstention option and varying the cost of an incorrect answer.","experiments":"The main empirical finding is that pretrained VLMs show some memory compression over text but generally fail to do so over video. They are also poorly calibrated in both modalities: increasing the cost of a wrong answer has little effect on coverage. compiler 把同一个 latent 实例物化成不同可执行 surface：SVG、DOM、canvas、tree、graph、table 等；每种 surface 都有自己的可执行引用（id/path/index 等）。\n3) 模型只需要输出环境要求的引用；评测器把预测 deterministically 映射回 latent 空间，对照 gold target，输出：\n- 是否 valid candidate（引用格式/词表是否合规）\n- 是否 exact（绑定是否正确）\n- 违反了哪些结构约束；在声明了结构顺序的 family 里给出 first-violation（用于诊断“最先错在行选择还是列选择/角色选择”等）。\n4) 额外强调 measurement：候选顺序扰动会改变错误频率与具体 case，但错误“类型”可能稳定；因此区分 outcome stability 与 diagnostic-type stability。\n数据规模刻意控制：需要唯一 gold、确定性映射、结构 oracle recovery、shortcut checks，目标是可诊断性与可靠性，不追求大样本排名。","experiments":"发布内容：主套件 250 case（broad suite）、额外 240 case 的 targeted resol","starred":true,"priority":"p1","reading_tier":"must_read","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"","category_id":"multimodal","cover_url":"/covers/2609_01147.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.CV","cs.CL"],"summary":{"core_problem":"论文解决的是像素空间文本表示学习的几个基础设计问题：如何让视觉编码器直接读渲染文本和真实图文输入，并在低分辨率预训练后泛化到高分辨率文档、避免靠字体和画布记忆走捷径、保持真实视觉语义 grounding，同时获得多语言文本感知能力。问题不只是“把文本渲染成图片再训练 ViT”，而是这种范式在文档检索、Visual STS、多语言视觉文本理解中为什么容易失效，以及哪些训练变量真正决定泛化。","importance":"这篇工作对基座预训练的价值在于，它把 pixel-text learning 从经验配方拆成了可验证的预训练设计原则。它直接关联三类问题：第一，统一视觉-语言编码器是否可以用像素作为文本接口，减少 OCR 或文本 tokenizer 的强依赖；第二，文档 RAG 和多模态检索是否可以依赖一个原生视觉编码器完成阅读、语义匹配和压缩；第三，视觉文本预训练的数据配比、渲染策略、分辨率课程和多语言课程如何共同影响表示质量。贡献量级不是单个 benchmark Linguist II 展开。作者先用 13M 样本的紧凑消融集做受控实验，再把结论扩展到 280M examples 的训练。核心技术有四点。第一，使用 Native-resolution Vision Transformer / NaViT 式编码器，支持可变分辨率和宽高比，避免固定 224×224 训练与 4K 文档推理之间的硬错配。作者强调，训练时不一定要直接用高分辨率文档，变量自然图像分辨率和渲染文本字体大小可以作为 spatial proxies，让模型学到尺度变化。第二，layout-aware visual augmentation 在训练时动态渲染文本，改变字体、背景、空间排布和视觉扰动。这样做是为了迫使模型编码文本语义和布局结构，而不是记住固定字体、纯色画布、字符局部纹理等低级线索。第三，用统一 contrastive objective 同时训练自然 image-text pairs 和 rendered text-text pairs。自然图文对不是附加项；它提供真实视觉世界的语义 grounding，防止模型退化成只在合成文本图像上工作的视觉文本编码器。第四，多语言训练采用两阶段 curriculum：先大规模无监督多语言视觉文本预训练，建立不同文字系统的像素感知能力；再用高质量语义 mid-training 激活并对齐跨语言语义。相对于固定分辨率 ViT、纯渲染文本预训练、单一字体/画布、单阶段多语言对齐，这个方案更关注训练信号之间的耦合关系。","experiments":"实验分为设计消融和规模化验证。消融部分围绕四个 RQ 展开，使用 13M-example ablation dataset。主要结论很清楚：变量自然图像分辨率和变量字体大小能帮助低分辨率预训练模型外推到高分辨率文档检索；去掉自然 image-text pairs This paper systematically investigates the synergy and conflict between understanding and generation at the representation, task, and system levels, using a structurally native (pixel-in, pixel-out) setting without pretrained vision priors.","importance":"This work provides a clear diagnosis of the underlying mechanisms in AR+Diffusion joint modeling. It dispels the myth that 'unification implies synergy,' revealing asymmetric interference when understanding and generation share computation paths. The proposed task-decoupled architecture offers crucial empirical guidance for architectural design (especially parameter allocation and routing) in future native multimodal pretraining.","technical_approach":"The study employs a controlled framework: raw pixels in and out (no vision encoder or VAE), combining autoregressive text modeling with flow matching for visual generation. At the representation level, it compares a dense shared model with a modality-decoupled Mixture of Tokens (MoT). Finding that the dense model favors understanding while modality-decoupled MoT favors generation, the authors propose a task-decoupled MoT: keeping understanding visual tokens in the language branch while routing generation visual tokens to a specialized branch, achieving soft alignment. The study also evaluates task-level synergy (geometry, vector graphics, 3D) and system-level performance (end-to-end UMM vs. agentic pipeline).","experiments":"Representation level: Joint training in the dense model improves understanding but degrades generation; modality-decoupled MoT shows the opposite. The task-decoupled MoT successfully mitigates this trade-off. Task level: Joint training yields bidirectional improvements when tasks share domain knowledge. System level: For complex image editing, the end-to-end UMM outperforms an agentic pipeline built on the same base model and data.","reading_guide":"Focus on the Introduction for the definitions and comparative logic of the three architectures (Dense, Modality-decoupled MoT, Task-decoupled MoT). However, it remains unclear how consistently these surface forms are processed, especially when the evidence conflicts with the model's parametric knowledge. We study modality robustness under knowledge conflict across 13 MLLMs and two datasets, and find them far from robust. (1) Contrary to common bel","starred":false,"priority":"p2","reading_tier":"skim","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"","category_id":"multimodal","cover_url":"/covers/2609_00550.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.CL"],"summary":{"core_problem":"这项工作研究 MLLM 在知识冲突下是否能对语义相同、模态不同的证据保持一致。例如，同一段文字以纯文本输入和以截图形式输入时，模型是否会给出相同答案；当文本证据和图像证据互相冲突时，模型会信任哪一方。","importance":"这对多模态预训练和对齐有直接启发。许多 MLLM 把文本 token 和视觉 token 送入不同的编码路径，再通过投影或融合模块接入语言模型。这样的架构可能让“证据权威性”受到模态形式影响，而不是只由语义内容决定。结果说明，文档图像、网页截图、PDF、扫描件等输入形式不能被简单视为文本的等价包装。对 native multimodal pretraining 来说，训练目标需要显式约束跨模态语义一致性，也需要在知识冲突场景下校准模型对外部证据和参数知识的取舍。","technical_approach":"作者把问题形式化为 knowledge conflict 下的 evidence-authority 判断。设模型参数知识支持答案 a_int，外部证据支持不同答案 a_ext。为了隔离模态因素，外部证据的语义内容保持不变，只改变呈现形式：纯文本 e_T 或由同一文本渲染得到的图像 e_I。实验包含两个设置：单证据设置中，外部证据与参数知识冲突，用来测试模型是否因为证据模态不同而改变对上下文的采纳；多证据设置中，文本和图像证据彼此冲突，用来测试模型在多源证据中如何分配信任。作者还扩展到两个实际场景：多模态 RAG 和安全拒答，并比较 prompting 与 conflict-aware fine-tuning 等缓解方式。","experiments":"实验覆盖 13 个 MLLM 和两个数据集。主要发现有三点。第一，在单证据冲突场景中，和常见的“文本偏置”说法不同，模型更容易采纳图像形式的上下文，即使该图像只是同一段文字的渲染版本。第二，当文本证据和图像证据同时出现且互相冲突时，模型偏好的模态并不稳定，会随输入顺序、模型家族和数据集变化。第三，这种不稳定会影响实际系统：在多模态 RAG 中，把关键信息以图像形式呈现时，模型对无关上下文的干扰更小，提示它可能缓解 lost-in-the-middle；在安全场景中，将有害请求渲染为图像会使攻击成功率平均提高 6.99 个百分点。缓解实验显示，简单 prompt 不足以稳定模型，基于知识冲突数据的微调能部分平衡模态偏好，但不能完全消除不一致。","reading_guide":"建议泛读，预计 25 到 35 分钟。重点看问题形式化、数据构造和主结果表。数据构造尤其重要：它是否真正把参数知识、外部证据内容和模态形式分开，决定了结论的可信度。主结果部分需要关注单证据和多证据设置的差异，因为两者测到的不是同一个现象。若关注多模态预训练，建议额外看缓解实验，尤其是 conflict-aware fine-tuning 的数据格式和训练目标；这部分能提示如何在训练阶段加入跨模态一致性约束。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"The paper asks whether multimodal LLMs treat semantically identical evidence consistently when it is presented in different modalities, especially under knowledge conflict. A typical case is a factual passage shown as plain text versus the same passage rendered as an image, with the passage contradicting the model’s parametric knowledge.","importance":"The work matters for multimodal foundation-model training because it shows that modality is not just a surface format. Text tokens and visual tokens often enter the model through different tokenizers and encoders, and the model may assign authority to evidence based on that pathway rather than on semantic content. This is directly relevant to document understanding, multimodal RAG, screenshot-based agents, and native multimodal pretraining. It suggests that cross-modal semantic consistency and conflict-aware evidence calibration should be trained explicitly, rather than assumed to emerge from generic image-text instruction tuning.","technical_approach":"The paper formalizes modality robustness as an evidence-authority problem under knowledge conflict. The model’s parametric knowledge supports an internal answer, while external evidence supports a different answer. The semantic content of the evidence is held fixed, and only the modality changes: plain text or a rendered image of the same text. The evaluation has two main configurations. In the single-evidence setting, external evidence conflicts with parametric knowledge, and the question is whether the model accepts it differently across modalities. In the multi-evidence setting, text and image evidence conflict with each other, and the question is which modality the model follows. The paper also tests downstream effects in multimodal RAG and safety refusal, then evaluates mitigation methods including prompting and conflict-aware fine-tuning.","experiments":"The evaluation spans 13 multimodal LLMs and two datasets. The main findings are consistent and practically relevant. In the single-evidence setting, models are more willing to follow contradictory context when it is rendered as an image than when it is provided as text, which runs against the usual assumption of a text preference. In the two-evidence setting, modality preference becomes unstable: it varies with input order, model family, and dataset. The downstream tests show that format alone can change system behavior. In multimodal RAG, presenting salient knowledge as an image makes models less distracted by irrelevant context, suggesting a connection to lost-in-the-middle behavior. In safety evaluation, rendering harmful requests as images increases attack success rate by 6.99 percentage points on average. Prompting does not reliably fix the issue, while conflict-aware fine-tuning partially reduces the imbalance.","reading_guide":"Skim the paper in 25–35 minutes. pretraining，而不是直接复用现成视频模型。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"ZimaBlue addresses the scaling bottleneck in robot manipulation: action-labeled robot trajectories are costly and narrow, while large egocentric video corpora contain rich physical experience but lack action labels and therefore do not fit standard VLA training directly.","importance":"Its relevance to pretraining research is the framing of robotic generalization as scalable embodied video pretraining. The paper uses a World Action Model rather than a pure VLA policy or an off-the-shelf video generator because WAMs connect future visual evolution with action consequences. This makes unlabeled video useful for learning object affordances, contact dynamics, tool use, and long-horizon structure before dense robot action labels are available. The training split between video pretraining, cross-embodiment grounding, and target-embodiment calibration is the main design insight.","technical_approach":"ZimaBlue follows a three-stage curriculum. First, it performs causal embodied video pretraining on large-scale human and robot egocentric videos, scaling up to more than 120,000 hours. The model predicts future visual states from past observations and language instructions, encouraging semantic, temporal, and physical regularities to be learned before target-robot action supervision. Second, it uses multi-embodiment robot trajectories for video-action mid-training. A unified action representation standardizes states and actions across heterogeneous robot platforms, allowing action grounding to transfer across embodiments. Third, it performs specific-embodiment post-training to calibrate the model to the deployment robot and action space. This differs from generic video-model initialization by emphasizing causal, sequential, instruction-conditioned, action-relevant prediction. It differs from policy-only VLA training by using unlabeled video as a scalable source of physical priors.","experiments":"The evaluation focuses on zero-shot manipulation tasks absent from the training data, rather than only video prediction quality or in-distribution imitation. Adding large-scale video pretraining further improves performance and shows a consistent scaling trend, although the provided material does not expose the final success number. The most important ablations are likely video pretraining scale, the contribution of human egocentric videos, cross-embodiment robot data, the unified action representation, and task-level generalization patterns.","reading_guide":"Skim in about 30–40 minutes. Focus on the three-stage curriculum, the causal video pretraining objective, the unified action representation, and the zero-shot manipulation evaluation. For pretraining-oriented readers, the central point is the division of labor across data sources: action-free video supplies physical and temporal priors, cross-embodiment data supplies action grounding, and target-robot demonstrations calibrate control. The discussion of why generic video backbones are mismatched for robot control is also worth reading, since it motivates embodied video pretraining rather than direct reuse of existing video generators.","authors_note":""},"recommendation":{"zh":"展示了从无动作视频预训练到带动作微调的完整 pipeline，其 mid-training 阶段对齐视觉动力学和动作表征的思路，对多模态预训练研究员有启发。","en":"Demonstrates a complete pipeline from action-free video pretraining to action-conditioned fine-tuning; the idea of aligning visual dynamics and action representations during mid-training is inspiring for multimodal pretraining researchers."},"metadata":{"institutions":[],"authors":["Xionghao Wu","Yijun Yang","Shiyang Zhou","Haoze Sun","Jianhui Liu","Songsong Yu","Jiyao Zhang","Wenbo Li","Bo Wang","Guoqing Ma","Lin Song","Renjie Liao","Shenghe Zheng","Wei Tang","Xiaojuan Qi","Yanwei Li","Yuan Zhang","Zhuotao Tian","Haoyang Huang","Nan Duan"],"notable_authors":[],"tags":["World Action Models","Video Pre-training","Embodied AI","Robotic Manipulation"],"links":{"arxiv":"https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00188","pdf":"https://arxiv.org/pdf/2609.00188","github":null,"project":null,"source_url":null,"article":null}}},{"id":"2609.00231","title":"Beyond Language Priors: Diagnosing and Fixing Visual-Origin Hallucinations in Multimodal LLM","abstract":"Existing research on object hallucination in multimodal large language models (MLLMs) predominantly attributes the problem to language priors such as over-reliance on textual co-occurrence statistics. Gr","starred":false,"priority":"p2","reading_tier":"skim","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"","category_id":"multimodal","cover_url":"/covers/2609_00231.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.CV"],"summary":{"core_problem":"这篇工作要解决的不是泛泛的“多模态幻觉”，而是其中一个被长期低估的来源：视觉端本身的错误感知与跨模态对齐失真。作者指出，现有解释大多把物体幻觉归因于语言先验，例如文本共现偏置、解码时过度依赖语言 token、长输出中的语言惯性。但在短答案场景，尤其是“图中是否有某个物体”这类存在性判断里，模型往往没有太多语言推理空间，错误更可能来自视觉特征提取失败、图文嵌入错位，以及视觉注意力落点异常。论文的核心问题因此变成：如何证明这类 visual-origin hallucination 是独立存在、可测量、且可被针对性修复的机制，而不是语言先验的附属现象。","importance":"对基座研究的直接启发不在于提出了一个新的通用 MLLM 架构，而在于把“幻觉成因分解”往前推进了一步。它给出了一套较完整的诊断链条：先用图文 embedding 相似度和 Grad-CAM 熵证明 hallucinated case 的视觉表征异常，再通过替换视觉编码器、加噪、降采样等干预做因果验证，最后把诊断结果转成训练信号。这个思路对预训练和中期训练都有价值：如果幻觉的一部分来自视觉表征脆弱性，那么只靠语言侧对齐、RLHF 或解码修补并不够，视觉编码器质量、图文对齐目标、以及困难负样本构造都会变成更关键的训练设计点。贡献量级更像是“把一个常被混在一起的问题拆开，并给出低成本修复路径”，而不是改写整个范式。","technical_approach":"方法分两步。第一步是 AHAF，Adversarial Hallucination Attribute Flipping。作者用 PGD 对输入图像做定向微扰，目标不是传统分类攻击，而是让模型在同一目标物体的存在性判断上发生翻转，例如从“有”变“没有”或反过来。这样得到的原图与扰动图在语义上高度接近，差异被尽量限制在会触发幻觉边界的视觉因素上。作者强调这比普通 contrastive fine-tuning 里随机抽负样本更有效，因为随机负样本通常同时改变背景、主体、场景布局，模型很容易继续依赖粗粒度视觉差异，而学不到“哪些细微视觉线索会把判断推向幻觉”。\n\n第二步是 ACFT，Adversarial Contrastive Fine-Tuning。它把 AHAF 生成的成对样本用于对比式微调，核心目标是拉近正确图文对、拉远会诱发错误判断的对抗样本表示。论文的关键点不只是“用了 contrastive loss”，而是负样本构造被严格控制：正负对共享几乎相同的图像内容，唯一系统性差异是会改变 hallucination attribute 的微扰。这让训练信号更聚焦于视觉决策边界，而不是泛化成普通的图文匹配学习。\n\n从全文描述看，作者还把 AHAF 当成诊断工具：如果很小的像素级扰动就能翻转回答，说明当前 MLLM 的视觉表示离错误决策边界很近，clean image 上的判断稳定性并不高。这个观察比方法本身更值得注意，因为它提示很多“幻觉修复”可能只是把输出层校正了，但没有真正把视觉表征推离脆弱区域。\n\n与已有方法相比，VCD、OPERA、VTI 更偏推理时或后处理约束；RLHF、DPO 类方法偏输出偏好校正；普通 OCFT 的负样本不够针对。ACFT 的区别在于，它直接围绕视觉错配机制构造训练样本，并且没有推理时额外开销。","experiments":"实验部分最有信息量的是三类证据。\n\n第一类是诊断证据。作者在 LLaVA v1.5 上比较正确样本与幻觉样本的图文 embedding cosine similarity，正确样本平均为 0.158，幻觉样本为 -0.122，差距很大，说明 hallucination case 里跨模态对齐确实明显恶化。Grad-CAM 分析进一步显示注意力模式发生“反转”：当目标物体真实存在时，幻觉样本的热图更分散；当目标物体不存在时，幻觉样本反而更集中到无关区域。用归一化 Shannon entropy 量化后，在目标存在场景，幻觉样本熵比正确样本高 5.1%；在目标不存在场景，熵低 6.2%。这组结果支撑了“视觉端看错了”而不是单纯“语言端瞎编”。\n\n第二类是因果干预。作者直接改动视觉输入或视觉编码器：在 POPE 上，加入 Gaussian noise、做 downsampling、或换更弱的 encoder，会把平均准确率从 0.842 拉低到 0.739–0.822；换成更强的 SigLIP-SO400M encoder，则提升到 0.864。这个实验很关键，因为它把相关性推进到因果层面：视觉特征质量变化会系统性改变 hallucination 表现。\n\n第三类是方法效果。ACFT 只使用 COCO 的 0.9% 数据进行微调，就在 POPE 和 MME 上优于 VCD、OPERA、VTI，以及 LLaVA-RLHF、DPO 变体等后训练方法，同时保持一般视觉理解能力。摘要里没有给出完整表格数字，但从全文叙述看，作者重点强调三点：一是数据效率高，不需要大规模重训；二是零推理开销；三是相较普通 OCFT，ACFT 的优势来自更干净的困难负样本，而不是单纯多加一个对比损失。\n\n如果精读实验，建议特别看 OCFT 与 ACFT 的对比、target vs. non-target similarity gap 的分析，以及不同视觉编码器替换后的变化。这些部分最能说明方法为什么有效，而不是只看最终 benchmark 排名。","reading_guide":"这篇更适合带着“诊断链条是否闭环”去读。\n\n先看 Introduction 里两个 finding 和 causal validation，确认作者不是只做现象描述，而是把 embedding、attention、encoder intervention 串起来。接着重点看 Methods 中 AHAF 的构造细节：PGD 优化目标怎么定义，如何保证翻转的是 hallucination attribute 而不是把图像彻底破坏，正负样本如何配对进入 contrastive loss。这里决定了 ACFT 是否真的在学“幻觉边界”，还是只是在学对抗鲁棒性。\n\n实验部分优先看四块：第一，图文相似度与 Grad-CAM hallucination: errors that originate from the visual side rather than from language priors. Prior work mostly explains object hallucination through textual co-occurrence bias, language-dominant decoding, or insufficient reasoning supervision. The authors argue that this explanation is incomplete for short-form object-existence queries, where the model has little room to rely on long textual continuation. In that setting, hallucination can stem from poor visual feature extraction, weak image-text alignment, and unstable visual attention. The central question is therefore whether this visual-origin hallucination is a distinct, measurable mechanism, and whether it can be repaired with targeted training rather than generic post-hoc decoding fixes.","importance":"Its value for foundation-model research is diagnostic before it is architectural. The paper provides a fairly complete chain: identify abnormal image-text similarity and abnormal attention patterns on hallucinated samples, intervene on the visual encoder and input quality to test causality, then turn that diagnosis into a data-efficient fine-tuning objective. This matters because it shifts part of the hallucination discussion from language-side alignment to representation quality on the vision side. For pretraining and mid-training, that implies stronger attention to visual encoder quality, cross-modal alignment objectives, and the construction of hard negatives that isolate visual decision boundaries. opt","starred":false,"priority":"p2","reading_tier":"skim","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"","category_id":"post_train","cover_url":"/covers/2609_00213.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.CL"],"summary":{"core_problem":"这篇工作研究的是多奖励 RL 微调里的一个结构性问题：把多个奖励维度用固定权重压成单一标量后，训练信号会丢失维度信息，导致策略过早学会“容易拿分”的维度，而长期忽略真正困难但更关键的维度。作者把这个现象定义为 aggregation-induced reward hacking，其中包含两个机制：profile aliasing，即不同奖励组合被映射成同一个标量；shortcut lock-in，即训练被稠密、容易、波动小的奖励牵着走，最后卡在次优行为模式里。","importance":"它属于 post-train，但和预训练研究的接口很直接。现在很多推理、对齐、工具使用训练都在走多奖励路线，尤其是 correctness、format、length、safety、citation 这类混合监督。若聚合方式本身就会制造偏差，那么很多“RL 提升有限”或“模型学会投机格式”并不一定是奖励模型坏了，而可能是标量化设计有问题。这个判断对理解 post-train 信号质量、以及 pretrain 到 post-train 的能力转化都很有帮助。贡献量级上，它不是提出全新 RL 框架，而是指出一个常被忽略但很普遍的失败模式，并给出一个实现简单、可插入 PPO/GRPO/GDPO 的修正方案。","technical_approach":"方法核心是 Adaptive Multi-Reward Projection，简称 AMRP。它不改变底层 RL 优化器，而是替换固定加权求和这一步，把多维奖励到标量奖励的投影做成在线自适应。作者用三个信号来动态调节各奖励维度的权重。\n\n第一是 relative shortfall，用来衡量某个维度是否明显落后于其他维度。如果某个奖励长期偏低，说明策略在这方面欠账较多，AMRP会提高它的权重。第二是 reward volatility，衡量该维度近期是否不稳定。高波动意味着策略在该维度还没有收敛，继续给更高权重有助于避免过早把注意力转走。第三是 recent progress，衡量该维度最近是在进步、停滞还是退化。若某维度已接近饱和且稳定，权重就下调；若停滞或回退，则上调。\n\n这个设计针对的是固定权重的两个缺陷。对 profile aliasing，AMRP不能从根本上消除标量化的信息损失，但它通过时间变化的投影，减少“长期把不同 profile 当成同一类”的问题。对 shortcut lock-in，它通过提高落后维度的训练信号，迫使策略重新探索那些早期不容易优化、但最终更重要的奖励方向。和常见的 reward normalization 或手工调权不同，AMRP强调的是基于训练动态的闭环调节，而不是训练前拍脑袋设一组静态系数。","experiments":"实验覆盖三类多奖励场景：规则可验证的数学推理、带 citation evaluator 的 grounded generation、以及依赖 learned reward model 的开放式对齐。论文的结论是 AMRP 在三类设置下都带来更好的下游表现，其中数学推理和 citation-grounded generation 的提升更明显，在奖励相关性更高的开放式对齐任务上也有稳定增益。\n\n从机制验证看，作者不仅报最终任务分数，也分析 reward-profile dynamics，证明固定加权会让训练早期被格式、长度这类更稠密的奖励主导，而 correctness 等难维度长期上不去；AMRP则能把权重逐步转向落后维度，缓解这种塌缩。另一个有用点是兼容性实验：AMRP可以和 PPO、GRPO、GDPO 组合使用，说明它更像一个 reward aggregation 层的改件，而不是绑定某个特定 RL 算法。摘要里没有给出精确数字，全文主张的重点也不是单一 benchmark 上的大幅绝对提升，而是不同任务、不同优化器下都能观察到一致方向的改善，以及更健康的多维奖励演化轨迹。","reading_guide":"泛读建议先看 Introduction 和 Aggregation-Induced Reward Hacking 这一节。这里把问题定义得比较清楚，尤其 profile aliasing 与 shortcut lock-in 的区分值得记住。然后看 AMRP 方法公式，重点理解三个动态信号各自解决什么问题，以及权重更新是如何嵌入 PPO/GRPO 流程的。实验部分优先看数学推理案例和 reward dynamics 图，因为这类任务最容易暴露 correctness 被格式奖励压制的问题。若时间有限，30 分钟内看完问题定义、方法主公式、主结果表和一两张动态曲线图，就足够判断这篇工作的实用性。若要复现，需再细看超参数鲁棒性和不同 reward scale 下的处理方式。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"The paper studies a structural issue in multi-reward RL for LLM post-training: when several reward dimensions are collapsed into a single scalar with fixed weights, the policy loses dimension-wise learning signals and tends to optimize whichever rewards are easiest, densest, or most stable early in training. The authors call this aggregation-induced reward hacking. They decompose it into two mechanisms: profile aliasing, where qualitatively different reward vectors map to the same scalar value, and shortcut lock-in, where optimization gets trapped in superficially good but imbalanced reward profiles.","importance":"This is a post-training paper, but it is highly relevant to foundation-model research because many current alignment and reasoning pipelines rely on mixed reward signals: correctness, formatting, length, citation quality, safety, and learned preference scores. If the scalarization step itself is biased, then some observed RL failures may come from reward aggregation rather than from poor reward models or weak optimizers. That is useful for understanding the pretrain-to-posttrain interface, especially when trying to preserve hard capabilities while adding behavioral constraints. The contribution is not a new RL paradigm, but a clear diagnosis of a common failure mode plus a lightweight fix that can sit on top of PPO-, GRPO-, or GDPO-style training.","technical_approach":"$59","experiments":"$5a","reading_guide":"Start with the introduction and the section formalizing aggregation-induced reward hacking. Then read the AMRP method carefully, focusing on what each of the three signals is meant to detect and how the adaptive weights are injected into standard RL training. In the experiments, prioritize the mathematical reasoning setup and the reward-dynamics plots, since that setting makes the failure mode easiest to see. A 30-minute skim is enough to understand the main idea, the method, and the practical takeaway. c","starred":false,"priority":"p2","reading_tier":"skim","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"","category_id":"post_train","cover_url":"/covers/2609_00624.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.CL"],"summary":{"core_problem":"这篇工作处理的是 inference-time alignment 里的一个结构性问题：弱监督器在大多数解码位置上其实并不确定，但现有方法仍要求它对每个 token 持续介入。全文的关键观察不是“弱模型不如强模型”这么简单，而是监督置信度与介入频率之间存在系统性错配。作者用 token-level entropy 分析发现，弱监督器在通用任务和安全任务上都长期处于高熵状态，且这种不确定性不是偶发，而是沿着整段生成持续存在。于是，密集干预会把低置信度的偏置持续注入到基座模型的正常推理里，既损伤效用，也未必真正提升安全性。论文要解决的核心问题，就是如何在不重新训练模型的前提下，只在“监督器确实知道自己在做什么”的位置上进行稀疏、可信的对齐干预。","importance":"对预训练研究本身，它不是直接改 pretrain recipe 的工作，但对 pretrain 与 post-train 接口有现实意义。第一，它提醒人们：冻结基座模型后再靠小监督器做解码期控制，效果上限受监督器校准质量和 token-level uncertainty 强烈约束，这会反过来影响人们如何看待“训练时对齐能否被推理时对齐替代”。第二，它把 alignment 从“始终施加外力”改成“有条件地信任基座模型”，这个思路对后续 reward-guided decoding、verifier-guided generation、弱到强监督都成立。第三，论文给出的收益不只是安全指标，而是同时减少约一半干预开销并提升偏好率，说明在这类系统里，少干预有时比多干预更好，关键在于门控是否抓住了真正高价值的位置。","technical_approach":"方法核心是 TUSA，形式上是在基座模型 πθ 和弱监督器 πϕ 之间加入一个 training-free 的 gate，而不是像 MARA 一类 dense intervention 方法那样每步都融合两个分布。作者先把 inference-time alignment 写成条件控制问题：基座模型先给出候选 token，随后由 gate 决定这一时刻是否允许监督器介入。gate 的判定依据是一个 Joint Necessity Score，来自两个信号。\n\n第一部分是监督器置信度，文中主要用 token distribution entropy 来刻画。熵低意味着监督器分布更尖锐，说明它对当前 token 的判断更明确。第二部分是语义显著性，作者不希望把干预预算浪费在 function words、格式 token 或语义负载很低的位置上，因此引入 saliency 来衡量当前候选 token 是否值得管。只有当“监督器足够确定”且“这个 token 足够重要”同时成立时，arbiter 才打开 gate。\n\n从机制上看，这比单纯 entropy threshold 更稳。只看置信度会让模型在一些低价值 token 上也频繁介入；只看 saliency 又会在监督器其实没把握时强行改写生成。TUSA 把两者合成动态边界，等于把 alignment 变成 trust-based arbitration：默认信任强基座模型，只有在弱监督器既有把握又有必要时才接管。\n\n论文强调这是 training-free 框架，重点不在训练一个新 reward model 或新 policy，而在于重写解码时的控制逻辑。和 dense guidance 的区别有三点：一是控制粒度从“全程覆盖”变成“事件触发”；二是干预依据从“固定规则或恒定融合”变成“基于 uncertainty 的条件授权”；三是目标从“尽可能多地施加对齐信号”变成“避免低置信度噪声破坏原始推理”。如果把它放到更大的方法谱系里，它更接近 selective prediction / abstention 的思想，而不是传统意义上的更强监督。","experiments":"实验主要围绕两个问题展开：弱监督器是否真的长期高熵，以及稀疏干预是否优于密集干预。作者在 Mistral 和 Llama 系列基座上做评测，覆盖通用偏好基准和安全基准。全文最明确的结果有三类。\n\n第一，现象验证。Figure 2 给出监督器 entropy profile：无论是一般任务还是安全任务，弱监督器在大量 token 上的熵都接近理论上限，而且这种高熵会沿生成过程持续，而不是只出现在开头几步。这一结果支撑了“Confidence–Intervention Mismatch”不是个别样本现象。\n\n第二，主结果。相对 dense baseline，TUSA 将 intervention overhead 降低约 50%，同时在通用 benchmark 上 Preference Rate 最多提升 12.0%，在安全 benchmark 上最多提升 15.6%。这说明性能提升不是靠更频繁地改写输出，而是靠更少但更准的介入。\n\n第三，机制层分析。论文展示了稀疏门控能更好保留基座模型原本正确的 reasoning path，尤其是在监督器不确定但基座模型其实已经走在正确轨道上时，dense 方法容易把答案带偏。消融上，去掉 uncertainty 或去掉 semantic saliency 都会削弱效果，说明两部分信号是互补的，而不是任一项都可替代。虽然摘要里没有给出所有具体表格数字，但从全文叙述看，作者比较关注 utility-safety trade-off：TUSA 的收益来自减少无意义覆盖，而不是单纯提高安全拒答率。","reading_guide":"这篇更适合带着“解码期控制何时该 abstain”这个问题去读。先看 Introduction 和 Figure 1、Figure 2，抓住作者真正提出的问题：不是怎么让弱监督器更强，而是怎么避免它在不确定时添乱。然后看方法部分里 gate 的定义、Joint Necessity Score 的构成，以及为什么需要 uncertainty 和 saliency 两个条件同时成立。这里要特别留意作者如何把 dense aligned policy 改写成 conditional intervention policy，这一步是全文最有信息量的地方。\n\n实验部分建议重点看三类图表：一类是 entropy 分布和沿时间步的变化；一类是 intervention rate 与 preference/safety 指标的关系；一类是消融，尤其是只用 entropy、只用 saliency、以及不同阈值设置的比较。若只打算泛读，30 分钟左右足够抓住主线。若想深挖，可以进一步想两个问题：一，entropy 是否真能代表“监督器在该 token 上有资格介入”；二，这种 gate 是否可以和 verifier、RM、speculative decoding 或多监督器仲裁结合。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"The paper targets a structural flaw in inference-time alignment with weak supervisors. Existing methods assume a lightweight specialist can inspect and potentially steer every decoding step of a much stronger base model. The full paper argues this assumption is wrong at the token level: the specialist is highly uncertain for most positions, and that uncertainty often persists throughout the trajectory rather than appearing only at a few difficult steps. Dense intervention therefore creates a confidence–intervention mismatch: low-confidence guidance is injected exactly where the base model may already be reasoning correctly. The core problem is how to turn inference-time alignment into selective intervention, so the supervisor acts only when it is both confident and genuinely needed.","importance":"This is not a pretraining paper in the narrow sense, but it matters for the pretrain–posttrain boundary. It sharpens the limits of replacing training-time alignment with decoding-time control: once the base model is frozen, alignment quality depends heavily on the calibration and local competence of the auxiliary controller. It also introduces a useful design principle for weak-to-strong guidance, verifier-guided decoding, and reward-guided generation: trust the base model by default, and intervene only under evidence of local reliability. The practical significance is that the method reduces intervention cost by about half while improving both preference and safety metrics, which suggests that in alignment-by-decoding systems, better gating can matter more than stronger continuous control.","technical_approach":"$5b","experiments":"$5c","reading_guide":"$5d","authors_note":""},"recommendation":{"zh":"这篇的价值在诊断不是方法名：弱监督 token 级高熵时，dense guidance 往往比不管更坏；先看分析图，再决定要不要把 sparse gating 接进你们的 verifier 解码。","en":"The diagnosis matters more than the method name: when token-level supervision is high-entropy, dense guidance can be worse than doing nothing; read the analysis first, then decide whether sparse gating belongs in your verifier-guided decoding stack."},"metadata":{"institutions":["Harbin Institute of Technology","Huawei Technologies","Beijing Academy of Artificial Intelligence","Affiliation:Harbin Institute of Technology","Affiliation:Huawei Technologies Co., Ltd","Affiliation:Beijing Academy of Artificial Intelligence"],"authors":["Zeen Zhu","Zhuo Li","Weiyang Guo","Liye Zhao","Haibing Di","Yequan Wang","Jing Li"],"notable_authors":[],"tags":["inference-time-alignment","uncertainty","sparse-intervention","token-level-supervision","verifier-guidance"],"links":{"arxiv":"https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00624","pdf":"https://arxiv.org/pdf/2609.00624","github":null,"project":null,"source_url":null,"article":null}}},{"id":"2609.01422","title":"From Rollouts to Recipes: Self-Contained Post-Training for LLMs","abstract":"Post-training large language models usually applies a single training recipe to all samples, even though the model's own rollouts reveal different sample-level learning states. Without that diagnosis, later rounds may generate harder questions without targeting persistent reasoning weaknesses.","importance":"The useful signal for pretraining-oriented work is the data loop: failures from the current model are converted into structured training directions for the next round. Its main value is showing how to use failure trajectories as a curriculum source without external task corpora or human examples.","technical_approach":"DiagEvo introduces a diagnosis-guided self-play loop with hierarchical error memory. A challenger generates questions and a solver trains on filtered question–pseudo-label pairs; the two are updated in alternating rounds. A lightweight diagnostician compares failed solver trajectories with trajectories that agree with the pseudo-label, extracts transferable error causes, and stores them in memory. Each cause is tracked by frequency and state. Active causes are used as direct generation targets, while Mastered causes are no longer the main focus once the solver reaches high self-consistency on related questions. The challenger combines targeted generation, free exploration, and cross-state stitching. Before training the solver, double-confidence filtering removes samples where the top two candidate answers receive similar vote shares, since those are likely to carry unstable pseudo-labels.","experiments":"The evaluation uses Qwen3-4B, Qwen3-8B, and OctoThinker-8B on mathematical reasoning tasks. Recent work therefore compensates around the training with denser rewards, SFT priors, skill libraries, curated memory, or multi-agent orchestration. We argue the ceiling is an artifact of two failures of common practice. This paper aims to prove that this ceiling is an artifact of distorted training trajectory distributions, not an inherent limitation of outcome-only RL.","importance":"This paper challenges the consensus that outcome-only RL for long-horizon agent tasks requires complex auxiliary mechanisms. It reveals that hard tasks, previously deemed 'harmful', are actually starved of gradient signals due to under-exploration. By correcting the training distribution distortion, it demonstrates that pure interactive RL can achieve SOTA performance on small models, offering a minimalist and highly effective new paradigm for agent post-training.","technical_approach":"Introduces the CANOPY (Coverage-ANchored On-PolicY RL) protocol to address two core failure mechanisms:\n1. Resolving Signal Starvation: Increases same-task rollout group sizes and removes per-turn generation caps to ensure hard tasks produce mixed success/failure trajectories, thereby providing effective gradient signals.\n2. Resolving Policy Drift: Repeated training on a limited task pool degrades the policy. CANOPY uses a light KL anchor, strictly on-policy updates, and a token-level loss over action tokens only to maintain policy stability.\n3.
