checkpoint 的偶然梯度信号。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"The paper tackles stylistic bias in reward models, especially preferences for longer, more formatted, emoji-heavy, or list-like answers. The key constraint is that it does not collect new preference data or hand-code a debiasing rule. Instead, it estimates how each training preference pair affects benchmark losses and uses those estimates to reweight the existing preference dataset before retraining the reward model.","importance":"This is directly relevant to the pretrain–posttrain interface because reward models define the optimization signal used in RLHF. If the reward model encodes style preferences rather than answer quality, policy optimization will amplify those artifacts. The useful contribution is a data-side method for shaping reward models: turn a behavioral specification, measured by benchmark losses, into sample weights on the preference data. The result is not only an RM-Bench improvement; it is evidence that susceptibility-based data attribution can be applied at the scale of a 9B reward model, with some transfer of the learned weights across model sizes and architectures.","technical_approach":"The method is called patterning. A Gemma 2 9B Instruct reward model is first trained on Skywork-Reward-Preference v0.2. Around the trained checkpoint, the model parameters are treated through a posterior view, and selected observables are defined as benchmark losses, such as losses on RM-Bench difficulty splits or domain-by-split cells. For each preference pair, the method estimates its susceptibility: how a small change in that pair’s training weight changes the posterior expectation of the observables. These susceptibilities are then converted into a reweighting of the original preference pairs so that the target losses move in the desired direction. The reward model is retrained on the same data with the new weights. Compared with manual data curation, the target is specified by measurable losses rather than intuition. Compared with reward-model steering, the intervention changes the effective training distribution rather than the model’s activations or reward head at inference time.","experiments":"The main experiments use Gemma 2 9B Instruct trained on Skywork-Reward-Preference v0.2. With a three-observable target based on RM-Bench Easy, Normal, and Hard losses, the method improves RM-Bench Hard by +14.2±1.2 percentage points, preserves overall RM-Bench accuracy at -0.4±0.4 points, and costs -1.4±0.4 points on RewardBench 2. A finer fifteen-observable target, splitting five RM-Bench domains by three difficulty levels, improves Hard by +10.2±1.2 points and overall RM-Bench by +1.7±0.4 points, with a larger RewardBench 2 cost of -4.3±0.5 points. The closest published comparator cited is SteerRM, whose largest reported Hard-split gain is +13.2 points and whose main configuration gives +10.3 points. The paper also shows that the learned weights identify a small class of preference pairs responsible for a collapse in the safety-response domain, verified by ablation. Weight transfer is strong from Gemma 2 9B to Gemma 2 2B and 27B, and moderate to Llama 3.1 8B.","reading_guide":"Skim carefully in about 35–45 minutes. Read Sections 2.1, 2.4, and 3 to understand the susceptibility definition and how it differs from standard influence-function use: the object of interest is the change in posterior expected benchmark observables under sample reweighting. Then read Sections 4.1 and 4.2 for the two target designs. The main point is the trade-off between maximizing Hard-split debiasing and preserving broader reward-model performance. Section 4.1.2 is worth reading because it shows how the sample weights become a data-diagnostic tool. Section 4.3 is also important: transferability of weights suggests that the method is capturing dataset-level bias rather than only checkpoint-specific noise.","authors_note":""},"recommendation":{"zh":"针对 Reward Model 的 length/style bias 提出了一种基于 susceptibility 的样本重加权方法，在 RM-Bench Hard 上提升显著且权重可跨 scale 迁移，做 RLHF/RM 训练的同学值得关注其 reweighting 机制。","en":"Proposes a susceptibility-based sample reweighting method to address length/style bias in Reward Models. Reinforcement learning (RL) based methods adapt update strength using model-generated rollouts, but often require substantially more sampling and can be unstable on hard tasks. We propose \\textbf{Online Self-Weighted Fine-Tuning (OSW-FT)}, a simple method that augments SFT with online, trajectory-level weighting. For each ","starred":false,"priority":"p2","reading_tier":"skim","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"","category_id":"post_train","cover_url":"/covers/2609_00734.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.LG"],"summary":{"core_problem":"这篇工作处理的是一个很具体、也很常见的后训练问题：在可验证推理任务上，标准 SFT 对所有 expert trajectory 一视同仁，但模型在训练过程中对不同题目的掌握程度并不一样。已经会做的题继续给满权重，会浪费更新预算；完全不会做的题如果只靠 RL，又常常因为采不到正确轨迹而没有稳定学习信号。论文要解决的，就是如何在不放弃 expert supervision 稳定性的前提下，引入一点在线、自适应的难度感知，让更新强度随模型当前能力变化，而不是固定不变。","importance":"它的意义不在于替代 RL，而在于补上 SFT 和 RL 之间一块长期缺位的区域。对预训练/后训练接口来说，这类方法很有代表性：训练方向仍由高质量数据决定，在线信号只负责调节梯度幅度，而不是直接决定学什么。这样做的贡献主要有三点。第一，它把“样本权重应随模型状态变化”这件事写成了一个很干净的训练目标，而不是经验性 curriculum。第二，它给出一个和 control variate / advantage 相关的梯度解释，说明这种 weighting 不是拍脑袋 heuristic。第三，在 rollout 预算很紧时，它提供了比 RL 更便宜、比静态 SFT 更自适应的中间方案。对做 reasoning post-train、continual post-train、以及 pretrain 后期 annealing/cooldown 的人，这个思路都有参考价值。","technical_approach":"方法很简单：对每个训练 query q，不直接把 expert answer o* 以统一权重做 NLL，而是先让当前模型做 K 次 inference-only rollouts，估计该题当前通过率 p̂(q)。然后把标准 SFT loss 乘上一个 trajectory-level 权重 (1 - p̂(q))。也就是说，模型越容易做对，这条 expert trajectory 的梯度就越小；模型越做不对，更新就越强。\n\n关键点有几个。第一，它不对模型生成的 rollout 本身做优化，也不把 rollout token 混进训练目标，训练方向始终锚定在 expert trajectory 上。这和 PPO/GRPO 一类方法不同，后者既依赖 rollout 估计优势，也直接在 rollout 上更新，因此更容易受采样质量影响。第二，论文把 binary-verifiable setting 下的目标写成期望正确率最大化，并用 score-function estimator 加 query-dependent control variate 来分析方差。推导里会得到一个与成功率相关的最优基线项，进而说明为什么“按 1-p(q) 缩放 expert log-prob 梯度”是合理的 surrogate。第三，作者强调有限 K 下的 Monte Carlo 估计对其 surrogate update 是无偏的；也就是说，采样噪声只影响方差，不引入额外偏差。第四，他们还比较了连续权重和硬阈值权重，结论是连续 success-rate weighting 更平滑，也更符合能力逐步变化的训练动态。\n\n从方法定位看，OSW-FT 不是 reward-weighted regression，也不是 offline RL。它更像是 online difficulty-aware SFT：在线部分只估计“这题现在值不值得继续学”，离线部分仍然提供“应该学成什么样”的目标。","experiments":"实验覆盖 Qwen3 系列 0.6B 到 4B 模型，任务是 AIME、AMC、MATH-500、GPQA-Diamond 这类带二值可验证信号的推理评测。论文主结论比较克制：OSW-FT 在 small-to-medium scale 上相对标准 SFT 是稳定增益，但并不声称全面强于 GRPO。\n\n从全文描述看，结果主要支持四件事。第一，和标准 SFT 相比，OSW-FT 在多个模型尺度上都能提升 reasoning 表现，说明“在线估计当前成功率，再调节 expert loss”确实比固定权重更有效。第二，在 rollout budget 受限时，它的 compute-performance trade-off 往往更合适，因为每个样本只需要少量 inference-only rollouts，不需要像 RL 那样维护完整的 rollout optimization pipeline。第三，和 GRPO 的比较是分场景的：有些模型和指标上 OSW-FT 更占优，尤其是在有限 rollout 设置下；但也有配置里 GRPO 仍更强，说明它不是 RL 的普适替代。第四，ablation 支持方法设计本身：连续 success-rate weighting 优于 hard-threshold、随机权重等替代方案，说明收益不是来自“随便加点 sample reweighting”，而是来自与当前能力匹配的连续在线信号。\n\n摘要截断后具体表格数字没有全部给出，但从正文表述可以确认：作者重点强调的是跨 0.6B–4B 的一致趋势、有限 rollout 下的性价比，以及对 binary-verifiable reasoning 的适配性，而不是追求单点最高分。","reading_guide":"如果只花一小时，优先看三部分。\n\n第一看方法定义和推导部分，尤其是把 expected correctness、score-function estimator、control variate 连起来的那几页。重点不是记公式，而是看清楚：为什么最终更新仍然是 expert NLL，只是前面多了一个 online scalar weight；以及这个 weight 为什么自然对应“当前还没学会的程度”。\n\n第二看和 GRPO/SFT 的对比实验。要注意作者的措辞很谨慎：他们没有把 OSW-FT包装成 RL 替代品，而是限定在 limited-rollout、binary-verifiable reasoning 这个区间里。读这里时建议重点看不同模型规模下收益是否一致、在哪些 benchmark 上更明显、以及 rollout 数 K 变化时性能和成本怎么变。\n\n第三看 ablation。连续权重、硬阈值、随机权重、可能还有不同 K 的比较，这些最能说明方法到底吃的是哪一部分增益。如果要深挖，可以进一步想两个问题：一是 1-p̂(q) 是否是唯一合理形式，还是可以推广到更一般的 monotone weighting；二是当验证信号不是严格二值、而是程序化部分评分或 verifier score 时，这个框架如何扩展。\n\n泛读的话，预计 35–50 分钟能抓住主线；若要认真看理论细节和 surrogate objective 的边界条件，建议再加 30 分钟。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"The paper targets a narrow but important gap in post-training for verifiable reasoning. Standard SFT gives every expert demonstration the same weight, even though the model’s competence varies widely across queries and changes during training. RL-based methods adapt update strength using model rollouts, but they are expensive and can become brittle when correct trajectories are rare. The core problem here is how to keep the stable learning direction of expert supervision while making the update magnitude depend on what the model currently knows.","importance":"Its value is not that it replaces RL. The contribution is a cleaner middle ground between static SFT and rollout-optimized RL. For pretrain-to-posttrain research, this is a useful pattern: let curated data define the target behavior, and use online signals only to modulate how hard each example should push the model. That matters for reasoning post-training, continual post-training, and even late-stage annealing where sample utility becomes highly non-uniform. The paper also gives a principled gradient-level interpretation through control variates, which makes the weighting scheme more than a heuristic curriculum.","technical_approach":"$5e","experiments":"$5f","reading_guide":"Start with the method section and the gradient derivation. The key thing to verify is that the optimization direction remains the expert NLL gradient; only the magnitude changes through an online scalar. Then read the control-variate argument carefully enough to understand why success-rate weighting is tied to variance reduction rather than being an arbitrary curriculum trick.\n\nNext, go to the SFT vs GRPO comparison. Pay attention to the scope conditions. The paper is careful to position OSW-FT for binary-verifiable reasoning with limited rollout budget, not as a general RL replacement. The most informative plots are the ones that vary model size and rollout count K.\n\nFinally, read the ablations. P(x≻y)/P(y≻x) = exp(u(x)-u(y))，这与 MH 接受率所需的未归一化密度比同构。难点在于：MH 需要“精确的比值”，而实际只能通过对同一对 (x,y) 反复询问 judge 得到随机投票样本。论文先给出不可能性结果：任何固定次数比较、再 plug-in 估计胜率并代入 MH 的做法，都无法保证实现 oracle 接受规则（误差会系统性扭曲平稳分布）。随后提出 kernel；直觉上是用“随机停止/序贯检验式”的接受判定，把比较噪声吸收到接受率随机性里，而不是把噪声当作需要被消除的估计误差。","experiments":"给定的全文片段未包含完整实验表格与具体数值细节（例如比较次数、接受率、有效样本量、与基线的定量差距）。从引言可确认作者在分子设计（SMILES）等条件生成场景展示 Most existing methods optimize only outcome rewards, so the agent gets supervision from whether the final answer is correct, but not from whether intermediate search and evidence-gathering steps were actually useful. In search-heavy settings, this is a serious bottleneck: a successful final answer does not mean every retrieval was necessary, and a failed answer does not mean all earlier steps were useless. The paper’s core idea is to convert raw intermediate observations into explicit facts, estimate the posterior utility of those facts, and use that estimate to provide dense process-level rewards.","importance":"This is not a foundation-model pretraining paper, but it is relevant to post-training for reasoning and tool use. The main value for a pretraining-oriented reader is methodological. First, it shows one concrete way to decompose sparse outcome supervision into structured intermediate supervision without training a separate reward model. Second, it replaces long unstructured interaction history with an explicit fact store, which is a more favorable state representation for long-horizon reasoning and credit assignment. That design choice is interesting beyond agents: it suggests that some forms of reasoning and tool-use training may benefit from structured latent state rather than forcing everything back into a flat token stream. calibration in Deep Research agents. These agents retrieve evidence over multiple rounds and then produce an answer, but their final verbalized confidence is often overconfident and poorly aligned with answer correctness.","importance":"The work is more relevant to post-training and agent reliability than to core pretraining. Its useful signal is that uncertainty in multi-step retrieval agents is better exposed before answer generation than after it. Evidence Confidence, elicited after the final retrieval step, is a stronger calibration signal than Answer Confidence, elicited after the final answer. This matters for RL-trained agents because reward design can easily teach models to sound confident without making confidence track correctness.","technical_approach":"The authors augment a Search-R1-style Deep Research pipeline with confidence queries after each retrieval step, using <confidence>X</confidence>, while keeping a final answer confidence tag, <final-confidence>X</final-confidence>. The confidence after the final retrieval is called Evidence Confidence, and the confidence after answer generation is called Answer Confidence. Both are conditioned on nearly the same accumulated evidence, but they occur at different positions in the sequence. The analysis suggests that E-Conf is better calibrated and carries more discriminative internal representations, while A-Conf is heavily shaped by E-Conf rather than independently reflecting correctness. DualStake trains both signals with a confidence-dependent stake reward: high confidence increases reward for correct answers and increases penalty for wrong answers. A margin-clipping mechanism bounds the effective confidence range so that calibration optimization does not destabilize answer accuracy.","experiments":"Experiments are run with qwen2.5-7B, qwen2.5-7B-Instruct, and qwen3-4B across eight QA benchmarks. Before calibration-specific training, E-Conf generally has lower ECE than A-Conf, although A-Conf can show slightly higher AUC in some settings. The mechanistic analysis uses 1,024 reasoning traces per dataset from Qwen2.5-7B-Instruct on 2WikiMultiHopQA and HotpotQA, extracting logits and hidden states at the two confidence positions. One concrete finding is that A-Conf logits barely separate correct and incorrect cases: the probability of token “9” is about 0.57 for both groups, and token “1” is about 0.35 for both groups. For hidden-state probing, states across 28 layers are reduced from 3586 to 64 dimensions with PCA and evaluated with 5-fold logistic regression for correctness prediction. DualStake improves calibration while preserving answer accuracy.","reading_guide":"A 20–30 minute skim is enough for most pretraining researchers. Existing behavioral control methods typically intervene directly in activation or feature space, but such approaches can be sensitive to outliers, distributional shifts, noise, and other local perturbations. Motivated by Topological Data Analysis (TDA), which captures global rather than purely local structure, we propose Topological Steering, a new framework for steerin","starred":false,"priority":"p3","reading_tier":"skim","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"","category_id":"post_train","cover_url":"/covers/2609_00597.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.LG"],"summary":{"core_problem":"这项工作要解决的是 activation steering 对局部几何假设过强的问题。常见方法用均值差、线性方向或低维子空间来表示某种行为，例如拒答、顺从、诚实或安全倾向。但这些行为在 LLM 残差流里可能不是单一方向，而是由多个语义场景、提示类型和安全机制共同形成的多簇结构。单个全局 steering vector 容易把信号平均掉，也容易受离群点和分布偏移影响。","importance":"对预训练研究的直接贡献不在训练 recipe，而在表示分析和 inference-time control。它把 TDA 引入 LLM 激活空间，提供了一种观察行为表征“形状”的工具，而不是只看均值、方差或线性可分性。对基座研究员有两个启发：第一，行为特征可能是多模态、分层、非线性的，预训练和对齐阶段形成的内部机制不一定能被单一探针方向概括；第二，后训练安全控制可以从数据几何转向结构化表征分析，但计算成本和可扩展性仍是限制。","technical_approach":"方法从正负对比提示集开始，例如拒答诱导提示和顺从诱导提示。模型前向后抽取指定层、指定 token 位置的 residual-stream activation，把每一类行为的激活看成点云。然后做 joint dimensionality reduction，保证两类点云在同一低维空间里比较。接着用 Vietoris–Rips filtration 计算 persistent homology，并生成 persistence diagrams。persistent homology 会随着距离尺度变化跟踪连通分量、环以及更高维拓扑特征的出现和消失；持续时间长的特征通常被视为更稳定的结构信号。Topological Steering 不直接用正负样本均值差，而是找出目标行为点云中既稳定又具有行为特异性的拓扑结构，再回到这些结构对应的激活子集上构造局部 contrastive directions，最后聚合成 steering vector，在生成时注入残差流。它和 CAA 一类方法的主要区别是，方向不是由全体样本平均得到，而是由拓扑筛出的局部子结构得到。","experiments":"实验目标是验证该方法能在多个模型家族和不同规模上稳定改变模型行为。文中定位比较克制：Topological Steering 相比 unsteered baseline 能产生可测的行为变化，但当前实现并不声称超过高度优化的几何 steering 方法。主要收益是结构可解释性和对异质激活结构的利用。给出的全文片段没有包含具体模型名称、层选择、数据集大小、评价指标数值和消融表格，因此不适合补写精确数字。需要重点确认完整实验部分是否比较了 mean-difference steering、contrastive activation addition、rotational/nonlinear steering，以及拓扑筛选、降维方式、持久性阈值、层位置和 steering strength 的消融。","reading_guide":"建议泛读，预计 25–35 分钟。先看 Introduction 里对 activation steering 局限的定义，确认它要替代的是“全局线性方向”这一假设，而不是整个对齐训练流程。然后看方法部分的 pipeline：激活抽取、joint dimensionality reduction、Vietoris–Rips persistence diagrams、topological feature selection、localized direction aggregation。实验部分重点看三类内容：与 CAA/mean-difference 的对照，拓扑结构筛选是否真的带来鲁棒性，steering 后是否牺牲通用能力或产生副作用。若有图，优先看 persistence diagram 和被选中激活子集的可视化，它们决定这个方法是否只是换一种方式构造向量，还是确实提供了新的结构诊断信号。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"The paper addresses a limitation of standard activation steering: many methods assume that a behavior can be represented by a single global direction, a mean contrast, or a simple low-dimensional subspace. Behaviors such as refusal, compliance, honesty, or safety may instead occupy several heterogeneous regions in the residual stream. A global steering vector can average out the relevant signal, while local geometric interventions can be brittle under outliers, noise, and distribution shift.","importance":"The contribution is more relevant to representation analysis and inference-time control than to pretraining recipes. Its useful signal for foundation-model research is the shift from local geometry to global structure in activation space. It suggests that aligned behaviors may be organized as multi-component, nonlinear activation structures, not necessarily as clean linear probes. This matters for understanding what pretraining and post-training create inside the model, and for designing more inspectable control mechanisms at the pretrain–posttrain boundary.","technical_approach":"The method starts from contrastive prompt sets, such as prompts that elicit refusal and prompts that elicit compliance. It extracts residual-stream activations at a chosen layer and token position, treats them as point clouds, and applies joint dimensionality reduction so that the two behavioral regimes can be compared in a shared space. It then computes Vietoris–Rips persistent homology and represents the resulting multiscale structures as persistence diagrams. Long-lived topological features are used as robust structural signals. Instead of building a direction from the mean difference between all positive and negative activations, Topological Steering identifies persistent, behavior-specific substructures in the target activation cloud, maps them back to coherent activation subsets, constructs localized contrastive directions, aggregates them, and injects the resulting steering vector into the residual stream during generation.","experiments":"The experiments are framed as empirical validation that topology-derived interventions can consistently change model behavior across model families and sizes. The authors explicitly do not position the current implementation as outperforming highly optimized geometric steering methods; the trade-off is lower emphasis on raw steering efficacy and higher emphasis on structured, inspectable intervention design. The provided full-text excerpt does not expose exact model names, dataset sizes, metrics, or ablation numbers, so precise claims should be checked in the experimental tables. The important checks are comparisons against mean-difference or CAA-style steering, sensitivity to dimensionality reduction and persistence thresholds, layer and token-position choices, and side effects on unrelated behaviors.","reading_guide":"Skim in about 25–35 minutes. Start with the motivation in the introduction, especially the claim that behavioral activations can be multimodal and not well captured by a single vector. 仍接近 0，MMLU accuracy 从 0.371 降到 0.279。论文还通过 attention endpoint statistics 和结构性构造说明，模型可以学到尖锐且互不重叠的路由，从而最大化 ICS，却不把注意力放到答案相关 token 上。补充实验包括污染排查、完整训练轨迹、layer ablation，以及 B-ICS 和 pretrained anchoring attention 推到 disjoint one-hot，却不带来任务行为收益。若关心继续预训练中的能力保持，建议补看 B-ICS 与 pretrained anchoring，因为它们更接近可用的正则化方案。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"The paper tests whether attention-level sensitivity to demonstrations is a reliable proxy for preserving in-context learning after fine-tuning. The answer is no: an attention proxy can be optimized almost to its geometric ceiling while the behavioral ICL gap remains near zero.","importance":"The result matters for pretraining and continued-training research because attention diagnostics are often treated as cheap, differentiable indicators of preserved mechanisms. This work shows that once such a diagnostic becomes an optimization target, it can be gamed by the model. For continual pretraining, instruction tuning, distillation, and mechanism-preserving regularization, the key implication is that attention movement must be validated against behavior, not used alone.","technical_approach":"The paper defines a paired diagnostic setup using matched and mismatched demonstration prefixes. In-Context Sensitivity, or ICS, is the average distance between last-token attention rows under the matched prefix and a label-permuted mismatched prefix, averaged over samples, heads, and a mid-layer band. The main Llama-2-7B experiments use layers 8 through 16. Since each attention row is a probability distribution, ICS is geometrically bounded; it reaches the ceiling of about 1.414 only when the two attention rows are one-hot on disjoint token positions. The behavioral counterpart is ICL-GAP, the held-out accuracy difference between matched and mismatched prefixes. The stress test fine-tunes four model variants from the same checkpoint: attention-frozen, MLP-frozen, fully fine-tuned, and F_ICS-Max, which adds an ICS-maximizing term to the cross-entropy objective.","experiments":"All main runs use Llama-2-7B and train for 5,000 steps on a fixed instruction-tuning mixture. The unregularized arms stay close to the pretrained ICS value of 0.516. F_ICS-Max drives ICS to 1.413, within roughly 0.5% of the theoretical ceiling and about 2.74 times the initial attention divergence. The behavioral results move in the opposite direction: ICL-GAP on the held-out QA probe remains near zero, and MMLU accuracy drops from 0.371 to 0.279. Additional analyses using attention endpoint statistics and a constructive example show how sharp, disjoint routing can maximize the proxy without selecting answer-relevant tokens. The supplement reportedly includes contamination analysis, full training trajectories, layer ablations, and constructive variants such as behavior-gated B-ICS and pretrained anchoring.","reading_guide":"Skim in about 30–40 minutes, but read the metric definitions carefully. While prior work often attributes this failure to gradient conflict, we propose a fundamentally different Fisher-geometric explanation: safety Fisher is low-rank, and alignment makes the safety geometry flatter while preserving an output-routing pathway. modules,","starred":false,"priority":"p2","reading_tier":"skim","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"","category_id":"mechanism","cover_url":"/covers/2609_01455.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.CR","cs.AI"],"summary":{"core_problem":"论文研究一个很具体的问题：为什么已经对齐的 pretrain-posttrain 接口。它把安全对齐看成对预训练中已有表示的输出路由重写，而不是新知识注入。这个视角能解释两个现象：少量无害微调会让安全性崩掉，但少量安全样本或固定拒答前缀又能恢复一部分拒答。对持续预训练、领域自适应和安全保持都有启发：如果安全行为主要挂在晚层输出侧低秩子空间上，那么只控制数据是否无害不够，还要控制微调对 late MLP 和输出 routing 特征值整体变小，说明对齐把安全路由变得更平坦，但并没有删除有害内容的内部表示。无害微调后，变化高度局部化：晚层、输出侧 MLP 模块的 safety Fisher 重新变尖，尤其是 final-layer down_proj。作者进一步用 logit lens 和跨条件 activation patching 的安全保持更有参考意义。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"The paper asks why refusal behavior in aligned LLMs can collapse after fine-tuning on a very small set of benign examples. Its central claim is that the failure is not primarily driven by harmful data, outlier benign samples, or simple utility-safety gradient conflict. Refusal is implemented through a low-rank output-side routing pathway, and benign fine-tuning perturbs this pathway so that harmful internal representations are routed toward compliant answers instead of refusal.","importance":"The main relevance is at the interface between pretraining and post-training. The results support a view in which pretraining leaves rich harmful-content representations inside the model, while alignment mainly changes how these representations are mapped to outputs. This matters for domain adaptation, continual pretraining, and safety-preserving fine-tuning: filtering out harmful data is not sufficient if small updates can move late-layer routing modules in high-curvature safety directions. The work also gives a concrete reason why safety can be both fragile and recoverable.","technical_approach":"The analysis is built around Fisher geometry. The authors estimate and compare Fisher structure for safety behavior and utility behavior. Safety Fisher is more concentrated and lower-rank than utility Fisher, especially in the final layers. Alignment flattens the safety geometry by reducing safety-related eigenvalues, while preserving the internal representations needed for harmful-content recognition. After benign fine-tuning, the main geometric change is selective re-sharpening in late output-side MLP modules. The paper then connects this geometry to mechanisms using logit-lens analysis and cross-condition activation patching. These analyses indicate that safety-relevant activations remain available after fine-tuning, but late-layer computation controls whether the model emits refusal or compliance.","experiments":"The paper reports the fragility pattern across model families, alignment settings, datasets, and sample-selection strategies. Models with near-0% initial attack success rate can reach up to 85.7% ASR after only 100 benign fine-tuning examples, while utility degradation remains comparatively mild. Fisher measurements show top-1 concentration of 0.491 for safety versus 0.271 for utility on the baseline model; in the final layers the gap is 0.718 versus 0.223. Alignment reduces layer-wise safety top eigenvalues by roughly two orders of magnitude across most layers. After benign fine-tuning, final-layer down_proj sharpness rises by 11.2x for safety but only 1.2x for utility. Recovery experiments show that a small number of safety examples can restore refusal, and even a fixed refusal-style prefix can reduce ASR by about 50% without parameter updates. LoRA and ASAM help at small data scales but weaken as the amount of benign fine-tuning data grows.","reading_guide":"A useful skim takes about 30 to 40 minutes. training，但它提供了一个低成本诊断和编辑模型安全性的视角：如果安全信号已经存在于模型内部，适度放大相关组件可能比重新训练更便宜，也更容易分析副作用。需要注意的是，weight scaling 是针对已对齐模型的训练后干预，泛化到不同攻击类型、不同语言和更强自适应攻击时仍需谨慎。","technical_approach":"论文提出一个 detection-to-refusal circuit。第一类组件是 Harmful Detection Heads，主要位于早期到中间层，对有害输入更敏感。第二类是 Safety Neurons，位于中后层，承担在 residual stream 中调制和稳定安全信号的作用。第三类是 Refusal Heads，位于较后层，把上游安全信号转化为拒答相关 token 的生成倾向。方法上，作者先通过 attention head 和 scaling，对检测头、安全神经元和拒答头分别或联合放大，不引入额外训练目标，也不改变模型结构。联合 scaling 的效果显著强于单独 scaling，符合多阶段 circuit 协同的假设。","experiments":"论文在 6 个 LLM 上评估训练自由的 circuit-guided scaling。核心数字是：在 GCG 攻击下，平均安全率从 43.2% 提升到 refusal heads；第三，weight scaling 的实现细节和 utility trade-off。若关注可训练性和稳定性，可以进一步看 scaling 系数选择、不同组件单独放大的消融，以及是否存在过拒答或能力下降。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"The paper studies how refusal behavior is implemented inside LLMs and uses localized circuit components to improve safety under adversarial prompting without additional training or output-side filtering.","importance":"The work is relevant to the boundary between pretraining, alignment, and model editing. It frames refusal as a staged internal computation: harmful-intent detection, safety-signal propagation, and refusal-token generation. This does not replace RLHF or adversarial training, but it gives a low-cost way to diagnose and modify safety behavior in already aligned models. The main caveat is scope: circuit-guided scaling is a post-hoc intervention, and its robustness against adaptive attacks, multilingual jailbreaks, and distribution shifts needs more evidence.","technical_approach":"The proposed circuit has three roles. Harmful Detection Heads, mostly in early and middle layers, respond selectively to harmful inputs. Safety Neurons, typically in middle-to-late layers, modulate and stabilize safety-related signals in the residual stream. Refusal Heads, in later layers, convert those signals into a higher probability of safe or refusing responses. The paper identifies candidate heads and neurons using component-level attribution, then applies causal interventions such as selective zeroing and activation analysis. Suppressing upstream detection heads weakens downstream refusal-head activity, supporting a cross-layer dependency rather than a set of independent correlations. For intervention, the paper uses architecture-preserving weight scaling on the identified heads and neurons. Scaling each group helps; scaling detection heads, safety neurons, and refusal heads jointly produces larger gains, which is consistent with the staged-circuit picture.","experiments":"Across six LLMs, training-free circuit-guided scaling improves the average safety rate under GCG attacks from 43.2% to 69.7%, a 26.5-point absolute gain. Utility is mostly preserved, with an average accuracy drop of 1.7% across four task-oriented benchmarks. Ablations show that scaling detection heads, safety neurons, or refusal heads individually improves robustness, while joint scaling is stronger. Causal intervention experiments show that removing early detection heads or mid-to-late safety neurons reduces downstream refusal-head activity, linking the mechanistic story to the safety improvements.","reading_guide":"Skim in about 30–40 minutes. Read the component-identification procedure first, because the validity of the method depends on how the three circuit roles are defined. 三域；每个实例包含模型大概率在参数中记住的真实答案，以及上下文中提供的可置信但反事实替代答案。\n3) 评测过滤：先筛掉模型在无冲突时无法识别实体或无法回忆参数事实的样本，保证后续测到的是“冲突解决偏好”而不是“识别失败”。过滤后每个模型仍保留 1K+ 对。\n4) 机制分析主张：文本实体时，模型会通过注意力检测到上下文-记忆冲突，并抑制早层 MLP（引用 Geva et al. 2023 2）。差异在“本质属性类事实”（如名人职业）更明显。\n代表性数字：Gemma-3-12B 在 buildings-location 子集出现约 80% 的参数偏置差距（视觉实体相对文本实体）。\n模型差异：Gemma-3-27B 是少数在各子类上模态差距较小的模型；但差距不能用规模单调解释，因为更大的 Qwen/Ministral 仍存在（只是有所减弱）。Ministral-3-8B First, it predicts FLORES translation BLEU using source- and target-language monolingual competence measures, rather than language-pair identifiers. These measures include MultiBLiMP grammaticality accuracy, MultiBLiMP log-probability margins, and GlobalMMLU accuracy. Strong predictability without pair-specific interaction terms supports the view that translation reuses monolingual reading and generation machinery. Second, it looks for task-agnostic representations that are causally relevant for both monolingual tasks and translation. Third, it compares monolingual fine-tuning against parallel-data fine-tuning for adding translation capability in a new language. The shared language set includes Arabic, Czech, German, Greek, English, Persian, French, Hebrew, Hindi, Italian, Dutch, Polish, Portuguese, Romanian, Russian, Spanish, Turkish, and Ukrainian. The main empirical claims are that translation BLEU is largely predictable from monolingual competence, shared latent features matter causally for both translation and monolingual tasks, and monolingual fine-tuning recovers a substantial fraction of the gains from parallel fine-tuning. clean/corrupt 成对样本。然后在 Themis（Llama-3-8B judge）和 Prometheus（Mistral-7B judge）上做四类机制实验，包括 causal tracing、logit lens 词表投影和 attention head knockout。\n\n核心发现是 judge 模型内部存在较清晰的两阶段结构：较低层，主要是 attention 在做错误识别和信号路由；较高层，主要是 MLP 把这些信号整合成最终评分，分数在后期某个层位突然写入 residual stream。作者还区分了不同错误类型的注意力模式：可读性错误更依赖邻近句法上下文，充分性/忠实性类错误则更集中在被扰动 token 附近。另一个有意思的点是，fine-tuned judge 并不是从零学出一套评测电路，而是在 base model 已有的通用路由结构上做了两处改造：抑制低层 MLP 对最后位置的贡献，以及把“评分结晶”的层位提前。","experiments":"关键结果集中在机制层面。作者发现两种 judge 都表现出一致的阶段分工：15 层以下更偏向识别和路由，25 到 26 层附近出现明显的评分结晶。Themis 倾向于在顶层一次性写入评分；Prometheus 则在中后层逐步写入，并在最后一层做反向修正。对比 base Llama-3-8B 后，作者发现基础模型已经有类似的 routing 结构和晚层结晶特征，judge fine-tuning 主要是在这个底座上重塑信息流，而不是新建能力。实验还显示，不同攻击类型对应不同的注意力扩散模式，这支持按评价维度设计更细的监督信号。\n\n如果只是想抓住结果，不必逐个看所有八类攻击。重点看作者如何把 token-level perturbation map 的主图，理解“低层识别与路由，高层整合与评分写入”这个主结论。最后再看 Themis 与 Prometheus 的差异，以及和 base model 的对照。预计 25 到 35 分钟可以抓住主要内容；如果要复现实验或借鉴到 reward model 分析，再细读附录和攻击生成细节。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"The standalone harness 对比。配置包括 Codex with GPT-5.5 high、OpenCode with DeepSeek-V4-Pro、Pi with MiniMax-M3。三轮迭代后，GameCraft-Bench 提升 16.62–22.08 分，FrontierSWE 提升 19–29 分，ProgramBench 提升 6.09–16.85 分。在 FrontierSWE 上，HoH + Codex/GPT-5.5 high 经过十轮从 22% 提升到 72.67%。第二类是开放式多天游戏开发，从高层产品需求开始构建复杂游戏，并加入角色专用工具和 skills，覆盖引擎交互、素材获取与生成、参考检索、测试和项目状态管理。","reading_guide":"建议泛读 30–45 分钟。重点看 Introduction 对长程 coding 数据生成和评测协议的启发。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"The paper addresses long-horizon autonomous software development: how an LLM-based coding agent can turn high-level requirements into a complete software system over multi-day execution without losing coherence, looping on local repairs, or declaring success prematurely.","importance":"The connection to foundation-model pretraining is indirect. The contribution is mainly at the agent harness and evaluation level, not in model training objectives, pretraining data, or optimization. It is still useful for researchers interested in long-horizon agent environments, coding-agent evaluation, and the generation of structured traces that may later become training data.","technical_approach":"Harness-of-Harness wraps existing coding-agent harnesses and imposes an iterative planning–coding–testing structure. The planner consumes the requirements and accumulated evidence, then proposes a small verifiable increment that combines repair with new capability growth. The developer implements the plan and performs local tests during implementation. An independent tester then evaluates the system against both the global requirements and the current plan, using white-box and black-box tests. The framework constrains required artifacts rather than prescribing the agent’s internal workflow. It uses progressive disclosure instead of a dedicated memory module: plans, reports, and histories are stored in the file system, exposed first through concise indexes, and retrieved in detail only when needed. It also supports role-specific tools, lightweight Markdown skills, versioned project states, and rollback after major regressions.","experiments":"The evaluation covers three controlled benchmarks: GameCraft-Bench, FrontierSWE, and ProgramBench. HoH is tested on top of several harness–model configurations, including Codex with GPT-5.5 high, OpenCode with DeepSeek-V4-Pro, and Pi with MiniMax-M3. After three iterations, HoH improves over the corresponding standalone harnesses by 16.62–22.08 points on GameCraft-Bench, 19–29 points on FrontierSWE, and 6.09–16.85 points on ProgramBench. On FrontierSWE, HoH with Codex/GPT-5.5 high improves from 22% to 72.67% over ten iterations. A second setting studies open-ended multi-day game development from high-level product requirements, using role-specific tools and skills for engine interaction, asset acquisition and generation, reference retrieval, testing, and project-state management.","reading_guide":"Skim in about 30–45 minutes. around coding agents: the runtime that connects an LLM to tools, files, shells, context management, safety policies, execution loops, and extension mechanisms. target-side evidence to return to the original conjecture through checked implications.","importance":"The contribution is not a pretraining method. Its relevance comes from agent evaluation and tool-mediated reasoning. The useful idea is that open-ended discovery can be constrained by explicit, auditable objects: source representation, target representation, mapping, target-native operation, and return obligations. This is a good antidote to a common LLM-agent failure mode: producing convincing progress in a translated space without proving or refuting the original statement. For post-training and RL on tool-use tasks, it suggests that rewards should check directionality, assumptions, boundary cases, and return validity, not just intermediate target-side success.","technical_approach":"The system runs three competing route families around a fixed conjecture: direct source-domain search, adjacent-domain bridges, and distant-domain bridges. A bridge must provide an operation that is absent from the frozen direct and adjacent inventories, and its evidence must map back to the source statement. EULER uses multiple roles for generation, retrieval, tool use, criticism, and verification, supported by deterministic programs and Lean where applicable. Routes share lemmas and counterexamples, consume budgets, and record failed mappings. Before a bridge receives substantial search budget, six ordered stress tests look for failures in implication direction, assumptions, boundary cases, round trips, real tool gain, and return validity. The central design choice is to allocate budget based on executable operation gain and checked return safety, rather than on surface analogy.","experiments":"The evaluation uses 120 recent conjectures that were frozen before search and screened for contamination. The bridge-specific stress tests reduce incorrect returns from 9 to 3, which is the cleaner empirical result. A 2-by-2 experiment finds an interaction between bridge material and target-native operations: about 4.2 additional resolved tasks, or 3.5 percentage points. eff","starred":false,"priority":"p2","reading_tier":"skim","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"","category_id":"agent","cover_url":"/covers/2609_00813.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.AI"],"summary":{"core_problem":"这篇工作处理的是一个很具体但长期被忽略的问题：RL 搜索代理通常只在固定预算下训练，因此学到的是“某个预算点上的策略”，而不是“随预算变化自适应分配搜索资源的能力”。部署时一旦预算变了，模型往往只能机械地少搜或多搜，不能真正判断哪些问题值得把预算花在检索上，哪些问题应当直接依靠内部推理完成。作者要解决的不是简单的 tool-call 限额控制，而是把预算感知的搜索分配能力内化进单一策略，使其在推理时只给总预算，不依赖外部预算跟踪器或额外控制模块，也能在不同预算约束下稳定工作。","importance":"对预训练研究本身，它不是直接改 pretrain recipe 的论文，但对 pretrain↔post-train 接口有现实意义。原因有两点。第一，它把“资源约束”从外部系统规则变成了策略内部能力，这和把格式约束、工具使用、长链推理等能力内化进模型的思路一致，属于后训练阶段对模型行为边界的重新塑形。第二，它说明 RL 不只是优化正确率，也可以学习条件化的计算分配策略，这对 test-time compute、budgeted reasoning、tool-use policy，甚至未来把 compute allocation 作为预训练或中训目标的一部分，都有启发。贡献量级上，这更像一个扎实的 agent RL 方法改进，而不是改变基础范式的工作；价值主要在训练脚手架如何被逐步撤掉并完成能力内化，以及奖励如何同时约束正确性和效率。","technical_approach":"方法核心是一个两阶段 curriculum RL 框架 AnySearch。任务被形式化为带资源约束的序列决策：状态由历史上下文 h_t 和剩余预算 b_t 组成，动作包括纯推理、调用搜索工具、输出答案三类。预算只对外部 search call 计费，因为一次检索带来的延迟和返回文档 token 开销远高于内部思维 token。\n\n第一阶段的重点是“扶着学”。作者加入训练脚手架，包含两部分：一是显式预算状态注入，让模型在上下文中看到当前剩余预算；二是结构化推理提示，引导模型在思维链中显式考虑是否值得搜索、预算是否还应保留、当前问题难度如何。训练时预算采用线性衰减，让模型在同一训练过程中持续接触从宽松到紧张的预算区间，而不是只在单点预算上收敛。\n\n第二阶段的重点是“撤掉拐杖”。脚手架被完全移除，训练输入和推理时保持一致，只保留总预算约束，让模型自己隐式维护预算分配策略。这里作者没有简单均匀采样预算，而是做了自适应预算采样：根据不同预算水平上的近期表现，更多采样当前薄弱的预算区间，同时用均匀平滑保持覆盖，避免策略只对少数预算点过拟合。这一点很关键，因为预算条件化策略的难点不是单点最优，而是整条 budget-performance 曲线都要平稳。\n\n奖励设计是另一条主线。作者使用复合奖励，把答案正确性和预算效率绑在一起，同时提供绝对与相对两类效率信号。更重要的是，效率项权重不是固定的，而是按 query 难度自适应调整，避免模型在难题上为了省搜索而牺牲正确率。这个设计针对的是常见失败模式：如果效率奖励过强，策略会学成“少搜少错但也少答对”；如果效率奖励过弱，又会退化成无节制搜索。论文的处理方式可以理解为 difficulty-aware reward balancing。","experiments":"实验覆盖 7 个 benchmark、3 个 backbone，目标是比较不同预算水平下的准确率、工具生产率和总 token 消耗。摘要和正文给出的主结论比较一致：AnySearch 在所有测试预算上都优于基线，并且能泛化到训练中未见过的预算值。这一点比固定预算训练更重要，因为它说明模型学到的不是离散预算点上的模板行为，而是连续的预算分配规律。\n\n作者重点比较了几类基线：固定预算训练的 RL 搜索代理、只做 reward shaping 以减少冗余调用的方法，以及需要外部预算跟踪器的 BATS 类方法。结果显示，AnySearch 不仅准确率更高，而且 tool productivity 最好，也就是每次搜索调用带来的有效信息增益更高；同时总 token consumption 最低，说明它不是靠更多内部思维 token 换取表面上的少搜索，而是真正提升了单位预算的利用率。\n\n消融实验围绕三个问题展开。第一，脚手架是否真的被内化。作者在 Phase I 后移除显式预算跟踪和结构化提示，性能不会塌陷，说明模型不是依赖外部控制逻辑，而是把预算感知行为学进了策略。第二，两阶段 curriculum 是否必要。去掉 Phase I 的显式引导，或者不做 Phase II 的无脚手架适配，都会降低跨预算泛化。第三，奖励各部分是否必要。去掉相对效率信号、绝对效率信号或自适应权重，都会让准确率—效率权衡变差。摘要里没有给出完整数字表，但从正文描述看，作者强调的是“全预算区间一致提升”和“未见预算泛化”这两个结果，而不是单一 benchmark 上的峰值分数。","reading_guide":"泛读时先看 Introduction 和 Figure 1，抓住它和固定预算 RL agent、BATS 这类外部预算跟踪方法的区别。然后看 Method 里的 3.2、3.3、3.4 三节：训练脚手架是什么、为什么要分两阶段、adaptive budget sampling 如何决定采样分布、复合奖励如何避免正确率与效率互相拉扯。这几处是方法真正有信息量的地方。\n\n实验部分建议重点看三类图表：一是不同预算下的性能曲线，而不是单点平均值；二是 unseen budgets 的泛化结果；三是 ablation，尤其是 scaffold removal 和 adaptive weighting。若只想快速判断是否值得深入，30–40 分钟足够。若想把它迁移到更一般的 tool-use RL 或 test-time compute 分配问题，需要仔细读奖励定义、预算采样策略和失败案例分析。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"The paper targets a specific limitation of RL-based search agents: they are usually trained under a fixed budget, so they learn a policy for one budget regime rather than a budget-conditional allocation strategy. When deployment constraints change, these agents do not truly adapt; they merely search less or more in a brittle way. The real problem is not enforcing a hard cap on tool calls, but learning when external search is worth spending budget on and when internal reasoning is sufficient. The goal is to internalize this budget-aware allocation skill into a single policy, so inference only needs a total budget and no external budget tracker or controller.","importance":"This is not a pretraining paper in the narrow sense, but it is relevant to the pretrain–post-train interface. It shows how a behavior that is often handled by external system logic can be moved into the model policy through RL. That matters for tool use, test-time compute allocation, and budgeted reasoning more broadly. The paper also makes a useful point for foundation-model training: RL can optimize not only correctness, but conditional compute allocation under varying constraints. That idea could later connect to mid-training or continual training setups where compute usage itself becomes part of the learning objective. The contribution is methodical rather than paradigm-shifting; the main value lies in scaffolded internalization and in the reward design that balances accuracy against efficiency.","technical_approach":"$70","experiments":"$71","reading_guide":"For a quick pass, start with the introduction and Figure 1 to understand how this differs from fixed-budget RL agents and external budget-tracking methods such as BATS. Benchmarks now isolate it as an agent skill, yet frontier LLMs still struggle: the best published PM-Bench scaffold reaches only 65.1% Set-F1. We argue that this loop is schema-constrained state tracking rather than open-ended reasoning, and that small models can execute it when the action space is typed. We propose the Prospective Intention Store (PIS) that puts lifecycle logic in code","starred":false,"priority":"p2","reading_tier":"skim","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"","category_id":"agent","cover_url":"/covers/2609_01272.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.AI"],"summary":{"core_problem":"当前 Agent 记忆研究多集中于回顾性记忆（检索过去），而前瞻性记忆（Prospective Memory, PM，即在未来特定条件满足时执行延迟意图）仍是短板。即使是前沿 LLM 在 PM 任务上也表现不佳，且现有方法难以在小模型（SLM）上高效实现。","importance":"首次系统形式化了语言 Agent 的前瞻性记忆操作，并证明了通过将意图类型化和生命周期逻辑代码化，小模型（SLM）无需微调即可在 PM 任务上超越大模型，为端侧 Agent 的长周期任务执行提供了低成本、高效率的新范式。","technical_approach":"提出前瞻性意图存储（Prospective Intention Store, PIS）。核心思想是将 PM 视为模式约束的状态追踪（schema-constrained state tracking），而非开放式推理。PIS 将延迟意图定义为类型化记录 $I=(\\phi, \\alpha, \\sigma)$（触发条件、动作、状态），将生命周期逻辑（如过期、更新）交由外部代码处理，而将受限的语言理解任务（如判断条件是否满足）交由 SLM 执行。整个系统包含 Form、Revise、Filter、Decide 四个阶段的循环，无需任何参数微调（training-free）。","experiments":"在 PM-Bench 上，搭载 PIS 的 DeepSeek-Chat 达到了 82.9% 的 Set-F1 分数，创下 SOTA。更重要的是，在 Gemma-E2B 这样的小模型上，PIS 使其表现大幅超越了已发表的基于大模型的最佳脚手架（65.1%），同时显著降低了 token 消耗和推理延迟。消融实验显示，没有 PIS 时 Gemma-E2B 的 Set-F1 仅为 4.2%。","reading_guide":"重点关注 Figure 1 中 PIS 的架构设计，理解意图的类型化表示和四阶段处理流程。建议阅读 Introduction 了解 PM 与回顾性记忆的本质区别，以及为什么 SLM 适合处理这种模式约束的任务。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"Current agent memory research focuses heavily on retrospective memory (retrieving the past), while prospective memory (PM)—executing deferred intentions when specific future conditions are met—remains a significant weakness. Even frontier LLMs struggle with PM, and existing methods are difficult to implement efficiently on small language models (SLMs).","importance":"Provides the first systematic formalization of prospective memory operations for language agents. It demonstrates that by typing intentions and externalizing lifecycle logic into code, SLMs can outperform large models on PM tasks without any fine-tuning, offering a low-cost, high-efficiency paradigm for long-horizon agent execution on-device.","technical_approach":"Proposes the Prospective Intention Store (PIS). The core insight is to treat PM as schema-constrained state tracking rather than open-ended reasoning. PIS defines deferred intentions as typed records $I=(\\phi, \\alpha, \\sigma)$ (trigger, action, status), offloads lifecycle logic (e.g., expiration, updates) to external code, and assigns scoped language grounding tasks (e.g., evaluating trigger conditions) to the SLM. The system operates in a training-free Form-Revise-Filter-Decide loop.","experiments":"On PM-Bench, DeepSeek-Chat with PIS achieves a new SOTA of 82.9% Set-F1. Crucially, when applied to a small model like Gemma-E2B, PIS enables it to surpass the best published large-model scaffold (65.1%), while significantly reducing token usage and wall-clock time. Ablations show Gemma-E2B achieves only 4.2% Set-F1 without PIS.","reading_guide":"Focus on Figure 1 for the PIS architecture, paying attention to the typed representation of intentions and the four-stage processing loop. Optimizing either component in isolation can leave the system bottlenecked by its frozen counterpart: weight updates can change which harness is effective, while harness updates can change which model capabilities are exposed. Existing joint-adaptation methods optimize weights and textual prompts but leave the broader harness fixed. We propose Weight-Harness Alternatin","starred":false,"priority":"p2","reading_tier":"skim","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"","category_id":"agent","cover_url":"/covers/2609_00196.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.LG","cs.AI"],"summary":{"core_problem":"这篇工作处理的是 agent 系统里一个常被拆开看、但实际强耦合的问题：模型权重和外部 harness 代码需要联合优化。这里的 harness 不是简单 prompt，而是决定上下文拼接、工具暴露、错误处理、控制流和终止条件的可执行程序。单独训权重，容易被一个差 harness 卡住；单独搜 harness，又可能因为当前模型能力不够而搜不到有效结构。论文要解决的不是“如何做更强 agent”，而是“在权重更新和 harness 搜索时钟不同、反馈噪声不同的情况下，怎样做稳定、预算可控、可归因的联合优化”。","importance":"对基座研究的直接启发不在于 agent 应用本身，而在于它把“模型参数之外的可学习系统层”正式纳入训练闭环。对 pretrain/post-train 接口来说，这很重要：很多能力并不只由参数决定，还受推理时程序结构约束。WHALE 给出的结论是，交替优化比 stagewise 或只调一侧更稳，也更省 rollout 预算。这件事对 tool-use、reasoning scaffold、test-time compute 乃至后训练系统设计都有参考价值。量级上，它更像一个训练框架和实验范式贡献，而不是新的基础模型算法；但如果关心 RL/SFT 与 agent runtime 的耦合，这篇值得记住。","technical_approach":"方法很简单，核心是 alternating optimization。论文把联合目标写成在任务分布上最大化 verifier reward 的问题，策略由模型参数 θ 和 harness h 共同决定。直接同时更新两者会遇到 credit assignment 混乱：harness 搜索需要长周期 proposal-evaluate-accept，在线微调则是 rollout 和梯度更新交替，二者步频不匹配。WHALE 的做法是把问题拆成两个条件子问题：\n1）固定 harness，做 weight-update phase；\n2）固定更新后的模型，做 harness-search phase。\n\n在具体实例化上，权重更新用 online RSFT。也就是模型在当前 harness 下采样多条轨迹，只保留 verifier 通过的 rollout 做监督学习。这个选择比直接 policy gradient 更稳，且更容易和已有 SFT 管线兼容。harness 搜索则用 Meta-Harness，在固定模型下对可执行 harness 程序做搜索，搜索空间覆盖 system instruction、tool schema、上下文管理、parser/execution logic、termination policy 等，而不是只改 prompt。\n\n论文还提出 adaptive WHALE。固定 schedule 的问题是每个 phase 持续多久很难预先设定：太短，证据不足；太长，会对固定 counterpart 过拟合。adaptive 版本要求每个 phase 至少运行一个最小时长，之后当当前 phase 的训练信号不再改善时切换。这个设计抓住了联合优化里的关键张力：每一侧都要在“学到足够多”和“别把另一侧当静态环境过拟合”之间找平衡。\n\n和已有方法相比，最接近的是只联合权重和 textual prompt 的方法，以及 FST 这类 fast-slow training。这里的区别有两点。第一，优化对象从 prompt 扩展到完整 executable harness，搜索空间大得多，也更接近真实 agent 系统。第二，论文强调 conditional alternation，而不是并行 co-adaptation；目的不是更快，而是让每次改动都在固定对手下评估，减少归因混乱。","experiments":"实验覆盖三个环境：SearchQA、带代码执行的数学推理、以及 chess puzzles。对比对象包括 weight-only、harness-only、以及 FST。为了公平，FST 实现使用和 WHALE 相同的更新方法与 schedule，只是把搜索空间限制在 system/user prompts，从而隔离“完整 harness 搜索空间”这一因素。\n\n主结果上，WHALE 相比单侧优化基线提升 7.67 到 24.38 个百分点；相比 FST 提升 4.15 到 13.00 个百分点。这个幅度说明收益不只是来自多做了一轮搜索，而是来自交替机制和更大的 harness 搜索空间。\n\n论文里一个很有信息量的观察是两种不同瓶颈区间：\n- 在 SearchQA，harness-only 很快就能达到 weight-only 的峰值准确率，而且用更少 rollouts。这说明这里更偏 harness-limited：检索、上下文组织和工具调用逻辑决定了上限。\n- 在 Math，harness-only 几乎不涨，直到先做一小步 weight update 后，同样的 harness search 才开始有效。这说明这里先是 weight-limited：模型本身推理/代码使用能力不到位，harness 再好也带不动。\n\n这组结果很关键，因为它解释了为什么 stagewise 不够好。若先把一侧训满，再去动另一侧，就默认瓶颈在整个训练过程中不变；但实验表明瓶颈会迁移。\n\n论文也直接比较了 alternating 和 stagewise。在相同预算下，小步交替更新在 SearchQA 和 Math 上分别比 stagewise 高 5.32 和 9.16 个百分点；而且只用到后者 29% 和 49% 的 rollouts 就已经超过 stagewise 最终结果。这个结果说明交替不是“同预算下略优”，而是更早进入有效协同区间。\n\n另外，adaptive WHALE 在两个评测域上都优于调过超参的固定 schedule，说明 phase 切换确实不适合完全手工定死。摘要里没有给出更多细粒度数字，但从全文叙述看，作者重点不是追求单任务峰值，而是证明这种训练组织方式在不同瓶颈结构下都更稳。","reading_guide":"泛读建议是先看 Introduction 和 Figure 1，先把问题设定和三类对比关系看清：single-component、FST、stagewise。然后看 Preliminaries 里对联合目标和两阶段分解的定义，理解为什么他们坚持 alternation 而不是并行更新。\n\n重点建议看三部分：\n1）weight-update phase 的 online RSFT 细节。要看清 verifier-accepted rollout 如何构造训练信号，以及它为什么适合作为和 harness 搜索配套的稳定更新器。\n2）harness-search phase 的搜索空间定义。这里决定了论文和 prompt optimization 的边界在哪里。若只把它理解成 prompt search，会低估工作价值。\n3）主实验里 SearchQA 与 Math 对比部分。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"The paper studies a coupled optimization problem in agentic LM systems: model weights and the executable harness should be improved together. Here, the harness is not just a prompt. It includes context construction, tool exposure, parsing, error handling, control flow, and termination logic. Optimizing only weights can leave the system bottlenecked by a brittle harness; optimizing only the harness can fail because the current model cannot exploit the improved structure. The real question is how to coordinate these two updates when they operate on different timescales and produce different kinds of noisy feedback.","importance":"Its value for foundation-model researchers is less about the specific agent tasks and more about the training principle: useful capability can live outside the parameter tensor, in the surrounding executable system. The main takeaway is that alternating optimization is more sample-efficient and more robust than stagewise optimization or single-side adaptation. This is not a new base-model objective, but it is a meaningful systems-level training recipe for settings where policy quality depends jointly on weights and runtime structure.","technical_approach":"$74","experiments":"$75","reading_guide":"Start with the introduction and Figure 1.
